# 19.시편 (NAS2020)



## 1장

**1:1** Blessed is the person who does not walk in the counsel of the wicked,<br>Nor stand in the <sup>①</sup>path of sinners,<br>Nor sit in the seat of scoffers!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>way</i>  
**1:2** But his delight is in the Law of the LORD,<br>And on His Law he meditates day and night.  
**1:3** He will be like a tree planted by <sup>①</sup>streams of water,<br>Which yields its fruit in its season,<br>And its <sup>②</sup>leaf does not wither;<br>And <sup>③</sup>in whatever he does, he prospers.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>canals</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>foliage</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>all that he does prospers</i>  
**1:4** The wicked are not so,<br>But they are like chaff which the wind blows away.  
**1:5** Therefore the wicked will not stand in the judgment,<br>Nor sinners in the assembly of the righteous.  
**1:6** For the LORD <sup>①</sup>knows the way of the righteous,<br>But the way of the wicked will perish.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>approves;</i> or <i>has regard to</i>  

## 2장

**2:1** Why are the <sup>①</sup>nations restless<br>And the peoples plotting in vain?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Gentiles</i>  
**2:2** The kings of the earth take their stand<br>And the rulers conspire together<br>Against the LORD and against His <sup>①</sup>Anointed, <i>saying,</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Messiah</i>  
**2:3** “Let’s tear their shackles apart<br>And throw their ropes away from us!”  
**2:4** He who <sup>①</sup>sits in the heavens laughs,<br>The Lord scoffs at them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>is enthroned</i>  
**2:5** Then He will speak to them in His anger<br>And terrify them in His fury, <i>saying,</i>  
**2:6** “But as for Me, I have installed My King<br>Upon Zion, My holy mountain.”  
**2:7** “I will announce the <sup>①</sup>decree of the LORD:<br>He said to Me, ‘You are My Son,<br>Today I have fathered You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>decree: The LORD said to Me</i>  
**2:8** Ask <i>it</i> of Me, and I will certainly give the <sup>①</sup>nations as Your inheritance,<br>And the ends of the earth as Your possession.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Gentiles</i>  
**2:9** You shall <sup>①</sup>break them with a <sup>②</sup>rod of iron,<br>You shall shatter them like <sup>③</sup>earthenware.’ ”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Another reading is <i>rule</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>scepter;</i> or <i>staff</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>potter’s ware</i>  
**2:10** Now then, you kings, use insight;<br>Let yourselves be instructed, you <sup>①</sup>judges of the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>leaders</i>  
**2:11** Serve the LORD with <sup>①</sup>reverence<br>And rejoice with trembling.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>fear</i>  
**2:12**  <sup>①</sup>Kiss the Son, that He not be angry and you perish <i>on</i> the way,<br>For His wrath may be kindled quickly.<br>How blessed are all who take refuge in Him!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., probably kiss the feet of the Son  

## 3장

**3:1** [A Psalm of David, when <sup>①</sup>he fled from his son Absalom.]<br>LORD, how my enemies have increased!<br>Many are rising up against me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>2 Sam 15:13-17, 29  
**3:2** Many are saying of my soul,<br>“There is no salvation for him in God.” <i>Selah</i>  
**3:3** But You, LORD, are a shield around me,<br>My glory, and the One who lifts my head.  
**3:4** I was crying out to the LORD with my voice,<br>And He answered me from His holy <sup>①</sup>mountain. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>hill</i>  
**3:5**  <sup>①</sup>I lay down and slept;<br>I awoke, for the LORD sustains me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>As for me, I</i>  
**3:6** I will not be afraid of ten thousands of people<br>Who have set themselves against me all around.  
**3:7** Arise, LORD; save me, my God!<br>For You <sup>①</sup>have struck all my enemies on the <sup>②</sup>cheek;<br>You <sup>③</sup>have shattered the teeth of the wicked.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>strike</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>jaw</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>shatter</i>  
**3:8**  <sup>①</sup>Salvation belongs to the LORD;<br><i>May</i> Your blessing <sup>②</sup><i>be</i> upon Your people! <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Deliverance</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>is</i>  

## 4장

**4:1** [ <sup>①</sup>For the music director; on stringed instruments. A Psalm of David.]<br>Answer me when I call, God <sup>②</sup>of my righteousness!<br>You have <sup>③</sup>relieved me in my distress;<br>Be gracious to me and hear my prayer.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., Belonging to the choir director’s anthology <sup>②</sup>I.e., who maintains my right <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>made room for</i>  
**4:2** You sons of man, how long will my <sup>①</sup>honor be <i>treated as</i> an insult?<br><i>How long</i> will you love what is worthless and strive for a lie? <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>glory</i>  
**4:3** But know that the LORD has <sup>①</sup>set apart the godly person for Himself;<br>The LORD hears when I call to Him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Another reading is <i>dealt wonderfully with</i>  
**4:4**  <sup>①</sup>Tremble, <sup>②</sup>and do not sin;<br> <sup>③</sup>Meditate in your heart upon your bed, and be still. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., with anger or fear <sup>②</sup>Or <i>but</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>Speak</i>  
**4:5** Offer <sup>①</sup>the sacrifices of righteousness,<br>And trust in the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>righteous sacrifices</i>  
**4:6** Many are saying, “Who will show us <i>anything</i> good?”<br>Lift up the light of Your face upon us, LORD!  
**4:7** You have put joy in my heart,<br>More than when their grain and new wine are abundant.  
**4:8** In peace I will <sup>①</sup>both lie down and sleep,<br>For You alone, LORD, have me dwell in safety.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>at the same time</i>  

## 5장

**5:1** [For the music director; for <sup>①</sup>flute accompaniment. A Psalm of David.]<br>Listen to my words, LORD,<br>Consider my sighing.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb Nehiloth  
**5:2** Listen to the sound of my cry for help, my King and my God,<br>For to You I pray.  
**5:3** In the morning, LORD, <sup>①</sup>You will hear my voice;<br>In the morning I will present <i>my</i> <sup>②</sup><i>prayer</i> to You and be on the watch.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>May You hear</i> <sup>②</sup>Or sacrifice  
**5:4** For You are not a God who takes pleasure in wickedness;<br>No evil can dwell with You.  
**5:5** The boastful will not stand before Your eyes;<br>You hate all who do injustice.  
**5:6** You destroy those who speak lies;<br>The LORD loathes the person of bloodshed and deceit.  
**5:7** But as for me, by Your abundant graciousness I will enter Your house,<br> <sup>①</sup>At Your holy temple I will bow in reverence for You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Toward</i>  
**5:8** LORD, lead me in Your righteousness because of my enemies;<br>Make Your way <sup>①</sup>straight before me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>smooth</i>  
**5:9** For there is nothing <sup>①</sup>trustworthy in <sup>②</sup>their mouth;<br>Their inward part is destruction <i>itself.</i><br>Their throat is an open grave;<br>They <sup>③</sup>flatter with their tongue.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>true</i> <sup>②</sup>So ancient versions; MT <i>his</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>make their tongue smooth</i>  
**5:10** Make them pay, God;<br>Have them fall by their own schemes!<br>Scatter them in the multitude of their wrongdoings,<br>For they are rebellious against You.  
**5:11** But rejoice, all who take refuge in You,<br>Sing for joy forever!<br>And <sup>①</sup>may You shelter them,<br>That those who love Your name may rejoice in You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>You shelter</i>  
**5:12** For You bless the righteous person, LORD,<br>You surround him with favor as with a shield.  

## 6장

**6:1** [For the music director; with stringed instruments, <sup>①</sup>upon an eight-string lyre. A Psalm of David.]<br>LORD, do not rebuke me in Your anger,<br>Nor discipline me in Your wrath.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or according to a lower octave (Heb Sheminith)  
**6:2** Be gracious to me, LORD, for I <i>am</i> frail;<br>Heal me, LORD, for my bones are horrified.  
**6:3** And my soul is greatly horrified;<br>But You, LORD—how long?  
**6:4** Return, LORD, rescue my <sup>①</sup>soul;<br>Save me because of Your mercy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>life</i>  
**6:5** For there is no <sup>①</sup>mention of You in death;<br>In <sup>②</sup>Sheol, who will praise You?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>remembrance</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**6:6** I am weary with my sighing;<br>Every night I make my bed swim,<br>I flood my couch with my tears.  
**6:7** My eye has wasted away with grief;<br>It has grown old because of all my enemies.  
**6:8** Leave me, all you who practice injustice,<br>For the LORD has heard the sound of my weeping.  
**6:9** The LORD has heard my pleading,<br>The LORD receives my prayer.  
**6:10** All my enemies will be put to shame and greatly horrified;<br>They shall <sup>①</sup>turn back, they will suddenly be put to shame.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>again be abruptly put to shame</i>  

## 7장

**7:1** [A <sup>①</sup>Shiggaion of David, which he sang to the Lord <sup>②</sup>concerning Cush, a Benjaminite.]<br>O LORD my God, in You I have taken refuge;<br>Save me from all those who pursue me, and rescue me,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., Dithyrambic rhythm; or wild, passionate song <sup>②</sup>Or concerning the words of  
**7:2** Or he will tear <sup>①</sup>my soul like a lion,<br>Dragging me away, while there is no one to rescue <i>me.</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>me</i>  
**7:3** O LORD my God, if I have done this,<br>If there is injustice in my hands,  
**7:4** If I have done evil to <sup>①</sup>my friend,<br>Or have plundered <sup>②</sup>my enemy for no reason,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>him who was at peace with me</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>him who for no reason was my enemy,</i>  
**7:5** Let the enemy pursue <sup>①</sup>my soul and overtake <sup>②</sup><i>it;</i><br>And let him trample my life to the ground<br>And lay my glory in the dust. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>me</i> <sup>②</sup>Or me  
**7:6** Arise, LORD, in Your anger;<br>Raise Yourself against the rage of my enemies,<br>And stir Yourself <sup>①</sup>for me; You have ordered judgment.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>One ancient version reads <i>O my God</i>  
**7:7** Let the assembly of the peoples encompass You,<br>And return on high over it.  
**7:8** The LORD judges the peoples;<br> <sup>①</sup>Vindicate me, LORD, according to my righteousness and my integrity that is in me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Judge</i>  
**7:9** Please let the evil of the wicked come to an end, but establish the righteous;<br>For the righteous God puts hearts and <sup>①</sup>minds to the test.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>kidneys,</i> figurative for inner man  
**7:10** My shield is <sup>①</sup>with God,<br>Who saves the upright in heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>upon</i>  
**7:11** God is a righteous judge,<br>And a God who shows indignation every day.  
**7:12** If one does not repent, He will sharpen His sword;<br>He has bent His bow and taken aim.  
**7:13** He has also prepared deadly weapons for Himself;<br>He makes His arrows fiery <i>shafts.</i>  
**7:14** Behold, <i>an evil person</i> is pregnant with injustice,<br>And he conceives harm and gives birth to lies.  
**7:15** He has dug a pit and hollowed it out,<br>And has fallen into the hole which he made.  
**7:16** His harm will return on his own head,<br>And his violence will descend on the top of his own head.  
**7:17** I will give thanks to the LORD according to His righteousness<br>And will sing praise to the name of the LORD Most High.  

## 8장

**8:1** LORD, our Lord,<br>How majestic is Your name in all the earth,<br>You who have <sup>①</sup>displayed Your splendor above the heavens!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>put</i>  
**8:2** [For the music director; on the Gittith. A Psalm of David.]<br>From the mouths of infants and nursing babies You have established <sup>①</sup>strength<br>Because of Your enemies,<br>To do away with the enemy and the revengeful.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>a strength</i>  
**8:3** When I <sup>①</sup>consider Your heavens, the work of Your fingers,<br>The moon and the stars, which You have set in place;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>see</i>  
**8:4** What is man that You think of him,<br>And a son of man that You are concerned about him?  
**8:5** Yet You have made him a little lower than <sup>①</sup>God,<br>And You crown him with glory and majesty!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>LXX <i>angels</i>  
**8:6** You have him rule over the works of Your hands;<br>You have put everything under his feet,  
**8:7** All sheep and oxen,<br>And also the animals of the field,  
**8:8** The birds of the sky, and the fish of the sea,<br>Whatever passes through the paths of the seas.  
**8:9** LORD, our Lord,<br>How majestic is Your name in all the earth!  

## 9장

**9:1** [For the music director; on <sup>①</sup>Muth-labben. A Psalm of David.]<br>I will give thanks to the LORD with all my heart;<br>I will tell of all Your <sup>②</sup>wonders.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Meaning of the Heb uncertain <sup>②</sup>Or <i>miracles</i>  
**9:2** I will rejoice and be jubilant in You;<br>I will sing praise to Your name, O Most High.  
**9:3** When my enemies turn back,<br>They stumble and perish before You.  
**9:4** For You have maintained <sup>①</sup>my just cause;<br>You have sat on the throne <sup>②</sup>judging righteously.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>my right and my cause</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>a righteous Judge</i>  
**9:5** You have rebuked the nations, You have eliminated the wicked;<br>You have wiped out their name forever and ever.  
**9:6**  <sup>①</sup>The enemy has come to an end <i>in</i> everlasting ruins,<br>And You have uprooted the cities;<br>The very memory of them has perished.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>O enemy, desolations are finished forever; And their cities You have uprooted</i>  
**9:7** But the LORD <sup>①</sup>sits <i>as King</i> forever;<br>He has established His throne for judgment,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>remains forever</i>  
**9:8** And He will judge the world in righteousness;<br>He will execute judgment for the peoples fairly.  
**9:9**  <sup>①</sup>The LORD will also be a stronghold for the oppressed,<br>A stronghold in times of trouble;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Let the LORD</i> also be  
**9:10** And <sup>①</sup>those who know Your name will put their trust in You,<br>For You, LORD, have not abandoned those who seek You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>let those...name put</i>  
**9:11** Sing praises to the LORD, who dwells in Zion;<br>Declare His deeds among the peoples.  
**9:12** For He who <sup>①</sup>requires blood remembers them;<br>He does not forget the cry of the needy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., avenges bloodshed  
**9:13** Be gracious to me, LORD;<br>See my oppression from those who hate me,<br>You who lift me up from the gates of death,  
**9:14** So that I may tell of all Your praises,<br>That in the gates of the daughter of Zion<br>I may rejoice in Your <sup>①</sup>salvation.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>deliverance</i>  
**9:15** The nations have sunk down into the pit <i>which</i> they have made;<br>In the net which they hid, their own foot has been caught.  
**9:16** The LORD has made Himself known;<br>He has executed judgment.<br>A wicked one is ensnared in the work of his own hands. <sup>①</sup><i>Higgaion Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Perhaps, resounding music or meditation  
**9:17** The wicked will <sup>①</sup>return to <sup>②</sup>Sheol,<br>All the nations who forget God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>turn</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**9:18** For the needy will not always be forgotten,<br>Nor the hope of the afflicted perish forever.  
**9:19** Arise, LORD, do not let mankind prevail;<br>Let the nations be judged before You.  
**9:20** Put them in fear, LORD;<br>Let the nations know that they are <i>merely</i> human. <i>Selah</i>  

## 10장

**10:1** Why do You stand far away, LORD?<br>Why do You hide <sup>①</sup><i>Yourself</i> in times of trouble?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or Your eyes  
**10:2** In arrogance the wicked hotly pursue the needy;<br> <sup>①</sup>Let them be caught in the plots which they have devised.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>They will be caught</i>  
**10:3** For the wicked boasts of his soul’s desire,<br>And <sup>①</sup>the greedy person curses <i>and</i> shows disrespect to the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>blesses the greedy man</i>  
**10:4** The wicked, <sup>①</sup>in his haughtiness, does not seek <i>Him.</i><br>There is no God <i>in</i> all his schemes.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the height of his nose</i>  
**10:5** His ways succeed at all times;<br><i>Yet</i> Your judgments are on high, out of his sight;<br>As for all his enemies, he snorts at them.  
**10:6** He says to himself, “I will not be moved;<br> <sup>①</sup>Throughout the generations I will not be in adversity.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>To</i>  
**10:7** His mouth is full of cursing, deceit, and oppression;<br>Under his tongue is harm and injustice.  
**10:8** He sits in the lurking places of the villages;<br>He kills the innocent in the secret places;<br>His eyes <sup>①</sup>surreptitiously watch for the <sup>②</sup>unfortunate.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>lie in wait</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>poor</i>  
**10:9** He lurks in secret like a lion in his <sup>①</sup>lair;<br>He lurks to catch the needy;<br>He catches the needy when he pulls him into his net.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>thicket</i>  
**10:10** Then he crushes <i>the needy one, who</i> cowers;<br>And <sup>①</sup>unfortunate people fall by his mighty <i>power.</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>unhappy</i>  
**10:11** He says to himself, “God has forgotten;<br>He has hidden His face; He will never see it.”  
**10:12** Arise, LORD; God, lift up Your hand.<br>Do not forget the humble.  
**10:13** Why has the wicked treated God disrespectfully?<br>He has said to himself, “ <sup>①</sup>You will not require <i>an account.</i>”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., as though God will ignore it  
**10:14** You have seen <i>it,</i> for You have looked at harm and provocation to <sup>①</sup>take it into Your hand.<br>The <sup>②</sup>unfortunate commits <i>himself</i> to You;<br>You have been the helper of the orphan.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>put, give</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>poor</i>  
**10:15** Break the arm of the wicked and the evildoer,<br> <sup>①</sup>Seek out his wickedness until You find none.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>May You seek</i>  
**10:16** The LORD is King forever and ever;<br>Nations have perished from His land.  
**10:17** LORD, You have heard the desire of the humble;<br>You will strengthen their heart, You will make Your ear attentive  
**10:18** To <sup>①</sup>vindicate the orphan and the oppressed,<br>So that mankind, which is of the earth, will no longer cause terror.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>judge</i>  

## 11장

**11:1** [For the music director. <i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>In the LORD I take refuge;<br>How can you say to my soul, “Flee <i>as</i> a bird to your mountain?  
**11:2** For, behold, the wicked bend the bow,<br>They have <sup>①</sup>set their arrow on the string<br>To shoot in darkness at the upright in heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>fixed</i>  
**11:3** If the foundations are destroyed,<br>What can the righteous do?”  
**11:4** The LORD is in His holy temple; the <sup>①</sup>LORD’S throne is in heaven;<br>His eyes see, His eyelids test the sons of mankind.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>LORD, His throne</i>  
**11:5** The LORD tests the righteous and the wicked,<br>And His soul hates one who loves violence.  
**11:6** He will rain <sup>①</sup>coals of fire upon the wicked,<br>And brimstone and burning wind will be the portion of their cup.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>As in a Gr version; MT <i>snares; Fire and</i>  
**11:7** For the LORD is righteous, He loves <sup>①</sup>righteousness;<br>The upright will see His face.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>righteous deeds</i>  

## 12장

**12:1** [For the music director; <sup>①</sup>upon an eight-stringed lyre. A Psalm of David.]<br>Help, LORD, for the godly person has come to an end,<br>For the faithful have disappeared from the sons of mankind.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or according to a lower octave (Heb Sheminith)  
**12:2** They speak <sup>①</sup>lies to one another;<br>They speak with <sup>②</sup>flattering lips and a double heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>worthless</i> speech <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>a smooth lip</i>  
**12:3** May the LORD cut off all <sup>①</sup>flattering lips,<br>The tongue that speaks great things;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>a smooth lip</i>  
**12:4** Who have said, “With our tongue we will prevail;<br>Our lips are <sup>①</sup>our own; who is lord over us?”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>with us</i>  
**12:5** “Because of the devastation of the poor, because of the groaning of the needy,<br>Now I will arise,” says the LORD; “I will put him in the safety for which he longs.”  
**12:6** The words of the LORD are pure words;<br>Like silver refined in a furnace on the ground, filtered seven times.  
**12:7** You, LORD, will keep them;<br>You will protect him from this generation forever.  
**12:8** The wicked strut about on every side<br>When <sup>①</sup>vileness is exalted among the sons of mankind.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>worthlessness</i>  

## 13장

**13:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>How long, LORD? Will You forget me forever?<br>How long will You hide Your face from me?  
**13:2** How long am I to feel anxious in my soul,<br><i>With</i> grief in my heart all the day?<br>How long will my enemy be exalted over me?  
**13:3** Consider <i>and</i> answer me, O LORD my God;<br>Enlighten my eyes, or I will sleep the <i>sleep of</i> death,  
**13:4** And my enemy will say, “I have overcome him,”<br><i>And</i> my adversaries will rejoice when I am shaken.  
**13:5** But I have trusted in Your faithfulness;<br>My heart shall rejoice in Your salvation.  
**13:6** I will sing to the LORD,<br>Because He has looked after me.  

## 14장

**14:1** [For the music director. <i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>The fool has said in his heart, “There is no God.”<br>They are corrupt, they have committed detestable acts;<br>There is no one who does good.  
**14:2** The LORD has looked down from heaven upon the sons of mankind<br>To see if there are any who <sup>①</sup>understand,<br>Who seek God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>act wisely</i>  
**14:3** They have all turned aside, together they are corrupt;<br>There is no one who does good, not even one.  
**14:4** Do all the workers of injustice not know,<br>Who devour my people <i>as</i> they eat bread,<br><i>And</i> do not call upon the LORD?  
**14:5** There they are in great dread,<br>For God is with a righteous generation.  
**14:6** You would put to shame the plan of the poor,<br>But the LORD is his refuge.  
**14:7** Oh, that the salvation of Israel <i>would come</i> out of Zion!<br>When the LORD restores the fortunes of His people,<br>Jacob will rejoice, Israel will be glad.  

## 15장

**15:1** [A Psalm of David.]<br>LORD, who may reside in Your tent?<br>Who may settle on Your holy hill?  
**15:2** One who walks with integrity, practices righteousness,<br>And speaks truth in his heart.  
**15:3** He does not slander <sup>①</sup>with his tongue,<br>Nor do evil to his neighbor,<br>Nor bring shame on his friend;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>according to</i>  
**15:4** A despicable person is despised in his eyes,<br>But he honors those who fear the LORD;<br>He takes an oath to his own detriment, and does not change;  
**15:5** He does not <sup>①</sup>lend his money <sup>②</sup>at interest,<br>Nor does he take a bribe against the innocent.<br>One who does these things will never be shaken.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>give</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., to a fellow Israelite  

## 16장

**16:1** [A <sup>①</sup>Mikhtam of David.]<br>Protect me, God, for I take refuge in You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly Epigrammatic Poem or Atonement Psalm  
**16:2**  <sup>①</sup>I said to the LORD, “You are <sup>②</sup>my Lord;<br>I have nothing good besides You.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or O my soul, <i>you said</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>the Lord</i>  
**16:3** As for the <sup>①</sup>saints who are on the earth,<br> <sup>②</sup>They are the majestic ones; all my delight is in them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>holy ones;</i> i.e., God’s people <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>They, even the</i>  
**16:4**  <sup>①</sup>The pains of those who have acquired another <i>god</i> will be multiplied;<br>I will not pour out their drink offerings of blood,<br>Nor will I take their names upon my lips.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Their pains, they</i> who <i>acquired</i>  
**16:5** The LORD is the portion of my inheritance and my cup;<br>You support my lot.  
**16:6** The measuring lines have fallen for me in pleasant places;<br>Indeed, my inheritance is beautiful to me.  
**16:7** I will bless the LORD who has advised me;<br>Indeed, my <sup>①</sup>mind instructs me in the night.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>kidneys,</i> figurative for inner man  
**16:8** I have set the LORD continually before me;<br>Because He is at my right hand, I will not be shaken.  
**16:9** Therefore my heart is glad and my glory rejoices;<br>My flesh also will dwell securely.  
**16:10** For You will not abandon my soul to <sup>①</sup>Sheol;<br>You will not <sup>②</sup>allow Your <sup>③</sup>Holy One to <sup>④</sup>undergo decay.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the netherworld <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>give</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>godly one</i> <sup>④</sup>Or <i>see corruption;</i> or <i>the pit</i>  
**16:11** You will make known to me the way of life;<br>In Your presence is fullness of joy;<br>In Your right hand there are pleasures forever.  

## 17장

**17:1** [A Prayer of David.]<br>Hear a just cause, LORD, give <i>Your</i> attention to my cry;<br>Listen to my prayer, which is not from deceitful lips.  
**17:2** Let my <sup>①</sup>judgment come forth from Your presence;<br>Let Your eyes look with integrity.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., vindication  
**17:3** You have put my heart to the test;<br>You have visited <i>me</i> by night;<br>You have sifted me and You find <sup>①</sup>nothing;<br>My intent is that my mouth will not offend.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>no shameful act in me; My mouth</i>  
**17:4** As for the works of mankind, by the word of Your lips<br>I have kept from the ways of the violent.  
**17:5** My steps have held to Your <sup>①</sup>paths.<br>My feet have not slipped.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>tracks</i>  
**17:6** I have called upon You, for You will answer me, God;<br>Incline Your ear to me, hear my speech.  
**17:7** Show Your wonderful faithfulness,<br>Savior of those who take refuge <sup>①</sup>at Your right hand<br>From those who rise up <i>against them.</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>from those who rise up...at Your right hand</i>  
**17:8** Keep me as <sup>①</sup>the apple of the eye;<br>Hide me in the shadow of Your wings<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the pupil, the daughter of the eye</i>  
**17:9** From the wicked who deal violently with me,<br>My deadly enemies who surround me.  
**17:10** They have closed their <sup>①</sup>unfeeling <i>hearts,</i><br>With their mouths they speak proudly.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>fat</i>  
**17:11** They have now surrounded us in our steps;<br>They set their eyes to cast <i>us</i> down to the ground.  
**17:12** He is like a lion that is eager to tear,<br>And as a young lion lurking in secret places.  
**17:13** Arise, LORD, confront him, make him bow down;<br>Save my soul from the wicked with Your sword,  
**17:14** From people by Your hand, LORD,<br>From people <sup>①</sup>of the world, whose portion is in <i>this</i> life,<br>And whose belly You fill with Your treasure;<br>They are satisfied with children,<br>And leave their abundance to their babies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>whose portion in life is of the world</i>  
**17:15** As for me, I shall behold Your face in righteousness;<br>I shall be satisfied with Your likeness when I awake.  

## 18장

**18:1** [For the music director. A <i>Psalm</i> of David, the servant of the LORD, <sup>①</sup>who spoke to the LORD the words of this song on the day that the LORD rescued him from the hand of all his enemies and from the hand of Saul. And he said,]<br>“I love You, LORD, my strength.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>2 Sam 22:1-51  
**18:2** The LORD is my rock and my fortress and my savior,<br>My God, my rock, in whom I take refuge;<br>My shield and the horn of my salvation, my stronghold.  
**18:3** I call upon the LORD, who is worthy to be praised,<br>And I am saved from my enemies.  
**18:4** The ropes of death encompassed me,<br>And the torrents of <sup>①</sup>destruction <sup>②</sup>terrified me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>ungodliness;</i> Heb <i>Belial</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>were assailing;</i> or <i>terrifying</i>  
**18:5** The ropes of <sup>①</sup>Sheol surrounded me;<br>The snares of death confronted me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**18:6** In my distress I called upon the LORD,<br>And cried to my God for help;<br>He heard my voice from His temple,<br>And my cry for help before Him came into His ears.  
**18:7** Then the earth shook and quaked;<br>And the foundations of the mountains were trembling<br>And were shaken, because He was angry.  
**18:8** Smoke went up <sup>①</sup>out of His nostrils,<br>And fire from His mouth was devouring;<br>Coals burned from it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>in His wrath</i>  
**18:9** He also bowed the heavens down low, and came down<br>With thick darkness under His feet.  
**18:10** He rode on a cherub and flew;<br>And He sped on the wings of the wind.  
**18:11** He made darkness His hiding place, His <sup>①</sup>canopy around Him,<br>Darkness of waters, thick clouds.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>pavilion</i>  
**18:12** From the brightness before Him passed His thick clouds,<br>Hailstones and coals of fire.  
**18:13** The LORD also thundered in the heavens,<br>And the Most High uttered His voice,<br>Hailstones and coals of fire.  
**18:14** He sent out His arrows, and scattered them,<br>And lightning flashes in abundance, and <sup>①</sup>routed them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>confused</i>  
**18:15** Then the channels of water appeared,<br>And the foundations of the world were exposed<br>By Your rebuke, LORD,<br>At the blast of the breath of Your nostrils.  
**18:16** He sent from on high, He took me;<br>He drew me out of many waters.  
**18:17** He saved me from my strong enemy,<br>And from those who hated me, for they were too mighty for me.  
**18:18** They confronted me in the day of my disaster,<br>But the LORD was my support.  
**18:19** He also brought me out into an open place;<br>He rescued me, because He delighted in me.  
**18:20** The LORD has rewarded me according to my righteousness;<br>According to the cleanness of my hands He has repaid me.  
**18:21** For I have kept the ways of the LORD,<br>And have not acted wickedly against my God.  
**18:22** For all His judgments were before me,<br>And I did not put away His statutes from me.  
**18:23** I was also <sup>①</sup>blameless with Him,<br>And I kept myself from my wrongdoing.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>complete;</i> or <i>having integrity;</i> or <i>perfect</i>  
**18:24** Therefore the LORD has repaid me according to my righteousness,<br>According to the cleanness of my hands in His eyes.  
**18:25** With the faithful You show Yourself faithful;<br>With the <sup>①</sup>blameless You prove Yourself blameless;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>See note v 23  
**18:26** With the pure You show Yourself pure,<br>And with the crooked You show Yourself <sup>①</sup>astute.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>twisted</i>  
**18:27** For You save an afflicted people,<br>But You humiliate haughty eyes.  
**18:28** For You light my lamp;<br>The LORD my God illumines my darkness.  
**18:29** For by You I can run at a <sup>①</sup>troop of warriors;<br>And by my God I can leap over a wall.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>wall</i>  
**18:30** As for God, His way is <sup>①</sup>blameless;<br>The word of the LORD is refined;<br>He is a shield to all who take refuge in Him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>perfect</i>  
**18:31** For who is God, but the LORD?<br>And who is a rock, except our God,  
**18:32** The God who encircles me with strength,<br>And <sup>①</sup>makes my way <sup>②</sup>blameless?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>has made</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>complete;</i> or <i>having integrity</i>  
**18:33** He makes my feet like deer’s <i>feet,</i><br>And sets me up on my high places.  
**18:34** He trains my hands for battle,<br>So that my arms can bend a bow of bronze.  
**18:35** You have also given me the shield of Your salvation,<br>And Your right hand upholds me;<br>And Your <sup>①</sup>gentleness makes me great.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>condescension</i>  
**18:36** You enlarge my steps under me,<br>And my <sup>①</sup>feet have not slipped.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>ankles</i>  
**18:37** I pursued my enemies and overtook them,<br>And I did not turn back until they were consumed.  
**18:38** I shattered them, so that they were not able to rise;<br>They fell under my feet.  
**18:39** For You have encircled me with strength for battle;<br>You have forced those who rose up against me to bow down under me.  
**18:40** You have also made my enemies turn their backs to me,<br>And I <sup>①</sup>destroyed those who hated me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>silenced</i>  
**18:41** They cried for help, but there was no one to save,<br><i>They cried</i> to the LORD, but He did not answer them.  
**18:42** Then I beat them fine like the dust before the wind;<br>I emptied them out like the mud of the streets.  
**18:43** You have rescued me from the contentions of the people;<br>You have placed me as head of the nations;<br>A people whom I have not known serve me.  
**18:44** As soon as they hear, they obey me;<br>Foreigners pretend to obey me.  
**18:45** Foreigners lose heart,<br>And come trembling out of their <sup>①</sup>fortresses.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>prisons</i>  
**18:46** The LORD lives, and blessed be my rock;<br>And exalted be the God of my salvation,  
**18:47** The God who executes vengeance for me,<br>And subdues peoples under me.  
**18:48** He rescues me from my enemies;<br>You indeed lift me above those who rise up against me;<br>You rescue me from a violent man.  
**18:49** Therefore I will give thanks to You among the nations, LORD,<br>And I will sing praises to Your name.  
**18:50** He gives great <sup>①</sup>salvation to His king,<br>And shows faithfulness to His anointed,<br>To David and his <sup>②</sup>descendants forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., victories; lit <i>salvations</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  

## 19장

**19:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>The heavens tell of the glory of God;<br>And their expanse declares the work of His hands.  
**19:2** Day to day pours forth speech,<br>And night to night reveals knowledge.  
**19:3** There is no speech, nor are there words;<br>Their voice is not heard.  
**19:4** Their <sup>①</sup>line has gone out into all the earth,<br>And their words to the end of the world.<br>In them He has placed a tent for the sun,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Another reading is <i>sound</i>  
**19:5** Which is like a groom coming out of his chamber;<br>It rejoices like a strong person to run his course.  
**19:6** Its rising is from <sup>①</sup>one end of the heavens,<br>And its circuit to the <sup>②</sup>other end of them;<br>And there is nothing hidden from its heat.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the end</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>the ends</i>  
**19:7** The Law of the LORD is <sup>①</sup>perfect, restoring the soul;<br>The testimony of the LORD is sure, making wise the simple.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., blameless  
**19:8** The precepts of the LORD are right, rejoicing the heart;<br>The commandment of the LORD is pure, enlightening the eyes.  
**19:9** The fear of the LORD is clean, enduring forever;<br>The judgments of the LORD are true; they are righteous altogether.  
**19:10** They are more desirable than gold, yes, than much pure gold;<br>Sweeter also than honey and drippings of the honeycomb.  
**19:11** Moreover, Your servant is warned by them;<br>In keeping them there is great reward.  
**19:12** Who can discern <i>his</i> errors? Acquit me of hidden <i>faults.</i>  
**19:13** Also keep Your servant back from presumptuous <i>sins;</i><br>Let them not rule over me;<br>Then I will be innocent,<br>And I will be blameless of great wrongdoing.  
**19:14** May the words of my mouth and the meditation of my heart<br>Be acceptable in Your sight,<br>LORD, my rock and my Redeemer.  

## 20장

**20:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>May the LORD answer you on a day of trouble!<br>May the name of the God of Jacob <sup>①</sup>protect you!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>make you high and inaccessible</i>  
**20:2** May He send you help from the sanctuary,<br>And support you from Zion!  
**20:3** May He remember all your meal offerings<br>And <sup>①</sup>accept your burnt offering! <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>consider your...as fat</i>  
**20:4** May He grant you your heart’s desire<br>And fulfill your whole plan!  
**20:5**  <sup>①</sup>We will sing for joy over your <sup>②</sup>victory,<br>And in the name of our God we will set up our banners.<br>May the LORD fulfill all your desires.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Let’s sing</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>salvation</i>  
**20:6** Now I know that the LORD saves His anointed;<br>He will answer him from His holy heaven<br>With the <sup>①</sup>saving strength of His right hand.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>mighty deeds of the victory of His right hand</i>  
**20:7** Some <i>praise their</i> chariots and some <i>their</i> horses,<br>But we will praise the name of the LORD, our God.  
**20:8** They have bowed down and fallen,<br>But we have risen and stood upright.  
**20:9**  <sup>①</sup>Save, LORD;<br>May the King answer us on the day we call.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>O LORD, save the king; answer us</i>  

## 21장

**21:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>LORD, in Your strength the king will be glad,<br>And in Your <sup>①</sup>salvation how greatly he will rejoice!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>victory</i>  
**21:2** You have given him his heart’s desire,<br>And You have not withheld the request of his lips. <i>Selah</i>  
**21:3** For You meet him with the blessings of good things;<br>You set a crown of pure gold on his head.  
**21:4** He asked for life from You,<br>You gave it to him,<br>Length of days forever and ever.  
**21:5** His glory is great through Your <sup>①</sup>salvation,<br>Splendor and majesty You place upon him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>victory</i>  
**21:6** For You make him <sup>①</sup>most blessed forever;<br>You make him joyful with the joy of Your presence.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>blessings</i>  
**21:7** For the king trusts in the LORD,<br>And through the faithfulness of the Most High he will not be shaken.  
**21:8** Your hand will find all your enemies;<br>Your right hand will find those who hate you.  
**21:9** You will make them as a fiery oven in the time <sup>①</sup>of your anger;<br>The LORD will swallow them up in His wrath,<br>And fire will devour them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>of your presence</i>  
**21:10** You will eliminate their <sup>①</sup>descendants from the earth,<br>And their <sup>②</sup>children from among the sons of mankind.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>fruit</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**21:11** Though they <sup>①</sup>intended evil against You<br><i>And</i> devised a plot,<br>They will not succeed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>stretched out</i>  
**21:12** For You will make them turn their back;<br>You will take aim at their faces with Your bowstrings.  
**21:13** Be exalted, LORD, in Your strength;<br>We will sing and praise Your power.  

## 22장

**22:1** [For the music director; upon <sup>①</sup>Aijeleth Hashshahar. A Psalm of David.]<br>My God, my God, why have You forsaken me?<br>Far from my <sup>②</sup>help are the words of my <sup>③</sup>groaning.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit the doe of the morning <sup>②</sup>Or <i>deliverance</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>screaming</i>  
**22:2** My God, I cry out by day, but You do not answer;<br>And by night, but <sup>①</sup>I have no rest.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>there is no silence for me</i>  
**22:3** Yet You are holy,<br>You who <sup>①</sup>are enthroned upon the praises of Israel.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>inhabit the praises</i>  
**22:4** In You our fathers trusted;<br>They trusted and You rescued them.  
**22:5** To You they cried out and they fled to safety;<br>In You they trusted and were not <sup>①</sup>disappointed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>ashamed</i>  
**22:6** But I am a worm and not a person,<br>A disgrace of mankind and despised by the people.  
**22:7** All who see me deride me;<br>They <sup>①</sup>sneer, they shake their heads, <i>saying,</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>open with the lip</i>  
**22:8** “ <sup>①</sup>Turn <sup>②</sup><i>him</i> over to the LORD; let Him save him;<br>Let Him rescue him, because He delights in him.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Roll...away to</i> <sup>②</sup>Or it  
**22:9** Yet You are He who brought me forth from the womb;<br>You made me trust <i>when</i> upon my mother’s breasts.  
**22:10** I was cast upon You from <sup>①</sup>birth;<br>You have been my God from my mother’s womb.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit a <i>womb</i>  
**22:11** Do not be far from me, for <sup>①</sup>trouble is near;<br>For there is no one to help.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>distress</i>  
**22:12** Many bulls have surrounded me;<br>Strong <i>bulls</i> of Bashan have encircled me.  
**22:13** They open their mouths wide at me,<br>As a ravening and roaring lion.  
**22:14** I am poured out like water,<br>And all my bones are out of joint;<br>My heart is like wax;<br>It is melted within <sup>①</sup>me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>my inward parts</i>  
**22:15** My strength is dried up like a piece of pottery,<br>And my tongue clings to my jaws;<br>And You lay me <sup>①</sup>in the dust of death.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to</i>  
**22:16** For dogs have surrounded me;<br> <sup>①</sup>A band of evildoers has encompassed me;<br> <sup>②</sup>They pierced my hands and my feet.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>An assembly</i> <sup>②</sup>Another reading is <i>Like a lion, my...</i>  
**22:17** I can count all my bones.<br>They look, they stare at me;  
**22:18** They divide my garments among them,<br>And they cast lots for my clothing.  
**22:19** But You, LORD, do not be far away;<br><i>You who are</i> my help, hurry to my assistance.  
**22:20** Save my <sup>①</sup>soul from the sword,<br>My only <i>life</i> from the <sup>②</sup>power of the dog.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>life</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>paw</i>  
**22:21** Save me from the lion’s mouth;<br>From the horns of the wild oxen You answer me.  
**22:22** I will proclaim Your name to my brothers;<br>In the midst of the assembly I will praise You.  
**22:23** You who fear the LORD, praise Him;<br>All you <sup>①</sup>descendants of Jacob, glorify Him,<br>And stand in awe of Him, all you <sup>②</sup>descendants of Israel.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**22:24** For He has not despised nor scorned the suffering of the afflicted;<br>Nor has He hidden His face from him;<br>But when he cried to Him for help, He heard.  
**22:25** From You <i>comes</i> my praise in the great assembly;<br>I shall pay my vows before those who fear Him.  
**22:26** The <sup>①</sup>afflicted will eat and be satisfied;<br>Those who seek Him will praise the LORD.<br>May your heart live forever!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>poor</i>  
**22:27** All the ends of the earth will remember and turn to the LORD,<br>And all the families of the nations will worship before <sup>①</sup>You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some ancient versions <i>Him</i>  
**22:28** For the kingdom is the LORD’S<br>And He rules over the nations.  
**22:29** All the <sup>①</sup>prosperous of the earth will eat and worship,<br>All those who go down to the dust will kneel before Him,<br>Even he who <sup>②</sup>cannot keep his soul alive.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>fat ones</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>did not</i>  
**22:30** A <sup>①</sup>posterity will serve Him;<br>It will be told of the Lord to the <i>coming</i> generation.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**22:31** They will come and will declare His righteousness<br>To a people who will be born, that He has performed <i>it.</i>  

## 23장

**23:1** [A Psalm of David.]<br>The LORD is my shepherd,<br>I will not be in need.  
**23:2** He lets me lie down in green pastures;<br>He leads me beside <sup>①</sup>quiet waters.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>waters of rest</i>  
**23:3** He restores my soul;<br>He guides me in the <sup>①</sup>paths of righteousness<br>For the sake of His name.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>tracks</i>  
**23:4** Even though I walk through the <sup>①</sup>valley of the shadow of death,<br>I fear no <sup>②</sup>evil, for You are with me;<br>Your rod and Your staff, they comfort me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>valley of deep darkness</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>harm</i>  
**23:5** You prepare a table before me in the presence of my enemies;<br>You <sup>①</sup>have anointed my head with oil;<br>My cup overflows.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>anoint</i>  
**23:6**  <sup>①</sup>Certainly goodness and <sup>②</sup>faithfulness will follow me all the days of my life,<br>And my dwelling <i>will be</i> in the house of the LORD <sup>③</sup>forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Only</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>mercy</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>for length of days</i>  

## 24장

**24:1** [A Psalm of David.]<br>The earth is the LORD’S, and <sup>①</sup>all it contains,<br>The world, and those who live in it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>its fullness</i>  
**24:2** For He has founded it upon the seas<br>And established it upon the rivers.  
**24:3** Who may ascend onto the hill of the LORD?<br>And who may stand in His holy place?  
**24:4** One who has clean hands and a pure heart,<br>Who has not lifted up his soul to deceit<br>And has not sworn deceitfully.  
**24:5** He will receive a blessing from the LORD<br>And <sup>①</sup>righteousness from the God of his salvation.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., as vindicated  
**24:6**  <sup>①</sup>This is the generation of those who seek Him,<br>Who seek Your face—<i>even</i> Jacob. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Such</i>  
**24:7** Lift up your heads, you gates,<br>And be lifted up, you <sup>①</sup>ancient doors,<br>That the King of glory may come in!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>everlasting</i>  
**24:8** Who is the King of glory?<br>The LORD strong and mighty,<br>The LORD mighty in battle.  
**24:9** Lift up your heads, you gates,<br>And lift <i>them</i> up, you <sup>①</sup>ancient doors,<br>That the King of glory may come in!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>everlasting</i>  
**24:10** Who is this King of glory?<br>The LORD of armies,<br>He is the King of glory. <i>Selah</i>  

## 25장

**25:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>To You, LORD, I lift up my soul.  
**25:2** My God, in You I trust,<br>Do not let me be ashamed;<br>Do not let my enemies rejoice over me.  
**25:3** Indeed, none of those who wait for You will be ashamed;<br> <sup>①</sup>Those who deal treacherously without cause will be ashamed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Let those...be ashamed</i>  
**25:4** Make me know Your ways, LORD;<br>Teach me Your paths.  
**25:5** Lead me in Your truth and teach me,<br>For You are the God of my salvation;<br>For You I wait all the day.  
**25:6** Remember, LORD, Your compassion and Your faithfulness,<br>For they have been <sup>①</sup>from of old.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>everlasting</i>  
**25:7** Do not remember the sins of my youth or my wrongdoings;<br>Remember me according to Your faithfulness,<br>For Your goodness’ sake, LORD.  
**25:8** The LORD is good and upright;<br>Therefore He instructs sinners in the way.  
**25:9** He leads the <sup>①</sup>humble in justice,<br>And He teaches the <sup>②</sup>humble His way.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>afflicted</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>afflicted</i>  
**25:10** All the paths of the LORD are faithfulness and truth<br>To those who comply with His covenant and His testimonies.  
**25:11** For the sake of Your name, LORD,<br>Forgive my wrongdoing, for it is great.  
**25:12** Who is the person who fears the LORD?<br>He will instruct him in the way he should choose.  
**25:13** His soul will dwell in <sup>①</sup>prosperity,<br>And his <sup>②</sup>descendants will inherit the <sup>③</sup>land.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>good</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>seed</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>earth</i>  
**25:14** The <sup>①</sup>secret of the LORD is for those who fear Him,<br> <sup>②</sup>And He will make them know His covenant.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>counsel;</i> or <i>intimacy</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>And His covenant, to make them know it</i>  
**25:15** My eyes are continually toward the LORD,<br>For He will <sup>①</sup>rescue my feet from the net.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>bring out</i>  
**25:16** Turn to me and be gracious to me,<br>For I am lonely and afflicted.  
**25:17**  <sup>①</sup>The troubles of my heart are enlarged;<br>Bring me out of my distresses.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some commentators <i>Relieve the troubles of my heart</i>  
**25:18** Look at my misery and my trouble,<br>And forgive all my sins.  
**25:19** Look at my enemies, for they are many,<br>And they hate me with violent hatred.  
**25:20** Guard my soul and save me;<br>Do not let me be ashamed, for I take refuge in You.  
**25:21** Let integrity and uprightness protect me,<br>For I wait for You.  
**25:22** Redeem Israel, God,<br>From all his distress.  

## 26장

**26:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br> <sup>①</sup>Vindicate me, LORD, for I have walked in my integrity,<br>And I have trusted in the LORD <sup>②</sup>without wavering.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Judge</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>I do not slide</i>  
**26:2** Examine me, LORD, and put me to the test;<br>Refine my <sup>①</sup>mind and my heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>kidneys,</i> figurative for inner man  
**26:3** For Your <sup>①</sup>goodness is before my eyes,<br>And I have walked in Your truth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**26:4** I do not sit with <sup>①</sup>deceitful people,<br>Nor will I go with <sup>②</sup>pretenders.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>worthless men;</i> lit <i>men of falsehood</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>dissemblers, hypocrites</i>  
**26:5** I hate the assembly of evildoers,<br>And I will not sit with the wicked.  
**26:6** I will wash my hands in innocence,<br>And I will go around Your altar, LORD,  
**26:7** That I may proclaim with the voice of thanksgiving<br>And declare all Your <sup>①</sup>wonders.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>miracles</i>  
**26:8** LORD, I love the dwelling of Your house,<br>And the place <sup>①</sup>where Your glory remains.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of the tabernacle of Your glory</i>  
**26:9** Do not <sup>①</sup>take my soul away <i>along</i> with sinners,<br>Nor my life with men of bloodshed,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>gather</i>  
**26:10** In whose hands is a wicked scheme,<br>And whose right hand is full of bribes.  
**26:11** But as for me, I will walk in my integrity;<br>Redeem me, and be gracious to me.  
**26:12** My foot stands on level ground;<br>In the congregations I will bless the LORD.  

## 27장

**27:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>The LORD is my light and my salvation;<br>Whom should I fear?<br>The LORD is the <sup>①</sup>defense of my life;<br>Whom should I dread?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>refuge</i>  
**27:2** When evildoers came upon me to devour my flesh,<br>My adversaries and my enemies, they stumbled and fell.  
**27:3** If an army encamps against me,<br>My heart will not fear;<br>If war arises against me,<br>In <i>spite of</i> this I am confident.  
**27:4** One thing I have asked from the LORD, that I shall seek:<br>That I may dwell in the house of the LORD all the days of my life,<br>To behold the <sup>①</sup>beauty of the LORD<br>And to <sup>②</sup>meditate in His temple.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>delightfulness</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>inquire</i>  
**27:5** For on the day of trouble He will conceal me in His <sup>①</sup>tabernacle;<br>He will hide me in the secret place of His tent;<br>He will lift me up on a rock.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>shelter</i>  
**27:6** And now my head will be lifted up above my enemies around me,<br>And I will offer sacrifices in His tent <sup>①</sup>with shouts of joy;<br>I will sing, yes, I will sing praises to the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of shouts</i>  
**27:7** Hear, LORD, when I cry with my voice,<br>And be gracious to me and answer me.  
**27:8** <i>When You said,</i> “Seek My face,” my heart said to You,<br>“I shall seek Your face, LORD.”  
**27:9** Do not hide Your face from me,<br>Do not turn Your servant away in anger;<br>You have been my help;<br>Do not abandon me nor forsake me,<br>God of my salvation!  
**27:10**  <sup>①</sup>For my father and my mother have forsaken me,<br>But the LORD will take me up.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>If my father...forsake me, Then the LORD</i>  
**27:11** Teach me Your way, LORD,<br>And lead me on a level path<br>Because of my enemies.  
**27:12** Do not turn me over to the <sup>①</sup>desire of my enemies,<br>For false witnesses have risen against me,<br>And <i>the</i> violent witness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>soul</i>  
**27:13** I certainly believed that I would see the goodness of the LORD<br>In the land of the living.  
**27:14** Wait for the LORD;<br>Be strong and let your heart take courage;<br>Yes, wait for the LORD.  

## 28장

**28:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>To You, LORD, I call;<br>My rock, do not be deaf to me,<br>For if You are silent to me,<br>I will become like those who go down to the pit.  
**28:2** Hear the sound of my pleadings when I cry to You for help,<br>When I raise my hands toward <sup>①</sup>Your holy sanctuary.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the innermost place of Your sanctuary</i>  
**28:3** Do not drag me away with the wicked<br>And with those who practice injustice,<br>Who speak peace with their neighbors,<br>While evil is in their hearts.  
**28:4** Give <i>back</i> to them according to their work and according to the evil of their practices;<br>Give <i>back</i> to them according to the work of their hands;<br>Repay them what is due them.  
**28:5** Because they do not regard the works of the LORD<br>Nor the deeds of His hands,<br>He will tear them down and not build them up.  
**28:6** Blessed be the LORD,<br>Because He has heard the sound of my pleading.  
**28:7** The LORD is my strength and my shield;<br>My heart trusts in Him, and I am helped;<br>Therefore my heart triumphs,<br>And with my song I shall thank Him.  
**28:8** The LORD is <sup>①</sup>their strength,<br>And He is a refuge of salvation to His anointed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>A few mss and ancient versions <i>the strength of His people</i>  
**28:9** Save Your people and bless Your inheritance;<br>Be their shepherd also, and carry them forever.  

## 29장

**29:1** [A Psalm of David.]<br>Ascribe to the LORD, <sup>①</sup>sons of the mighty,<br>Ascribe to the LORD glory and strength.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>sons of gods</i>  
**29:2** Ascribe to the LORD the glory <sup>①</sup>due His name;<br>Worship the LORD in holy attire.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of His name</i>  
**29:3** The voice of the LORD is on the waters;<br>The God of glory thunders,<br>The LORD is over <sup>①</sup>many waters.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>great</i>  
**29:4** The voice of the LORD is powerful,<br>The voice of the LORD is majestic.  
**29:5** The voice of the LORD breaks the cedars;<br>Yes, the LORD breaks the cedars of Lebanon in pieces.  
**29:6** He makes Lebanon skip like a calf,<br>And Sirion like a young wild ox.  
**29:7** The voice of the LORD divides <sup>①</sup>flames of fire.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., lightning  
**29:8** The voice of the LORD <sup>①</sup>shakes the wilderness;<br>The LORD shakes the wilderness of Kadesh.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>causes...to whirl</i>  
**29:9** The voice of the LORD makes the deer give birth<br>And strips the forests bare;<br>And in His temple everything says, “Glory!”  
**29:10** The LORD sat <i>as King</i> at the flood;<br>Yes, the LORD sits as King forever.  
**29:11**  <sup>①</sup>The LORD will give strength to His people;<br> <sup>②</sup>The LORD will bless His people with peace.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>May the LORD</i> give <sup>②</sup>Or <i>May the LORD</i> bless  

## 30장

**30:1** [A Psalm; a Song at the Dedication of the House. <i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>I will exalt You, LORD, for You have lifted me up,<br>And have not let my enemies rejoice over me.  
**30:2** LORD my God,<br>I cried to You for help, and You healed me.  
**30:3** LORD, You have brought up my soul from <sup>①</sup>Sheol;<br>You have kept me alive, <sup>②</sup>that I would not go down to the pit.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the netherworld <sup>②</sup>Some mss <i>from among those who go down</i>  
**30:4** Sing praise to the LORD, you His godly ones,<br>And praise the mention of His holiness.  
**30:5** For His anger is but for a moment,<br>His favor is for a lifetime;<br>Weeping may last for the night,<br>But a shout of joy <i>comes</i> in the morning.  
**30:6** Now as for me, I said in my prosperity,<br>“I will never be moved.”  
**30:7** LORD, by Your favor You have made my mountain to stand strong;<br>You hid Your face, I was dismayed.  
**30:8** To You, LORD, I called,<br>And to the Lord I pleaded for compassion:  
**30:9** “What gain is there in my blood, if I go down to the pit?<br>Will the dust praise You? Will it declare Your faithfulness?  
**30:10** “Hear, LORD, and be gracious to me;<br>LORD, be my helper.”  
**30:11** You have turned my mourning into dancing for me;<br>You have untied my sackcloth and encircled me with joy,  
**30:12** That <i>my</i> <sup>①</sup>soul may sing praise to You and not be silent.<br>LORD my God, I will give thanks to You forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>glory</i>  

## 31장

**31:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>In You, LORD, I have taken refuge;<br>Let me never be <sup>①</sup>put to shame;<br>In Your righteousness rescue me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>ashamed;</i>  
**31:2** Incline Your ear to me, rescue me quickly;<br>Be a rock of <sup>①</sup>strength for me,<br>A <sup>②</sup>stronghold to save me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>refuge, protection</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>house of a stronghold</i>  
**31:3** For You are my rock and my fortress;<br>For the sake of Your name You will lead me and guide me.  
**31:4** You will pull me out of the net which they have secretly laid for me,<br>For You are my strength.  
**31:5** Into Your hand I entrust my spirit;<br>You have redeemed me, LORD, God of <sup>①</sup>truth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**31:6** I hate those who devote themselves to worthless <sup>①</sup>idols,<br>But I trust in the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>futilities</i>  
**31:7** I will rejoice and be glad in Your faithfulness,<br>Because You have seen my misery;<br>You have known the troubles of my soul,  
**31:8** And You have not handed me over to the enemy;<br>You have set my feet in a large place.  
**31:9** Be gracious to me, LORD, for I am in distress;<br>My eye is wasted away from grief, my soul and my body <i>too.</i>  
**31:10** For my life is spent with sorrow<br>And my years with sighing;<br>My strength has failed because of my guilt,<br>And my <sup>①</sup>body has wasted away.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>bones, substance</i>  
**31:11** Because of all my adversaries, I have become a disgrace,<br>Especially to my neighbors,<br>And an object of dread to my acquaintances;<br>Those who see me in the street flee from me.  
**31:12** I am forgotten like a dead person, out of mind;<br>I am like a broken vessel.  
**31:13** For I have heard the <sup>①</sup>slander of many,<br>Terror is on every side;<br>While they took counsel together against me,<br>They schemed to take away my life.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>whispering</i>  
**31:14** But as for me, I trust in You, LORD,<br>I say, “You are my God.”  
**31:15** My times are in Your hand;<br>Rescue me from the hand of my enemies and from those who persecute me.  
**31:16** Make Your face shine upon Your servant;<br>Save me in Your faithfulness.  
**31:17** Let me not be put to shame, LORD, for I call upon You;<br>Let the wicked be put to shame, let them be silent in <sup>①</sup>Sheol.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**31:18** Let the lying lips be speechless,<br>Which speak arrogantly against the righteous<br>With pride and contempt.  
**31:19** How great is Your goodness,<br>Which You have stored up for those who fear You,<br>Which You have performed for those who take refuge in You,<br>Before the sons of mankind!  
**31:20** You hide them in the secret place of Your presence from the conspiracies of mankind;<br>You keep them secretly in a <sup>①</sup>shelter from the strife of tongues.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>pavilion</i>  
**31:21** Blessed be the LORD,<br>For He has shown His marvelous faithfulness to me in a besieged city.  
**31:22** As for me, I said in my alarm,<br>“I am cut off from Your eyes”;<br>Nevertheless You heard the sound of my pleadings<br>When I called to You for help.  
**31:23** Love the LORD, all His godly ones!<br>The LORD watches over the faithful<br>But fully repays the one who acts arrogantly.  
**31:24** Be strong and let your heart take courage,<br>All you who wait for the LORD.  

## 32장

**32:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David. A <sup>①</sup>Maskil.]<br>How blessed is he whose wrongdoing is forgiven,<br>Whose sin is covered!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm  
**32:2** How blessed is a person whose guilt the LORD does not take into account,<br>And in whose spirit there is no deceit!  
**32:3** When I kept silent <i>about my sin,</i> my <sup>①</sup>body wasted away<br>Through my <sup>②</sup>groaning all day long.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>bones, substance</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>roaring</i>  
**32:4** For day and night Your hand was heavy upon me;<br>My <sup>①</sup>vitality failed <i>as</i> with the dry heat of summer. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>life juices were turned into the drought of summer</i>  
**32:5** I acknowledged my sin to You,<br>And I did not hide my guilt;<br>I said, “I will confess my wrongdoings to the LORD”;<br>And You forgave the guilt of my sin. <i>Selah</i>  
**32:6** Therefore, let everyone who is godly pray to You <sup>①</sup>in a time when You may be found;<br>Certainly in a flood of great waters, they will not reach him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in a time of finding out</i>  
**32:7** You are my hiding place; You keep me from trouble;<br>You surround me with <sup>①</sup>songs of deliverance. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>shouts</i>  
**32:8** I will instruct you and teach you in the way which you should go;<br>I will advise you with My eye upon you.  
**32:9** Do not be like the horse or like the mule, which have no understanding,<br>Whose trappings include bit and bridle to hold them in check,<br><i>Otherwise</i> they will not come near to you.  
**32:10** The sorrows of the wicked are many,<br>But the one who trusts in the LORD, goodness will surround him.  
**32:11** Be glad in the LORD and rejoice, you righteous ones;<br>And shout for joy, all you who are upright in heart.  

## 33장

**33:1** Sing for joy in the LORD, you righteous ones;<br>Praise is becoming to the upright.  
**33:2** Give thanks to the LORD with the lyre;<br>Sing praises to Him with a harp of ten strings.  
**33:3** Sing to Him a new song;<br>Play skillfully with a shout of joy.  
**33:4** For the word of the LORD is right,<br>And all His work is <i>done</i> in faithfulness.  
**33:5** He loves righteousness and justice;<br>The earth is full of the goodness of the LORD.  
**33:6** By the word of the LORD the heavens were made,<br>And by the breath of His mouth all their <sup>①</sup>lights.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>host;</i> i.e., sun, stars, etc.  
**33:7** He gathers the waters of the sea together <sup>①</sup>as a heap;<br>He puts the depths in storehouses.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some ancient versions <i>in a water skin;</i> i.e., container  
**33:8** Let all the earth fear the LORD;<br>Let all the inhabitants of the world stand in awe of Him.  
**33:9** For He spoke, and it was done;<br>He commanded, and it <sup>①</sup>stood firm.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>stood forth</i>  
**33:10** The LORD nullifies the plan of nations;<br>He frustrates the plans of peoples.  
**33:11** The plan of the LORD stands forever,<br>The plans of His heart from generation to generation.  
**33:12** Blessed is the nation whose God is the LORD,<br>The people He has chosen for His own inheritance.  
**33:13** The LORD looks from heaven;<br>He sees all the sons of mankind;  
**33:14** From His dwelling place He looks out<br>On all the inhabitants of the earth,  
**33:15** He who fashions <sup>①</sup>the hearts of them all,<br>He who understands all their works.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>their heart together</i>  
**33:16** The king is not saved by a mighty army;<br>A warrior is not rescued by great strength.  
**33:17** A horse is a false hope for victory;<br>Nor does it rescue anyone by its great strength.  
**33:18** Behold, the eye of the LORD is on those who fear Him,<br>On those who wait for His faithfulness,  
**33:19** To rescue their soul from death<br>And to keep them alive in famine.  
**33:20** Our soul waits for the LORD;<br>He is our help and our shield.  
**33:21** For our heart rejoices in Him,<br>Because we trust in His holy name.  
**33:22** Let Your favor, LORD, be upon us,<br>Just as we have waited for You.  

## 34장

**34:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David, when he pretended to be insane before <sup>①</sup>Abimelech, who drove him away, and he departed.]<br>I will bless the LORD at all times;<br>His praise shall continually be in my mouth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly a title of King Achish of Gath, see 1 Sam 21:10-15  
**34:2** My soul will make its boast in the LORD;<br>The humble will hear it and rejoice.  
**34:3** Exalt the LORD with me,<br>And let’s exalt His name together.  
**34:4** I sought the LORD and He answered me,<br>And rescued me from all my fears.  
**34:5** They looked to Him and were radiant,<br>And their faces will never be ashamed.  
**34:6** This wretched man cried out, and the LORD heard <i>him,</i><br>And saved him out of all his troubles.  
**34:7** The angel of the LORD encamps around those who fear Him,<br>And rescues them.  
**34:8** Taste and see that the LORD is good;<br>How blessed is the man who takes refuge in Him!  
**34:9** Fear the LORD, you His saints;<br>For to those who fear Him there is no lack <i>of anything.</i>  
**34:10** The young lions do without and suffer hunger;<br>But they who seek the LORD will not lack any good thing.  
**34:11** Come, you children, listen to me;<br>I will teach you the fear of the LORD.  
**34:12** Who is the person who desires life<br>And loves <i>length of</i> days, that he may see good?  
**34:13** Keep your tongue from evil<br>And your lips from speaking deceit.  
**34:14** Turn from evil and do good;<br>Seek peace and pursue it.  
**34:15** The eyes of the LORD are toward the righteous,<br>And His ears are toward their cry for help.  
**34:16** The face of the LORD is against evildoers,<br>To eliminate the memory of them from the earth.  
**34:17** <i>The righteous</i> cry out, and the LORD hears<br>And rescues them from all their troubles.  
**34:18** The LORD is near to the brokenhearted<br>And saves those who are <sup>①</sup>crushed in spirit.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>contrite</i>  
**34:19** The afflictions of the righteous are many,<br>But the LORD rescues him from them all.  
**34:20** He protects all his bones,<br>Not one of them is broken.  
**34:21** Evil will bring death to the wicked,<br>And those who hate the righteous will suffer for their guilt.  
**34:22** The LORD redeems the souls of His servants,<br>And none of those who take refuge in Him will suffer for their guilt.  

## 35장

**35:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>Contend, LORD, with those who contend with me;<br>Fight against those who fight against me.  
**35:2** Take hold of <sup>①</sup>buckler and shield<br>And rise up as my help.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., small shield  
**35:3** Draw also the spear and <sup>①</sup>the battle-axe to meet those who pursue me;<br>Say to my soul, “I am your salvation.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>close up the path against those</i>  
**35:4** Let those be ashamed and dishonored who seek my <sup>①</sup>life;<br>Let those be turned back and humiliated who devise evil against me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>soul</i>  
**35:5** Let them be like chaff before the wind,<br>With the angel of the LORD driving <i>them</i> on.  
**35:6** Let their way be dark and slippery,<br>With the angel of the LORD pursuing them.  
**35:7** For they hid their net for me without cause;<br>Without cause they dug a pit for my soul.  
**35:8** Let destruction come upon him when he is unaware,<br>And let the net which he hid catch him;<br>Let him fall into that very destruction.  
**35:9** So my soul shall rejoice in the LORD;<br>It shall rejoice in His salvation.  
**35:10** All my bones will say, “LORD, who is like You,<br>Who rescues the afflicted from one who is too strong for him,<br>And the afflicted and the poor from one who robs him?”  
**35:11** Malicious witnesses rise up;<br>They ask me things that I do not know.  
**35:12** They repay me evil for good,<br><i>To</i> the bereavement of my soul.  
**35:13** But as for me, when they were sick, my clothing was sackcloth;<br>I humbled my soul with fasting,<br>But my prayer kept returning to <sup>①</sup>me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>my chest</i>  
**35:14** I went about as though it were my friend or brother;<br>I bowed down <sup>①</sup>in mourning, like one who mourns for a mother.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>dressed for mourning</i>  
**35:15** But at my <sup>①</sup>stumbling they rejoiced and gathered themselves together;<br>The <sup>②</sup>afflicted people whom I did not know gathered together against me,<br>They <sup>③</sup>slandered me without ceasing.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>limping</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>struck ones</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>tore</i>  
**35:16** Like godless jesters at a feast,<br>They gnashed at me with their teeth.  
**35:17** Lord, how long will You look on?<br>Rescue my soul from their ravages,<br>My only <i>life</i> from the lions.  
**35:18** I will give You thanks in the great congregation;<br>I will praise You among a mighty people.  
**35:19** Do not let those who are wrongfully my enemies rejoice over me;<br>Nor let those who hate me for no reason <sup>①</sup>wink maliciously.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>wink the eye</i>  
**35:20** For they do not speak peace,<br>But they devise deceitful words against those who are quiet in the land.  
**35:21** They opened their mouth wide against me;<br>They said, “Aha, aha! Our eyes have seen it!”  
**35:22** You have seen it, LORD, do not keep silent;<br>Lord, do not be far from me.  
**35:23** Stir Yourself, and awake to my right<br>And to my cause, my God and my Lord.  
**35:24** Judge me, LORD my God, according to Your righteousness,<br>And do not let them rejoice over me.  
**35:25** Do not let them say in their heart, “Aha, our desire!”<br>Do not let them say, “We have swallowed him up!”  
**35:26** May those be ashamed and altogether humiliated who rejoice at my distress;<br>May those who exalt themselves over me be clothed with shame and dishonor.  
**35:27** May those shout for joy and rejoice, who take delight in my vindication;<br>And may they say continually, “The LORD be exalted,<br>Who delights in the prosperity of His servant.”  
**35:28** And my tongue shall proclaim Your righteousness<br><i>And</i> Your praise all day long.  

## 36장

**36:1** [For the music director. <i>A Psalm</i> of David the servant of the LORD.]<br> <sup>①</sup>Wrongdoing speaks to the ungodly within <sup>②</sup>his heart;<br>There is no fear of God before his eyes.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Rebellion</i> <sup>②</sup>Another reading is <i>my heart</i>  
**36:2** For it flatters him in his <i>own</i> eyes<br>Concerning the discovery of his wrongful deed <i>and</i> the hatred <i>of it.</i>  
**36:3** The words of his mouth are wickedness and deceit;<br>He has ceased to <sup>①</sup>be wise <i>and</i> to do good.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>understand to do good</i>  
**36:4** He plans wickedness on his bed;<br>He sets himself on a path that is not good;<br>He does not reject evil.  
**36:5** Your mercy, LORD, <sup>①</sup>extends to the heavens,<br>Your faithfulness <i>reaches</i> to the skies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>is in</i>  
**36:6** Your righteousness is like the <sup>①</sup>mountains of God;<br>Your judgments are <i>like</i> the great deep.<br>LORD, You <sup>②</sup>protect mankind and animals.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>mighty mountains</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>help</i>  
**36:7** How precious is Your mercy, God!<br>And the sons of mankind take refuge in the shadow of Your wings.  
**36:8** They drink their fill of the <sup>①</sup>abundance of Your house;<br>And You allow them to drink <i>from</i> the river of Your delights.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>fatness</i>  
**36:9** For the fountain of life is with You;<br>In Your light we see light.  
**36:10** Prolong Your mercy to those who know You,<br>And Your righteousness to the upright of heart.  
**36:11** May the foot of pride not come upon me,<br>And may the hand of the wicked not drive me away.  
**36:12** Those who do injustice have fallen there;<br>They have been thrust down and cannot rise.  

## 37장

**37:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>Do not get upset because of evildoers,<br>Do not be envious of wrongdoers.  
**37:2** For they will wither quickly like the grass,<br>And decay like the green plants.  
**37:3** Trust in the LORD and do good;<br>Live in the land and <sup>①</sup>cultivate faithfulness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>feed securely;</i> or <i>feed on His faithfulness</i>  
**37:4** Delight yourself in the LORD;<br>And He will give you the desires of your heart.  
**37:5** Commit your way to the LORD,<br>Trust also in Him, and He will do it.  
**37:6** He will bring out your righteousness as the light,<br>And your judgment as the noonday.  
**37:7**  <sup>①</sup>Rest in the LORD and wait <sup>②</sup>patiently for Him;<br>Do not get upset because of one who is successful in his way,<br>Because of the person who carries out wicked schemes.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Be still</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>longingly</i>  
**37:8** Cease from anger and abandon wrath;<br>Do not get upset; <i>it leads</i> only to evildoing.  
**37:9** For evildoers will be eliminated,<br>But those who wait for the LORD, they will inherit the land.  
**37:10** Yet a little while and the wicked person will be no more;<br>And you will look carefully for his place and he will not be <i>there.</i>  
**37:11** But the humble will inherit the land<br>And will delight themselves in abundant prosperity.  
**37:12** The wicked plots against the righteous,<br>And gnashes at him with his teeth.  
**37:13** The Lord laughs at him,<br>For He sees that his day is coming.  
**37:14** The wicked have drawn the sword and bent their bow<br>To take down the afflicted and the needy,<br>To kill off those who are upright in conduct.  
**37:15** Their sword will enter their own heart,<br>And their bows will be broken.  
**37:16** Better is the little of the righteous<br>Than the abundance of many wicked.  
**37:17** For the arms of the wicked will be broken,<br>But the LORD sustains the righteous.  
**37:18** The LORD knows the days of the <sup>①</sup>blameless,<br>And their inheritance will be forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>complete;</i> or <i>perfect</i>  
**37:19** They will not be ashamed in the time of evil,<br>And in the days of famine they will have plenty.  
**37:20** But the wicked will perish;<br>And the enemies of the LORD will be like the <sup>①</sup>glory of the pastures,<br>They vanish—like smoke they vanish away.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., flowers  
**37:21** The wicked borrows and does not pay back,<br>But the righteous is gracious and gives.  
**37:22** For those blessed by Him will inherit the land,<br>But those cursed by Him will be eliminated.  
**37:23** The steps of a man are established by the LORD,<br>And He delights in his way.  
**37:24** When he falls, he will not be hurled down,<br>Because the LORD is the One <sup>①</sup>who holds his hand.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>who sustains him with His hand</i>  
**37:25** I have been young and now I am old,<br>Yet I have not seen the righteous forsaken<br>Or his <sup>①</sup>descendants begging for bread.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**37:26** All day long he is gracious and lends,<br>And his <sup>①</sup>descendants are a blessing.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**37:27** Turn from evil and do good,<br>So that you will dwell forever.  
**37:28** For the LORD loves <sup>①</sup>justice<br>And does not abandon His godly ones;<br>They are protected forever,<br>But the <sup>②</sup>descendants of the wicked will be eliminated.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>judgment</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**37:29** The righteous will inherit the land<br>And dwell in it forever.  
**37:30** The mouth of the righteous utters wisdom,<br>And his tongue speaks justice.  
**37:31** The Law of his God is in his heart;<br>His steps do not slip.  
**37:32** The wicked spies upon the righteous<br>And seeks to kill him.  
**37:33** The LORD will not leave him in his hand<br>Or let him be condemned when he is judged.  
**37:34** Wait for the LORD and keep His way,<br>And He will exalt you to inherit the land;<br>When the wicked are eliminated, you will see it.  
**37:35** I have seen a wicked, violent person<br>Spreading himself like a luxuriant <sup>①</sup>tree in its native soil.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>native;</i> Heb obscure  
**37:36** Then he passed away, and behold, he was no more;<br>I searched for him, but he could not be found.  
**37:37** Observe the <sup>①</sup>blameless person, and look at the upright;<br>For the person of peace will have a future.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>complete;</i> or <i>perfect</i>  
**37:38** But wrongdoers will altogether be destroyed;<br>The future of the wicked will be eliminated.  
**37:39** But the salvation of the righteous is from the LORD;<br>He is their strength in time of trouble.  
**37:40** The LORD helps them and rescues them;<br>He rescues them from the wicked and saves them,<br>Because they take refuge in Him.  

## 38장

**38:1** [A Psalm of David, for a memorial.]<br>LORD, do not rebuke me in Your wrath,<br>And do not punish me in Your burning anger.  
**38:2** For Your arrows have sunk deep into me,<br>And Your hand has pressed down on me.  
**38:3** There is no healthy part in my flesh because of Your indignation;<br>There is no health in my bones because of my sin.  
**38:4** For my guilty deeds have gone over my head;<br>Like a heavy burden they weigh too much for me.  
**38:5** My <sup>①</sup>wounds grow foul <i>and</i> fester<br>Because of my foolishness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>stripes</i>  
**38:6** I am bent over and greatly bowed down;<br>I go in mourning all day long.  
**38:7** For my <sup>①</sup>sides are filled with burning,<br>And there is no healthy part in my flesh.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>loins</i>  
**38:8** I feel faint and <sup>①</sup>badly crushed;<br>I <sup>②</sup>groan because of the <sup>③</sup>agitation of my heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>greatly</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>roar</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>growling</i>  
**38:9** Lord, all my desire is <sup>①</sup>before You;<br>And my sighing is not hidden from You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or known <i>to You</i>  
**38:10** My heart throbs, my strength fails me;<br>And the light of my eyes, even <sup>①</sup>that <sup>②</sup>has gone from me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>they have</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>is not with me</i>
