**38:11** My <sup>①</sup>loved ones and my friends stand aloof from my plague;<br>And my kinsmen stand far away.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>lovers</i>  
**38:12** Those who seek my life lay snares <i>for me;</i><br>And those who seek to injure me have <sup>①</sup>threatened destruction,<br>And they plot deception all day long.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>spoken</i>  
**38:13** But I, like a person who is deaf, do not hear;<br>And <i>I am</i> like a person who cannot speak, <i>who</i> does not open his mouth.  
**38:14** Yes, I am like a person who does not hear,<br>And in whose mouth are no arguments.  
**38:15** For I wait for You, LORD;<br>You will answer, Lord my God.  
**38:16** For I said, “May they not rejoice over me,<br><i>Who,</i> when my foot slips, would exalt themselves over me.”  
**38:17** For I am ready to fall,<br>And my <sup>①</sup>sorrow is continually before me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>pain</i>  
**38:18** For I admit my guilt;<br>I am full of anxiety because of my sin.  
**38:19** But my enemies are vigorous <i>and</i> <sup>①</sup>strong,<br>And those who wrongfully hate me are many.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>numerous</i>  
**38:20** And those who repay evil for good,<br>They become my enemies, because I follow what is good.  
**38:21** Do not abandon me, LORD;<br>My God, do not be far from me!  
**38:22** Hurry to help me,<br>Lord, my salvation!  

## 39장

**39:1** [For the music director, for <sup>①</sup>Jeduthun. A Psalm of David.]<br>I said, “I will keep watch over my ways<br>So that I do not sin with my tongue;<br>I will keep watch over my mouth as with a muzzle<br>While the wicked are in my presence.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>1 Chr 16:41  
**39:2** I was mute <sup>①</sup>and silent,<br>I <sup>②</sup>refused to say <i>even something</i> good,<br>And my pain was stirred up.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit with <i>silence</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>was silent from good</i>  
**39:3** My heart was hot within me,<br>While I was musing the fire burned;<br><i>Then</i> I spoke with my tongue:  
**39:4** “LORD, let me know my end,<br>And what is the extent of my days;<br>Let me know how transient I am.  
**39:5** Behold, You have made my days <i>like</i> <sup>①</sup>hand widths,<br>And my lifetime as nothing in Your sight;<br>Certainly all mankind standing is <sup>②</sup>a mere breath. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., short <sup>②</sup>Or <i>altogether breath</i>  
**39:6** Certainly every person walks around as <sup>①</sup>a fleeting shadow;<br>They certainly make an uproar for nothing;<br>He amasses <i>riches</i> and does not know who will gather them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>an image</i>  
**39:7** “And now, Lord, for what do I wait?<br>My hope is in You.  
**39:8** Save me from all my wrongdoings;<br>Do not make me an <i>object of</i> reproach for the foolish.  
**39:9** I have become mute, I do not open my mouth,<br>Because it is You who have done <i>it.</i>  
**39:10** Remove Your plague from me;<br>Because of the opposition of Your hand I am <sup>①</sup>perishing.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>wasting away</i>  
**39:11** With rebukes You punish a person for wrongdoing;<br>You consume like a moth what is precious to him;<br>Certainly all mankind is mere breath! <i>Selah</i>  
**39:12** “Hear my prayer, LORD, and listen to my cry for help;<br>Do not be silent to my tears;<br>For I am a stranger with You,<br>One who lives abroad, like all my fathers.  
**39:13** Turn Your eyes away from me, that I may become cheerful <i>again</i><br>Before I depart and am no more.”  

## 40장

**40:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>I waited <sup>①</sup>patiently for the LORD;<br>And He reached down to me and heard my cry.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>intently</i>  
**40:2** He brought me up out of the pit of <sup>①</sup>destruction, out of the mud;<br>And He set my feet on a rock, making my footsteps firm.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>wasteland;</i> i.e., the underworld  
**40:3** He put a new song in my mouth, a song of praise to our God;<br>Many will see and fear<br>And will trust in the LORD.  
**40:4** How blessed is the man who has made the LORD his trust,<br>And has not turned to the <sup>①</sup>proud, nor to those who become involved in falsehood.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>enemies</i>  
**40:5** Many, LORD my God, are the wonders which You have done,<br>And Your thoughts toward us;<br>There is no one to compare with You.<br>If I would declare and speak of them,<br>They would be too numerous to count.  
**40:6** You have not desired <sup>①</sup>sacrifice and meal offering;<br>You have <sup>②</sup>opened my ears;<br>You have not required burnt offering and sin offering.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., blood sacrifice <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>dug;</i> or possibly <i>pierced</i>  
**40:7** Then I said, “Behold, I have come;<br>It is <sup>①</sup>written of me in the scroll of the book.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>prescribed for</i>  
**40:8** I delight to do Your will, my God;<br>Your Law is within my <sup>①</sup>heart.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>inner being</i>  
**40:9** I have proclaimed good news of righteousness in the great congregation;<br>Behold, I will not restrain my lips,<br>LORD, You know.  
**40:10** I have not hidden Your righteousness within my heart;<br>I have spoken of Your faithfulness and Your salvation;<br>I have not concealed Your mercy and Your truth from the great congregation.  
**40:11** You, LORD, will not withhold Your compassion from me;<br> <sup>①</sup>Your mercy and Your truth will continually watch over me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>May...preserve</i>  
**40:12** For evils beyond number have surrounded me;<br>My guilty deeds have overtaken me, so that I am not able to see;<br>They are more numerous than the hairs of my head,<br>And my heart has <sup>①</sup>failed me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>forsaken</i>  
**40:13** Be pleased, LORD, to rescue me;<br>Hurry, LORD, to help me.  
**40:14** May those be ashamed and humiliated together<br>Who seek my <sup>①</sup>life to destroy it;<br>May those be turned back and dishonored<br>Who delight <sup>②</sup>in my hurt.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>soul</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>to injure me</i>  
**40:15** May those be <sup>①</sup>appalled because of their shame<br>Who say to me, “Aha, aha!”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>desolated</i>  
**40:16** May all who seek You rejoice and be glad in You;<br>May those who love Your salvation continually say,<br>“The LORD be exalted!”  
**40:17** But I am afflicted and needy;<br>May the Lord be mindful of me.<br>You are my help and my savior;<br>Do not delay, my God.  

## 41장

**41:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>Blessed is one who considers the <sup>①</sup>helpless;<br>The LORD will save him on a day of <sup>②</sup>trouble.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>poor</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>evil</i>  
**41:2** The LORD will protect him and keep him alive,<br>And he will be called <sup>①</sup>blessed upon the earth;<br>And do not turn him over to the desire of his enemies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>happy</i>  
**41:3** The LORD will sustain him upon his sickbed;<br>In his illness, You <sup>①</sup>restore him to health.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>turn all his bed</i>  
**41:4** As for me, I said, “LORD, be gracious to me;<br>Heal my soul, for I have sinned against You.”  
**41:5** My enemies speak evil against me,<br>“When will he die, and his name perish?”  
**41:6** And <sup>①</sup>when he comes to see <i>me,</i> he speaks <sup>②</sup>empty words;<br>His heart gathers wickedness to itself;<br>When he goes outside, he tells it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>if he</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>worthlessness</i>  
**41:7** All who hate me whisper together against me;<br>They plot my harm against me, <i>saying,</i>  
**41:8** “A wicked thing is poured out <sup>①</sup>upon him,<br>So that when he lies down, he will not get up again.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>within</i>  
**41:9** Even my <sup>①</sup>close friend in whom I trusted,<br>Who ate my bread,<br>Has lifted up his heel against me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>man of peace</i>  
**41:10** But You, LORD, be gracious to me and raise me up,<br>That I may repay them.  
**41:11** By this I know that You are pleased with me,<br>Because my enemy does not shout in triumph over me.  
**41:12** As for me, You uphold me in my integrity,<br>And You place me in Your presence forever.  
**41:13** Blessed be the LORD, the God of Israel,<br>From everlasting to everlasting.<br>Amen and Amen.  

## 42장

**42:1** [For the music director. A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of the sons of Korah.]<br>As the deer <sup>②</sup>pants for the water brooks,<br>So my soul <sup>③</sup>pants for You, God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>longs for</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>longs for</i>  
**42:2** My soul thirsts for God, for the living God;<br>When shall I come and <sup>①</sup>appear before God?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some mss <i>see the face of God</i>  
**42:3** My tears have been my food day and night,<br>While <i>they</i> say to me all day long, “Where is your God?”  
**42:4** I remember these things and pour out my soul within me.<br>For I used to go over with the multitude <i>and</i> walk them to the house of God,<br>With a voice of joy and thanksgiving, a multitude celebrating a festival.  
**42:5** Why are you <sup>①</sup>in despair, my soul?<br>And <i>why</i> are you restless within me?<br>Wait for God, for I will <sup>②</sup>again praise <sup>③</sup>Him<br><i>For</i> the <sup>④</sup>help of His <sup>⑤</sup>presence, my God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>dissolved away</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>still</i> <sup>③</sup>Some ancient versions <i>Him, the help of my face and my God</i> <sup>④</sup>Or <i>saving acts of</i> <sup>⑤</sup>Or <i>presence. O my God, my soul...</i>  
**42:6** My soul is <sup>①</sup>in despair within me;<br>Therefore I remember You from the land of the Jordan<br>And the <sup>②</sup>peaks of Hermon, from Mount Mizar.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>dissolved away</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Hermons</i>  
**42:7** Deep calls to deep at the sound of Your waterfalls;<br>All Your breakers and Your waves have passed over me.  
**42:8** The LORD will send His goodness in the daytime;<br>And His song will be with me in the night,<br>A prayer to the God of my life.  
**42:9** I will say to God my rock, “Why have You forgotten me?<br>Why do I go about mourning <sup>①</sup>because of the oppression of the enemy?”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>while the enemy oppresses</i>  
**42:10** As a shattering of my bones, my adversaries taunt me,<br>While they say to me all day long, “Where is your God?”  
**42:11** Why are you <sup>①</sup>in despair, my soul?<br>And why are you restless within me?<br>Wait for God, for I will again praise <sup>②</sup>Him<br><i>For</i> the <sup>③</sup>help of His presence, my God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>dissolved away</i> <sup>②</sup>As in some ancient mss, cf. v 5; MT <i>Him, the help of my face and my God</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>saving acts of</i>  

## 43장

**43:1** Vindicate me, God, and plead my case against an ungodly nation;<br> <sup>①</sup>Save me from the deceitful and unjust person!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>May You</i>  
**43:2** For You are the God of my strength; why have You rejected me?<br>Why do I go about mourning <sup>①</sup>because of the oppression of the enemy?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>while the enemy oppresses</i>  
**43:3** Send out Your light and Your truth, they shall lead me;<br>They shall bring me to Your holy hill<br>And to Your dwelling places.  
**43:4** Then I will go to the altar of God,<br>To God <sup>①</sup>my exceeding joy;<br>And I will praise You on the lyre, God, my God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the gladness of my joy</i>  
**43:5** Why are you <sup>①</sup>in despair, my soul?<br>And why are you restless within me?<br>Wait for God, for I will <sup>②</sup>again praise <sup>③</sup>Him<br><i>For</i> the <sup>④</sup>help of His presence, my God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>dissolved away</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>still</i> <sup>③</sup>As in some ancient mss; MT <i>Him, the help of my face and my God</i> <sup>④</sup>Or <i>saving acts of</i>  

## 44장

**44:1** [For the music director. A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of the sons of Korah.]<br>God, we have heard with our ears,<br>Our fathers have told us<br>The work that You did in their days,<br>In the days of old.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm  
**44:2** You with Your own hand drove out the nations;<br>Then You planted them;<br>You afflicted the peoples,<br>Then You let them go free.  
**44:3** For by their own sword they did not possess the land,<br>And their own arm did not save them,<br>But Your right hand and Your arm and the light of Your presence,<br>For You favored them.  
**44:4** You are my King, God;<br>Command <sup>①</sup>victories for Jacob.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>salvation</i>  
**44:5** Through You we will push back our adversaries;<br>Through Your name we will trample down those who rise up against us.  
**44:6** For I will not trust in my bow,<br>Nor will my sword save me.  
**44:7** But You have saved us from our adversaries,<br>And You have put to shame those who hate us.  
**44:8** In God we have boasted all day long,<br>And we will give thanks to Your name forever. <i>Selah</i>  
**44:9** Yet You have rejected <i>us</i> and brought us to dishonor,<br>And do not go out with our armies.  
**44:10** You cause us to turn back from the enemy;<br>And those who hate us have taken spoils for themselves.  
**44:11** You turn us over <sup>①</sup>to be eaten like sheep,<br>And have scattered us among the nations.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for food</i>  
**44:12** You sell Your people <sup>①</sup>cheaply,<br>And have not <sup>②</sup>profited by their sale.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for no wealth</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>set a high price on them</i>  
**44:13** You make us an <i>object of</i> reproach to our neighbors,<br><i>Of</i> scoffing and ridicule to those around us.  
**44:14** You make us a proverb among the nations,<br>A <sup>①</sup>laughingstock among the peoples.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>shaking of the head</i>  
**44:15** All day long my dishonor is before me<br>And <sup>①</sup>I am covered with my humiliation,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the shame of my face has covered me</i>  
**44:16** Because of the voice of one who taunts and reviles,<br>Because of the presence of the enemy and the avenger.  
**44:17** All this has come upon us, but we have not forgotten You,<br>And we have not dealt falsely with Your covenant.  
**44:18** Our heart has not turned back,<br>And our steps have not deviated from Your way,  
**44:19** Yet You have crushed us in a place of jackals<br>And covered us with <sup>①</sup>deep darkness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>the shadow of death</i>  
**44:20** If we had forgotten the name of our God<br>Or extended our <sup>①</sup>hands to a strange god,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>palms</i>  
**44:21** Would God not find this out?<br>For He knows the secrets of the heart.  
**44:22** But for Your sake we are killed all day long;<br>We are regarded as sheep to be slaughtered.  
**44:23** Wake Yourself up, why do You sleep, Lord?<br>Awake, do not reject us forever.  
**44:24** Why do You hide Your face<br><i>And</i> forget our affliction and oppression?  
**44:25** For our souls have sunk down into the dust;<br>Our bodies cling to the earth.  
**44:26** Rise up, be our help,<br>And redeem us because of Your mercy.  

## 45장

**45:1** [For the music director; according to the <sup>①</sup>Shoshannim. A <sup>②</sup>Maskil of the sons of Korah. A Song of Love.]<br>My heart is moved with a good theme;<br>I <sup>③</sup>address my <sup>④</sup>verses to the <sup>⑤</sup>King;<br>My tongue is the pen of a ready writer.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or possibly Lilies <sup>②</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Wisdom Psalm <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>am saying</i> <sup>④</sup>Lit <i>works</i> <sup>⑤</sup>Prob. refers to a Davidic king as a type of Christ  
**45:2** You are the most handsome of the sons of mankind;<br>Grace is poured <sup>①</sup>upon Your lips;<br>Therefore God has blessed You forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>through</i>  
**45:3** Strap Your sword on <i>Your</i> thigh, <sup>①</sup>Mighty One,<br><i>In</i> Your splendor and majesty!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Warrior</i>  
**45:4** And in Your majesty ride on victoriously,<br>For the cause of truth, humility, <i>and</i> righteousness;<br>Let Your right hand teach You <sup>①</sup>awesome things.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>fearful</i>  
**45:5** Your arrows are sharp;<br>The peoples fall under You;<br><i>Your arrows are</i> in the heart of the King’s enemies.  
**45:6** Your throne, God, is forever and ever;<br>The scepter of Your kingdom is a scepter of justice.  
**45:7** You have loved righteousness and hated wickedness;<br>Therefore God, Your God, has anointed You<br>With the oil of joy above Your companions.  
**45:8** All Your garments are <i>fragrant with</i> myrrh, aloes, <i>and</i> cassia;<br>From ivory palaces stringed instruments have made You joyful.  
**45:9** Kings’ daughters are among Your noble women;<br>At Your right hand stands the queen in gold from Ophir.  
**45:10** Listen, daughter, look and incline your ear:<br>Forget your people and your father’s house;  
**45:11** Then the King will crave your beauty.<br>Because He is your Lord, bow down to Him.  
**45:12** The daughter of Tyre <i>will come</i> with a gift;<br>The wealthy among the people will seek your favor.  
**45:13** The King’s daughter is all glorious within;<br>Her clothing is interwoven with gold.  
**45:14** She will be brought to the King in colorful garments;<br>The virgins, her companions who follow her,<br>Will be brought to You.  
**45:15** They will be brought with joy and rejoicing;<br>They will enter into the King’s palace.  
**45:16** In place of your fathers will be your sons;<br>You shall make them princes in all the earth.  
**45:17** I will make Your name known among all generations;<br>Therefore the peoples will praise You forever and ever.  

## 46장

**46:1** [For the music director. <i>A Psalm</i> of the sons of Korah, <sup>①</sup>set to Alamoth. A Song.]<br>God is our refuge and strength,<br>A <sup>②</sup>very ready help in <sup>③</sup>trouble.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly for soprano voices <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>help easily found</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>needs</i>  
**46:2** Therefore we will not fear, though the earth shakes<br>And the mountains slip into the heart of the <sup>①</sup>sea;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seas</i>  
**46:3** Though its waters roar <i>and</i> foam,<br>Though the mountains quake at its swelling pride. <i>Selah</i>  
**46:4** There is a river whose streams make the city of God happy,<br>The holy dwelling places of the Most High.  
**46:5** God is in the midst of her, she will not be moved;<br>God will help her <sup>①</sup>when morning dawns.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>at the turning of the morning</i>  
**46:6** The <sup>①</sup>nations made an uproar, the kingdoms tottered;<br>He <sup>②</sup>raised His voice, the earth quaked.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Gentiles</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>gave forth</i>  
**46:7** The LORD of armies is with us;<br>The God of Jacob is our stronghold. <i>Selah</i>  
**46:8** Come, behold the works of the LORD,<br> <sup>①</sup>Who has inflicted horrific events on the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Which He has inflicted as desolations</i>  
**46:9** He makes wars to cease to the end of the earth;<br>He breaks the bow and cuts the spear in two;<br>He burns the chariots with fire.  
**46:10** “ <sup>①</sup>Stop <i>striving</i> and know that I am God;<br>I will be exalted among the <sup>②</sup>nations, I will be exalted on the earth.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Let go, relax</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>Gentiles</i>  
**46:11** The LORD of armies is with us;<br>The God of Jacob is our stronghold. <i>Selah</i>  

## 47장

**47:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of the sons of Korah.]<br>Clap your hands, all you peoples;<br>Shout to God with a voice of joy.  
**47:2** For the LORD Most High is to be feared,<br>A great King over all the earth.  
**47:3** He subdues peoples under us<br>And nations under our feet.  
**47:4** He chooses our inheritance for us,<br>The pride of Jacob whom He loves. <i>Selah</i>  
**47:5** God has ascended <sup>①</sup>with a shout,<br>The LORD, <sup>②</sup>with the sound of a trumpet.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>amid</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>amid</i>  
**47:6** Sing praises to God, sing praises;<br>Sing praises to our King, sing praises.  
**47:7** For God is the King of all the earth;<br>Sing praises with a psalm of wisdom.  
**47:8** God reigns over the nations,<br>God <sup>①</sup>sits on His holy throne.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>has taken His seat</i>  
**47:9** The <sup>①</sup>princes of the people have assembled <i>as</i> the people of the God of Abraham,<br>For the shields of the earth belong to God;<br>He is highly exalted.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>nobles</i>  

## 48장

**48:1** [A Song; a Psalm of the sons of Korah.]<br>Great is the LORD, and greatly to be praised<br>In the city of our God, His holy mountain.  
**48:2** Beautiful in elevation, the joy of the whole earth,<br>Is Mount Zion <i>in</i> the far north,<br>The city of the great King.  
**48:3** In its palaces,<br>God has made Himself known as a stronghold.  
**48:4** For, behold, the kings arrived,<br>They passed by together.  
**48:5** They saw <i>it,</i> then they were amazed;<br>They were terrified, they fled in a hurry.  
**48:6**  <sup>①</sup>Panic seized them there,<br>Anguish, as <i>that</i> of a woman in childbirth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Trembling</i>  
**48:7** With the east wind<br>You smash the ships of Tarshish.  
**48:8** Just as we have heard, so have we seen<br>In the city of the LORD of armies, in the city of our God;<br>God will establish her forever. <i>Selah</i>  
**48:9** We have thought over Your goodness, God,<br>In the midst of Your temple.  
**48:10** As is Your name, God,<br>So is Your praise to the ends of the earth;<br>Your right hand is full of righteousness.  
**48:11** Mount Zion shall be glad,<br>The daughters of Judah shall rejoice<br>Because of Your judgments.  
**48:12** Walk around Zion and encircle her;<br>Count her towers;  
**48:13** Consider her ramparts;<br>Go through her palaces,<br>So that you may tell <i>of her</i> to the next generation.  
**48:14** For <sup>①</sup>such is God,<br>Our God forever and ever;<br>He will lead us <sup>②</sup>until death.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>this</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>upon;</i> some mss and LXX <i>forever</i>  

## 49장

**49:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of the sons of Korah.]<br>Hear this, all peoples;<br>Listen, all inhabitants of the world,  
**49:2** Both low and high,<br>Rich and poor together.  
**49:3** My mouth will speak wisdom,<br>And the meditation of my heart <i>will be</i> understanding.  
**49:4** I will incline my ear to a proverb;<br>I will <sup>①</sup>express my riddle on the harp.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>open up</i>  
**49:5** Why should I fear in days of adversity,<br>When the injustice of those who betray me surrounds me,  
**49:6** Those who trust in their wealth<br>And boast in the abundance of their riches?  
**49:7** No one can by any means redeem another<br>Or give God a ransom for him—  
**49:8** For the redemption of <sup>①</sup>his soul is priceless,<br>And he should cease <i>imagining</i> forever—<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>their</i>  
**49:9** That he might live on eternally,<br>That he might not <sup>①</sup>undergo decay.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>see corruption;</i> lit <i>see the pit</i>  
**49:10** For he sees <i>that even</i> wise people die;<br>The foolish and the stupid alike perish<br>And leave their wealth to others.  
**49:11** Their <sup>①</sup>inner thought is <i>that</i> their houses are forever<br><i>And</i> their dwelling places to all generations;<br>They have named their lands after their own names.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some ancient versions <i>graves are their houses</i>  
**49:12** But man in <i>his</i> splendor will not endure;<br>He is like the animals that <sup>①</sup>perish.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>are destroyed</i>  
**49:13** This is the way of those who are foolish,<br>And of those after them who approve their words. <i>Selah</i>  
**49:14** Like sheep they sink down to <sup>①</sup>Sheol;<br>Death will be their shepherd;<br>And the upright will rule over them in the morning,<br>And their form shall be for <sup>②</sup>Sheol to consume<br> <sup>③</sup>So that they have no lofty home.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the netherworld <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>Away from his habitation</i>  
**49:15** But God will redeem my soul from the <sup>①</sup>power of <sup>②</sup>Sheol,<br>For He will receive me. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>hand</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**49:16** Do not be afraid when a person becomes rich,<br>When the splendor of his house is increased;  
**49:17** For when he dies, he will take nothing <i>with him;</i><br>His <sup>①</sup>wealth will not descend after him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>glory</i>  
**49:18** Though while he lives he congratulates <sup>①</sup>himself—<br>And though <i>people</i> praise you when you do well for yourself—<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>his soul</i>  
**49:19**  <sup>①</sup>He will go to the generation of his fathers;<br>They will never see the light.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>You;</i> or <i>It</i>  
**49:20** Mankind in <i>its</i> splendor, yet without understanding,<br>Is like the animals <i>that</i> <sup>①</sup>perish.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>are destroyed</i>  

## 50장

**50:1** [A Psalm of <sup>①</sup>Asaph.]<br>The Mighty One, God, the LORD, has spoken<br>And summoned the earth, from <sup>②</sup>the rising of the sun to its setting.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>1 Chr 15:17; 2 Chr 29:30 <sup>②</sup>I.e., the east to the west  
**50:2** Out of Zion, the perfection of beauty,<br>God has shone.  
**50:3** May our God come and not keep silent;<br>Fire devours before Him,<br>And a storm is violently raging around Him.  
**50:4** He summons the heavens above,<br>And the earth, to judge His people:  
**50:5** “Gather My godly ones to Me,<br>Those who have made a covenant with Me by sacrifice.”  
**50:6** And the heavens declare His righteousness,<br>For God Himself is judge. <i>Selah</i>  
**50:7** “Hear, My people, and I will speak;<br>Israel, I will testify <sup>①</sup>against you;<br>I am God, your God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>to</i>  
**50:8** I do not rebuke you for your sacrifices,<br>And your burnt offerings are continually before Me.  
**50:9** I will not take a bull from your house,<br>Nor male goats from your folds.  
**50:10** For every animal of the forest is Mine,<br>The cattle on a thousand hills.  
**50:11** I know every bird of the mountains,<br>And everything that moves in the field is <sup>①</sup>Mine.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>in My mind;</i> lit <i>with Me</i>  
**50:12** If I were hungry I would not tell you,<br>For the world is Mine, and <sup>①</sup>everything it contains.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>its fullness</i>  
**50:13** Shall I eat the flesh of <sup>①</sup>bulls<br>Or drink the blood of male goats?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>strong ones</i>  
**50:14** Offer God a sacrifice of thanksgiving<br>And pay your vows to the Most High;  
**50:15** Call upon Me on the day of trouble;<br>I will rescue you, and you will honor Me.”  
**50:16** But to the wicked God says,<br>“What <i>right</i> do you have to tell of My statutes<br>And to take My covenant in your mouth?  
**50:17** For you yourself hate discipline,<br>And you throw My words behind you.  
**50:18** When you see a thief, you become friends with him,<br>And <sup>①</sup>you associate with adulterers.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>your part is with</i>  
**50:19** You <sup>①</sup>let your mouth loose in evil,<br>And your tongue harnesses deceit.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>send</i>  
**50:20** You sit and speak against your brother;<br>You slander your own mother’s son.  
**50:21** These things you have done <i>and</i> I kept silent;<br>You thought that I was <i>just</i> like you;<br>I will rebuke you and present <i>the case</i> before your eyes.  
**50:22** “Now consider this, you who forget God,<br>Or I will tear <i>you</i> in pieces, and there will be no one to save <i>you.</i>  
**50:23** He who offers a sacrifice of thanksgiving honors Me;<br>And to him who sets <i>his</i> way <i>properly</i><br>I will show the salvation of God.”  

## 51장

**51:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David, when <sup>①</sup>Nathan the prophet came to him, after he had gone in to Bathsheba.]<br>Be gracious to me, God, according to Your faithfulness;<br>According to the greatness of Your compassion, wipe out my wrongdoings.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>2 Sam 12:1  
**51:2** Wash me thoroughly from my guilt<br>And cleanse me from my sin.  
**51:3** For I know my wrongdoings,<br>And my sin is constantly before me.  
**51:4** Against You, You only, I have sinned<br>And done what is evil in Your sight,<br>So that You <sup>①</sup>are justified <sup>②</sup>when You speak<br>And <sup>③</sup>blameless when You judge.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>may be in the right</i> <sup>②</sup>Many mss <i>in Your words</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>pure</i>  
**51:5** Behold, I was brought forth in guilt,<br>And in sin my mother conceived me.  
**51:6** Behold, You desire truth in the <sup>①</sup>innermost being,<br>And in secret You will make wisdom known to me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>inward parts</i>  
**51:7**  <sup>①</sup>Purify me with hyssop, and I will be clean;<br> <sup>②</sup>Cleanse me, and I will be whiter than snow.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>May You purify...that I may be clean</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>May You cleanse</i>  
**51:8**  <sup>①</sup>Let me hear joy and gladness,<br>Let the bones You have broken rejoice.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>May You cause</i>  
**51:9** Hide Your face from my sins<br>And wipe out all my guilty deeds.  
**51:10** Create <sup>①</sup>in me a clean heart, God,<br>And renew a steadfast spirit within me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for</i>  
**51:11** Do not cast me away from Your presence,<br>And do not take Your Holy Spirit from me.  
**51:12** Restore to me the joy of Your salvation,<br>And sustain me with a willing spirit.  
**51:13** <i>Then</i> I will teach wrongdoers Your ways,<br>And sinners will <sup>①</sup>be converted to You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>turn back</i>  
**51:14** Save me from the guilt of bloodshed, God, the God of my salvation;<br><i>Then</i> my tongue will joyfully sing of Your righteousness.  
**51:15** Lord, <sup>①</sup>open my lips,<br>So that my mouth may declare Your praise.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>may You open</i>  
**51:16** For You do not delight in sacrifice, otherwise I would give it;<br>You do not take pleasure in burnt offering.  
**51:17** The sacrifices of God are a broken spirit;<br>A broken and a contrite heart, God, You will not despise.  
**51:18** By Your favor do good to Zion;<br> <sup>①</sup>Build the walls of Jerusalem.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>May You build</i>  
**51:19** Then You will delight in <sup>①</sup>righteous sacrifices,<br>In burnt offering and whole burnt offering;<br>Then <sup>②</sup>bulls will be offered on Your altar.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>sacrifices of righteousness</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>they will offer bulls</i>  

## 52장

**52:1** [For the music director. A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of David, <sup>②</sup>when Doeg the Edomite came and told Saul and said to him, “David has come to the house of Ahimelech.”]<br>Why do you boast in evil, you mighty man?<br>The faithfulness of God <i>endures</i> all day long.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm <sup>②</sup>1 Sam 22:9  
**52:2** Your tongue devises destruction,<br>Like a sharp razor, you worker of deceit.  
**52:3** You love evil more than good,<br>Lies more than speaking what is right. <i>Selah</i>  
**52:4** You love all words that devour,<br>You deceitful tongue.  
**52:5**  <sup>①</sup>But God will break you down forever;<br>He will snatch you up and tear you away from <i>your</i> tent,<br>And uproot you from the land of the living. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Also</i>  
**52:6** The righteous will see and fear,<br>And they will laugh at him, <i>saying,</i>  
**52:7** “Behold, the man who would not make God his refuge,<br>But trusted in the abundance of his riches<br><i>And</i> was strong in <sup>①</sup>his <i>evil</i> desire.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>his destruction</i>  
**52:8** But as for me, I am like a green olive tree in the house of God;<br>I trust in the faithfulness of God forever and ever.  
**52:9** I will praise You forever, because You have done <i>it,</i><br>And I will wait on Your name, for <i>it is</i> good, in the presence of Your godly ones.  

## 53장

**53:1** [For the music director; according to <sup>①</sup>Mahalath. A <sup>②</sup>Maskil of David.]<br>The fool has said in his heart, “There is no God.”<br>They are corrupt, and have committed abominable injustice;<br>There is no one who does good.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., sickness, a sad tone <sup>②</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm  
**53:2** God has looked down from heaven upon the sons of mankind<br>To see if there is anyone who <sup>①</sup>understands,<br>Who seeks after God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>acts wisely</i>  
**53:3** Every one of them has turned aside; together they have become corrupt;<br>There is no one who does good, not even one.  
**53:4** Have the workers of injustice no knowledge,<br>Who eat up My people <i>like</i> they ate bread,<br><i>And</i> have not called upon God?  
**53:5** They were in great <sup>①</sup>fear there, <i>where</i> no <sup>②</sup>fear had been;<br>For God scattered the bones of <sup>③</sup>him who encamped against you;<br>You put <i>them</i> to shame, because God had rejected them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>dread</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>dread</i> <sup>③</sup>Or possibly <i>those</i>  
**53:6** Oh, that the salvation of Israel <i>would come</i> from Zion!<br>When God restores the fortunes of His people,<br>Jacob shall rejoice, Israel shall be glad.  

## 54장

**54:1** [For the music director; on stringed instruments. A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of David, <sup>②</sup>when the Ziphites came and said to Saul, “Is David not keeping himself hidden among us?”]<br>Save me, God, by Your name,<br>And <sup>③</sup>vindicate me by Your power.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm <sup>②</sup>1 Sam 23:19; 26:1 <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>judge</i>  
**54:2** Hear my prayer, God;<br>Listen to the words of my mouth.  
**54:3** For strangers have risen against me<br>And violent men have sought my <sup>①</sup>life;<br>They have not set God before them. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>soul</i>  
**54:4** Behold, God is my helper;<br>The Lord is <sup>①</sup>the sustainer of my soul.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>as those who sustain</i>  
**54:5**  <sup>①</sup>He will pay back the evil to my enemies;<br> <sup>②</sup>Destroy them in Your <sup>③</sup>faithfulness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>LXX; MT <i>The evil will return</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>Put to silence</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>truth</i>  
**54:6**  <sup>①</sup>Willingly I will sacrifice to You;<br>I will praise Your name, LORD, for it is good.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>With a voluntary offering</i>  
**54:7** For <sup>①</sup>He has saved me from all <sup>②</sup>trouble,<br>And my eye has looked <i>with satisfaction</i> upon my enemies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>it;</i> i.e., His name <sup>②</sup>Or <i>distress</i>  

## 55장

**55:1** [For the music director; on stringed instruments. A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of David.]<br>Listen to my prayer, God;<br>And do not hide Yourself from my pleading.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm  
**55:2** Give <i>Your</i> attention to me and answer me;<br>I am restless in my complaint and <sup>①</sup>severely distracted,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Another reading is <i>I must moan</i>  
**55:3** Because of the voice of the enemy,<br>Because of the pressure of the wicked;<br>For they bring down <sup>①</sup>trouble upon me<br>And in anger they hold a grudge against me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>wickedness</i>  
**55:4** My heart is in anguish within me,<br>And the terrors of death have fallen upon me.  
**55:5** Fear and trembling come upon me,<br>And <sup>①</sup>horror has <sup>②</sup>overwhelmed me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>shuddering</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>covered</i>  
**55:6** I said, “Oh, that I had wings like a dove!<br>I would fly away and <sup>①</sup>be at rest.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>settle down</i>  
**55:7** Behold, I would flee far away,<br>I would spend my nights in the wilderness. <i>Selah</i>  
**55:8** I would hurry to my place of refuge<br>From the stormy wind <i>and</i> heavy gale.”  
**55:9** Confuse them, Lord, divide their tongues,<br>For I have seen violence and strife in the city.  
**55:10** Day and night they go around her upon her walls,<br>And evil and harm are in her midst.  
**55:11** Destruction is in her midst;<br>Oppression and deceit do not depart from her <sup>①</sup>streets.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>plaza</i>  
**55:12** For it is not an enemy who taunts me,<br>Then I could endure <i>it;</i><br>Nor is it one who hates me who has exalted himself against me,<br>Then I could hide myself from him.  
**55:13** But it is you, a man <sup>①</sup>my equal,<br>My companion and my confidant;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>according to my valuation</i>  
**55:14** We who had sweet <sup>①</sup>fellowship together,<br>Walked in the house of God among the commotion.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>counsel;</i> or <i>intimacy</i>  
**55:15** <i>May</i> <sup>①</sup>death <i>come</i> deceitfully upon them;<br>May they go down alive to <sup>②</sup>Sheol,<br>For evil is in their dwelling, in their midst.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Another reading is <i>devastation be upon them</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**55:16** As for me, I shall call upon God,<br>And the LORD will save me.  
**55:17** Evening and morning and at noon, I will complain and moan,<br>And He will hear my voice.  
**55:18** He will redeem my soul in peace <sup>①</sup>from the battle <i>which is</i> against me,<br>For they are many <i>who are aggressive</i> toward me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>so that none may approach me</i>  
**55:19** God will hear and humiliate them—<br>Even the one who <sup>①</sup>sits enthroned from ancient times—<i>Selah</i> With whom there <sup>②</sup>is no change,<br>And who do not fear God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>remains from</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>are no changes</i>  
**55:20** He has put forth his hands against those who were at peace with him;<br>He has <sup>①</sup>violated his covenant.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>profaned</i>  
**55:21** His <sup>①</sup>speech was smoother than butter,<br>But his heart was war;<br>His words were softer than oil,<br>Yet they were drawn swords.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>mouth</i>  
**55:22** Cast your burden upon the LORD and He will sustain you;<br>He will never allow the righteous to <sup>①</sup>be shaken.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>totter</i>  
**55:23** But You, God, will bring them down to the pit of destruction;<br>Men of bloodshed and deceit will not live out half their days.<br>But I will trust in You.  

## 56장

**56:1** [For the music director; according to <sup>①</sup>Jonath elem rehokim. A <sup>②</sup>Mikhtam of David, <sup>③</sup>when the Philistines seized him in Gath.]<br>Be gracious to me, God, for a man has <sup>④</sup>trampled upon me;<br> <sup>⑤</sup>Fighting all day long he oppresses me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or The silent dove of those who are far off; or The dove of the distant terebinths <sup>②</sup>Possibly Epigrammatic Poem; or Atonement Psalm <sup>③</sup>1 Sam 21:10, 11 <sup>④</sup>Or <i>snapped at</i> <sup>⑤</sup>Or <i>A fighting man</i>  
**56:2** My enemies have trampled upon me all day long,<br>For <sup>①</sup>they are many who fight proudly against me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>many are fighting</i>  
**56:3**  <sup>①</sup>When I am afraid,<br> <sup>②</sup>I will put my trust in You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>The day</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>I am one who puts</i>  
**56:4** In God, whose word I praise,<br>In God I have put my trust;<br>I shall not be afraid.<br>What can <i>mere</i> <sup>①</sup>mortals do to me?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>flesh</i>  
**56:5** All day long they distort my words;<br>All their <sup>①</sup>thoughts are against me for evil.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>purposes</i>  
**56:6** They <sup>①</sup>attack, they lurk,<br>They watch my <sup>②</sup>steps,<br>As they have waited <i>to take</i> my <sup>③</sup>life.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>stir up strife</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>heels</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>soul</i>  
**56:7** Because of <i>their</i> wickedness, <i>will there be</i> an escape for them?<br>In anger make the peoples fall down, God!  
**56:8** You have taken account of my <sup>①</sup>miseries;<br>Put my tears in Your bottle.<br><i>Are they</i> not in Your book?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>wanderings</i>  
**56:9** Then my enemies will turn back on the day when I call;<br>This I know, <sup>①</sup>that God is for me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>because</i>  
**56:10** In God, <i>whose</i> word I praise,<br>In the LORD, <i>whose</i> word I praise,  
**56:11** In God I have put my <sup>①</sup>trust, I shall not be afraid.<br>What can mankind do to me?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>trust without fear</i>  
**56:12** Your vows are <i>binding</i> upon me, God;<br>I will render thanksgiving offerings to You.  
**56:13** For You have saved my soul from death,<br> <sup>①</sup>Indeed my feet from stumbling,<br>So that I may walk before God<br>In the light of the <sup>②</sup>living.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Have You</i> not <i>delivered</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>life</i>  

## 57장

**57:1** [For the music director; set to <sup>①</sup>Al-tashheth. A <sup>②</sup>Mikhtam of David, <sup>③</sup>when he fled from Saul in the cave.]<br>Be gracious to me, God, be gracious to me,<br>For my soul takes refuge in You;<br>And in the shadow of Your wings I will take refuge<br>Until destruction passes by.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit Do Not Destroy <sup>②</sup>Possibly, Epigrammatic Poem; or Atonement Psalm <sup>③</sup>1 Sam 22:1; 24:3  
**57:2** I will cry to God Most High,<br>To God who accomplishes <i>all things</i> for me.  
**57:3** He will send from heaven and save me;<br>He rebukes the one who <sup>①</sup>tramples upon me. <i>Selah</i> God will send His <sup>②</sup>favor and His truth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>snaps at</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**57:4** My soul is among lions;<br>I must lie <i>among</i> those who devour,<br><i>Among</i> sons of mankind whose teeth are spears and arrows,<br>And their tongue is a sharp sword.  
**57:5** Be exalted above the heavens, God;<br><i>May</i> Your glory <i>be</i> above all the earth.  
**57:6** They have prepared a net for my steps;<br>My soul is bowed down;<br>They dug a pit before me;<br>They <i>themselves</i> have fallen into the midst of it. <i>Selah</i>  
**57:7** My heart is steadfast, God, my heart is steadfast;<br>I will sing, yes, I will sing praises!  
**57:8** Awake, my glory!<br>Awake, harp and lyre!<br>I will awaken the dawn.  
**57:9** I will praise You, Lord, among the peoples;<br>I will sing praises to You among the <sup>①</sup>nations.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>peoples</i>  
**57:10** For Your goodness is great to the heavens<br>And Your <sup>①</sup>truth to the clouds.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**57:11** Be exalted above the heavens, God;<br><i>May</i> Your glory <i>be</i> above all the earth.  

## 58장

**58:1** [For the music director; <i>set to</i> <sup>①</sup>Al-tashheth. A <sup>②</sup>Mikhtam of David.]<br>Do you indeed <sup>③</sup>speak righteousness, you <sup>④</sup>gods?<br>Do you judge <sup>⑤</sup>fairly, you sons of mankind?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit Do Not Destroy <sup>②</sup>Possibly Epigrammatic Poem or Atonement Psalm <sup>③</sup>Another reading is <i>speak righteousness in silence</i> <sup>④</sup>Or <i>mighty ones;</i> or <i>judges</i> <sup>⑤</sup>Or <i>mankind fairly?</i>  
**58:2** No, in heart you practice injustice;<br>On earth you clear a way for the violence of your hands.  
**58:3** The wicked have turned away from the womb;<br>These who speak lies go astray from <sup>①</sup>birth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the womb</i>  
**58:4** They have venom like the venom of a serpent;<br>Like a deaf cobra that stops up its ear,  
**58:5** So that it does not hear the voice of <sup>①</sup>charmers,<br><i>Or</i> a skillful caster of spells.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>whisperers</i>  
**58:6** God, shatter their teeth in their mouth;<br>Break out the fangs of the young lions, LORD.  
**58:7** May they flow away like water that runs off;<br><i>When</i> he <sup>①</sup>aims his arrows, <i>may they be</i> as <sup>②</sup>headless shafts.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>bends</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>though they were cut off</i>  
**58:8** <i>May they be</i> like a snail which goes along in slime,<br><i>Like</i> the miscarriage of a woman that never sees the sun.  
**58:9** Before your pots can feel <i>the fire of</i> thorns<br>He will sweep them away with a whirlwind, the <sup>①</sup>green and the burning alike.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>living</i>  
**58:10** The righteous will rejoice when he sees vengeance;<br>He will wash his feet in the blood of the wicked.  
**58:11** And people will say, “There certainly is <sup>①</sup>a reward for the righteous;<br>There certainly is a God who judges on the earth!”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>fruit</i>  

## 59장

**59:1** [For the music director; <i>set to</i> <sup>①</sup>Al-tashheth. A <sup>②</sup>Mikhtam of David, <sup>③</sup>when Saul sent <i>men</i> and they watched the house in order to kill him.]<br>Rescue me from my enemies, my God;<br> <sup>④</sup>Set me <i>securely</i> on high away from those who rise up against me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit Do Not Destroy <sup>②</sup>Possibly Epigrammatic Poem or Atonement Psalm <sup>③</sup>1 Sam 19:11 <sup>④</sup>Or <i>May You put me in an inaccessibly high place</i>  
**59:2** Rescue me from those who practice injustice,<br>And save me from men of bloodshed.  
**59:3** For behold, they have <sup>①</sup>set an ambush for my <sup>②</sup>life;<br> <sup>③</sup>Fierce men attack me,<br>Not for my wrongdoing nor for my sin, LORD,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>lain in wait</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>soul</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>Strong</i>  
**59:4**  <sup>①</sup>For no guilt <i>of mine,</i> they run and take their stand against me.<br>Stir Yourself to <sup>②</sup>help me, and see!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Without guilt</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>meet</i>  
**59:5** You, LORD God of armies, the God of Israel,<br>Awake to <sup>①</sup>punish all the nations;<br>Do not be gracious to any <i>who</i> deal treacherously in wrongdoing. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>visit</i>  
**59:6** They return at evening, they howl like a dog,<br>And prowl around the city.  
**59:7** Behold, they gush forth with their mouths;<br>Swords are in their lips,<br>For, <i>they say,</i> “Who hears?”  
**59:8** But You, LORD, laugh at them;<br>You scoff at all the nations.  
**59:9** <i>Because of</i> <sup>①</sup>his strength I will watch for You,<br>For God is my refuge.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>LXX, some mss and some ancient versions <i>my strength</i> (cf. v 17)  
**59:10**  <sup>①</sup>My God in His faithfulness will meet me;<br>God will let me look <i>triumphantly</i> upon my enemies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Many mss and some ancient versions <i>The God of my faithfulness</i>  
**59:11** Do not kill them, or my people will forget;<br> <sup>①</sup>Scatter them by Your power and bring them down,<br>Lord, our shield.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Make them wander</i>  
**59:12**  <sup>①</sup><i>On account of</i> the sin of their mouths <i>and</i> the words of their lips,<br>May they even be caught in their pride,<br>And on account of curses and lies which they tell.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>The sin of their mouth is the word of their lips,</i>  
**59:13**  <sup>①</sup>Destroy <i>them</i> in wrath, <sup>②</sup>destroy <i>them</i> so that they will no longer exist;<br>So that <i>people</i> may know that God <sup>③</sup>rules in Jacob,<br>To the ends of the earth. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Bring to an end</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Bring to an end</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>is Ruler</i>  
**59:14** They return at evening, they howl like a dog,<br>And prowl around the city.  
**59:15** They wander about <sup>①</sup>for food<br>And <sup>②</sup>murmur if they are not satisfied.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to eat</i> <sup>②</sup>Another reading is <i>linger all night</i>  
**59:16** But as for me, I will sing of Your strength;<br>Yes, I will joyfully sing of Your faithfulness in the morning,<br>For You have been my refuge<br>And a place of refuge on the day of my distress.  
**59:17** My strength, I will sing praises to You;<br>For God is my refuge, the <sup>①</sup>God who shows me favor.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>God of my favor</i>  

## 60장

**60:1** [For the music director; according to <sup>①</sup>Shushan Eduth. A <sup>②</sup>Mikhtam of David, to teach; <sup>③</sup>when he fought with Aram-naharaim and Aram-zobah, and Joab returned, and killed twelve thousand of Edom in the Valley of Salt.]<br>God, You have rejected us. You have <sup>④</sup>broken us;<br>You have been angry; restore us!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit The lily of testimony <sup>②</sup>Possibly, Epigrammatic Poem or Atonement Psalm <sup>③</sup>2 Sam 8:3, 13; 1 Chr 18:3, 12 <sup>④</sup>Or <i>broken out upon us</i>  
**60:2** You have made the <sup>①</sup>land quake, You have split it open;<br>Heal its cracks, for it sways.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>earth</i>  
**60:3** You have <sup>①</sup>made Your people experience hardship;<br>You have given us <sup>②</sup>wine to drink that makes us stagger.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>caused Your people to see</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>wine of staggering</i>  
**60:4** You have given a banner to those who fear You,<br>That it may be displayed because of the truth. <i>Selah</i>  
**60:5** That Your beloved may be rescued,<br>Save <i>us</i> with Your right hand, and answer <sup>①</sup>us!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some authorities <i>me</i>  
**60:6** God has spoken in His <sup>①</sup>holiness:<br>“I will triumph, I will divide up Shechem, and measure out the Valley of Succoth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>sanctuary</i>  
**60:7** Gilead is Mine, and Manasseh is Mine;<br>Ephraim also is the <sup>①</sup>helmet of My head;<br>Judah is My scepter.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>protection</i>  
**60:8** Moab is My washbowl;<br>I will throw My sandal over Edom;<br>Shout loud, Philistia, because of Me!”  
**60:9** Who will bring me into the besieged city?<br>Who <sup>①</sup>will lead me to Edom?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>has led</i>  
**60:10** Have You Yourself not rejected us, God?<br>And will You not go out with our armies, God?  
**60:11** Oh give us help against the enemy,<br>For rescue <sup>①</sup>by man is worthless.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of</i>  
**60:12**  <sup>①</sup>Through God we will do valiantly,<br>And it is He who will trample down our enemies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>In;</i> or <i>With</i>  

## 61장

**61:1** [For the music director; on a stringed instrument. <i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>Hear my cry, God;<br>Give <i>Your</i> attention to my prayer.  
**61:2** From the end of the earth I call to You when my heart is faint;<br>Lead me to the rock that is higher than I.  
**61:3** For You have been a refuge for me,<br>A tower of strength against the enemy.  
**61:4** Let me <sup>①</sup>dwell in Your tent forever;<br>Let me take refuge in the shelter of Your wings. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>sojourn</i>  
**61:5** For You have heard my vows, God;<br>You have given <i>me</i> the inheritance of those who fear Your name.  
**61:6** You will <sup>①</sup>prolong the king’s <sup>②</sup>life;<br>His years will be like generations.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>add days to</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>days</i>  
**61:7** He will <sup>①</sup>sit <i>enthroned</i> before God forever;<br>Appoint faithfulness and truth that they may watch over him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>remain before</i>  
**61:8** So I will sing praise to Your name forever,<br>That I may pay my vows day by day.  

## 62장

**62:1** [For the music director; <sup>①</sup>according to Jeduthun. A Psalm of David.]<br>My soul <i>waits in</i> silence for God alone;<br>From Him <i>comes</i> my salvation.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Cf. 1 Chr 16:41; 25:1; Ps 39 and 77 titles  
**62:2** He alone is my rock and my salvation,<br>My stronghold; I will not be greatly shaken.  
**62:3** How long will you attack a man,<br>That you may murder <i>him,</i> all of you,<br>Like a leaning wall, like a tottering fence?  
**62:4** They have planned only to thrust him down from his high position;<br>They delight in falsehood;<br>They bless with <sup>①</sup>their mouth,<br>But inwardly they curse. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>his</i>  
**62:5** My soul, wait in silence for God alone,<br>For my hope is from Him.  
**62:6** He alone is my rock and my salvation,<br>My refuge; I will not be shaken.  
**62:7** My salvation and my glory <i>rest</i> on God;<br>The rock of my strength, my refuge is in God.  
**62:8** Trust in Him at all times, you people;<br>Pour out your hearts before Him;<br>God is a refuge for us. <i>Selah</i>  
**62:9** People of low standing are only breath, and people of rank are a lie;<br>In the balances they go up.<br>Together they are <i>lighter</i> than breath.  
**62:10** Do not trust in oppression,<br>And do not vainly rely on robbery;<br>If wealth increases, do not set <i>your</i> heart <i>on it.</i>  
**62:11** God has spoken <sup>①</sup>once;<br> <sup>②</sup>Twice I have heard this:<br>That power belongs to God;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>one thing</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>These two things I have heard</i>  
**62:12** And faithfulness is Yours, Lord,<br>For You reward a person according to his work.  

## 63장

**63:1** [A Psalm of David, <sup>①</sup>when he was in the wilderness of Judah.]<br>God, You are my God; I shall be watching for You;<br>My soul thirsts for You, my flesh yearns for You,<br>In a dry and exhausted land where there is no water.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>1 Sam 22:5; 23:14  
**63:2** So have I seen You in the sanctuary,<br>To see Your power and glory.  
**63:3** Because Your <sup>①</sup>favor is better than life,<br>My lips will praise You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**63:4** So I will bless You as long as I live;<br>I will lift up my hands in Your name.  
**63:5** My soul is satisfied as with fat and fatness,<br>And my mouth offers praises with joyful lips.  
**63:6** When I remember You on my bed,<br>I meditate on You in the night watches,  
**63:7** For You have been my help,<br>And in the shadow of Your wings I sing for joy.  
**63:8** My soul clings <sup>①</sup>to You;<br>Your right hand takes hold of me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>after</i>  
**63:9** But those who seek my <sup>①</sup>life to destroy it,<br>Will go into the <sup>②</sup>depths of the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>soul</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>lowest places</i>  
**63:10**  <sup>①</sup>They will be <sup>②</sup>turned over to the power of the sword;<br>They will be a <sup>③</sup>prey for foxes.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>They will pour him out</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>poured out by</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>portion</i>  
**63:11** But the king will rejoice in God;<br>Everyone who swears by Him will boast,<br>For the mouths of those who speak lies will be stopped.  

## 64장

**64:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>Hear my voice, God, in my <sup>①</sup>complaint;<br>Protect my life from dread of the enemy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>concern</i>  
**64:2** Hide me from the secret discussion of evildoers,<br>From the restlessness of the workers of injustice,  
**64:3** Who have sharpened their tongues like a sword.<br>They aimed bitter speech <i>as</i> their arrows,  
**64:4** To shoot <sup>①</sup>from concealment at the innocent;<br>Suddenly they shoot at him, and do not fear.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in</i>  
**64:5** They make firm for themselves an evil <sup>①</sup>purpose;<br>They <sup>②</sup>talk of setting snares secretly;<br>They say, “Who can see them?”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>matter</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>tell of</i>  
**64:6** They <sup>①</sup>devise injustices, <i>saying,</i><br>“We are <sup>②</sup>ready <i>with</i> a well-conceived plot”;<br>For the <sup>③</sup>inward thought and the heart of a person are <sup>④</sup>deep.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>search out</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>complete</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>inward part</i> <sup>④</sup>Or <i>unsearchable</i>  
**64:7** But God <sup>①</sup>will shoot an arrow at them;<br>Suddenly <sup>②</sup>they will be wounded.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>shot</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>they were wounded;</i> lit <i>their wounds occurred</i>  
**64:8** So <sup>①</sup>they <sup>②</sup>will make him stumble;<br>Their own tongue is against them;<br>All who see <i>them</i> will shake their heads.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>they make their tongue a stumbling for themselves</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>made</i>  
**64:9** Then all people <sup>①</sup>will fear,<br>And they <sup>②</sup>will declare the work of God,<br>And <sup>③</sup>will consider <sup>④</sup>what He has done.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>feared</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>declared</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>considered</i> <sup>④</sup>Lit <i>His work</i>  
**64:10** The righteous person will be glad in the LORD and take refuge in Him;<br>And all the upright in heart will boast.  

## 65장

**65:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David. A Song.]<br>There will be silence <sup>①</sup>before You, <i>and</i> praise in Zion, God,<br>And the vow will be fulfilled for You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to</i>  
**65:2** You who hear prayer,<br>To You all <sup>①</sup>mankind comes.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>flesh comes</i>  
**65:3**  <sup>①</sup>Wrongdoings prevail against me;<br>As for our offenses, You <sup>②</sup>forgive them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Confessions of wrongdoings</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>cover, atone for</i>  
**65:4** Blessed <i>is the one</i> You choose and allow to approach <i>You;</i><br>He will dwell in Your courtyards.<br>We will be satisfied with the goodness of Your house,<br>Your holy temple.  
**65:5** By awesome <i>deeds</i> You answer us in righteousness, God of our salvation,<br><i>You who are</i> the trust of all the ends of the earth and the farthest <sup>①</sup>sea;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>seas</i>  
**65:6** Who establishes the mountains by His strength,<br>Who is encircled with might;  
**65:7** Who stills the roaring of the seas,<br>The roaring of their waves,<br>And the turmoil of the nations.  
**65:8** They who dwell at the ends <i>of the earth</i> stand in awe of Your signs;<br>You make the <sup>①</sup>sunrise and the sunset shout for joy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>outgoings of the morning and evening</i>  
**65:9** You visit the earth and cause it to overflow;<br>You greatly enrich it;<br>The <sup>①</sup>stream of God is full of water;<br>You prepare their grain, for so You prepare <sup>②</sup>the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>channel</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>it</i>  
**65:10** You water its furrows abundantly,<br>You <sup>①</sup>settle its ridges,<br>You soften it with showers,<br>You bless its growth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>smooth</i>  
**65:11** You have crowned the year <sup>①</sup>with Your goodness,<br>And Your <sup>②</sup>paths drip <i>with</i> fatness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., wagon tracks  
**65:12** The pastures of the wilderness drip,<br>And the hills encircle themselves with rejoicing.  
**65:13** The meadows are clothed with flocks<br>And the valleys are covered with grain;<br>They shout for joy, yes, they sing.  

## 66장

**66:1** [For the music director. A Song. A Psalm.]<br>Shout joyfully to God, all the earth;  
**66:2** Sing the glory of His name;<br>Make His praise glorious.  
**66:3** Say to God, “How awesome are Your works!<br>Because of the greatness of Your power Your enemies will pretend to obey You.  
**66:4** All the earth will worship You,<br>And will sing praises to You;<br>They will sing praises to Your name.” <i>Selah</i>  
**66:5** Come and see the works of God,<br><i>Who is</i> awesome in <i>His</i> deeds toward the sons of mankind.  
**66:6** He turned the sea into dry land;<br>They passed through the river on foot;<br>Let’s rejoice there, in Him!  
**66:7** He rules by His might forever;<br>His eyes keep watch on the nations;<br>The rebellious shall not exalt themselves! <i>Selah</i>  
**66:8** Bless our God, you peoples,<br>And <sup>①</sup>sound His praise abroad,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>cause to hear the sound of His praise</i>  
**66:9** Who <sup>①</sup>keeps us in life,<br>And does not allow our feet to <sup>②</sup>slip.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>puts our soul in life</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>dodder, stumble</i>  
**66:10** For You have put us to the test, God;<br>You have refined us as silver is refined.  
**66:11** You brought us into the net;<br>You laid an oppressive burden upon <sup>①</sup>us.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>our loins</i>  
**66:12** You made men ride over our heads;<br>We went through fire and through water.<br>Yet You brought us out into <i>a place of</i> abundance.  
**66:13** I shall come into Your house with burnt offerings;<br>I shall pay You my vows,  
**66:14** Which my lips uttered<br>And my mouth spoke when I was in distress.  
**66:15** I shall offer to You burnt offerings of fat animals,<br>With the smoke of rams;<br>I shall make <i>an offering of</i> <sup>①</sup>bulls with male goats. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>cattle</i>  
**66:16** Come <i>and</i> hear, all who <sup>①</sup>fear God,<br>And I will tell of what He has done for my soul.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**66:17** I cried to Him with my mouth,<br>And <sup>①</sup>He was exalted with my tongue.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>praise was under my tongue</i>  
**66:18** If I <sup>①</sup>regard wickedness in my heart,<br>The Lord will not hear;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>had regarded</i>  
**66:19** But God has heard;<br>He has given attention to the sound of my prayer.  
**66:20** Blessed be God,<br>Who has not turned away my prayer<br>Nor His favor from me.  

## 67장

**67:1** [For the music director; with stringed instruments. A Psalm. A Song.]<br>God be gracious to us and bless us,<br><i>And</i> cause His face to shine <sup>①</sup>upon us—<i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>with</i>  
**67:2** That Your way may be known on the earth,<br>Your salvation among all nations.  
**67:3** May the peoples praise You, God;<br>May all the peoples praise You.  
**67:4** May the nations be glad and sing for joy;<br>For You will judge the peoples with fairness<br>And guide the nations on the earth. <i>Selah</i>  
**67:5** May the peoples praise You, God;<br>May all the peoples praise You.  
**67:6** The earth has yielded its produce;<br>God, our God, blesses us.  
**67:7** God blesses us,<br> <sup>①</sup>So that all the ends of the earth may fear Him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>And let all...earth fear Him</i>  

## 68장

**68:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David. A Song.]<br> <sup>①</sup>May God arise, <sup>②</sup>may His enemies be scattered,<br>And <sup>③</sup>may those who hate Him flee from His presence.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>God shall</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>His enemies shall</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>those who hate Him shall</i>  
**68:2** As smoke is driven away, <i>so</i> drive <i>them</i> away;<br>As wax melts before a fire,<br><i>So</i> the wicked will perish before God.  
**68:3** But the righteous will be joyful; they will rejoice before God;<br>Yes, they will rejoice with gladness.  
**68:4** Sing to God, sing praises to His name;<br> <sup>①</sup>Exalt Him who rides through the deserts,<br>Whose name is <sup>②</sup>the LORD, and be jubilant before Him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Prepare the way for Him</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**68:5** A father of the fatherless and a judge <sup>①</sup>for the widows,<br>Is God in His holy dwelling.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of</i>  
**68:6** God <sup>①</sup>makes a home for the lonely;<br>He leads out the prisoners into prosperity,<br>Only the rebellious live in parched lands.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>makes the solitary to dwell in a house</i>  
**68:7** God, when You went forth before Your people,<br>When You marched through the desert, <i>Selah</i>  
**68:8** The earth quaked;<br>The heavens also dropped <i>rain</i> at the presence of God;<br> <sup>①</sup>Sinai itself <i>quaked</i> at the presence of God, the God of Israel.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>This is Sinai</i> which  
**68:9** You made plentiful rain fall, God;<br>You confirmed Your inheritance when it was <sup>①</sup>parched.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>weary</i>  
**68:10** Your creatures settled in it;<br>In Your kindness You provided for the poor, God.  
**68:11** The Lord gives the <sup>①</sup>command;<br>The women who proclaim good news are a great army:<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>word</i>  
**68:12** “Kings of armies flee, they flee,<br>And she who remains at home will divide the spoils!”  
**68:13**  <sup>①</sup>When you lie down among the sheepfolds,<br><i>You are like</i> the wings of a dove covered with silver,<br>And its pinions with <sup>②</sup>glistening gold.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>If</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., gold alloyed with silver  
**68:14** When the Almighty scattered the kings <sup>①</sup>there,<br>It was snowing in Zalmon.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in it</i>  
**68:15** The mountain of Bashan is a <sup>①</sup>mountain of God;<br>The mountain of Bashan is a mountain of many peaks.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>mighty mountain</i>  
**68:16** Why do you look with envy, you mountains of many peaks,<br>At the mountain God has desired as His dwelling?<br>Indeed, the LORD will dwell <i>there</i> forever.  
**68:17** The chariots of God are <sup>①</sup>myriads, thousands upon thousands;<br> <sup>②</sup>The Lord is among them <i>as at</i> Sinai, in holiness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>twice 10,000</i> <sup>②</sup>Another reading is <i>The Lord came from Sinai into the sanctuary</i>  
**68:18** You have ascended on high, You have led captive <i>Your</i> captives;<br>You have received gifts among people,<br>Even <i>among</i> the rebellious as well, that <sup>①</sup>the LORD God may dwell <i>there.</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**68:19** Blessed be the Lord, who daily bears our burden,<br>The God <i>who</i> is our salvation. <i>Selah</i>  
**68:20** God is to us a God of salvation;<br>And to <sup>①</sup>GOD the Lord belong ways of escape <sup>②</sup>from death.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YHWH,</i> usually rendered <i>LORD</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., in view of; lit <i>for</i>  
**68:21** God certainly will shatter the heads of His enemies,<br>The hairy <sup>①</sup>head of one who goes about in his guilt.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>skull</i>  
**68:22** The Lord <sup>①</sup>said, “I will bring <i>them</i> back from Bashan.<br>I will bring <i>them</i> back from the depths of the sea,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>says</i>  
**68:23** So that <sup>①</sup>your foot may shatter <i>them</i> in blood,<br><i>And</i> the tongue of your dogs <i>may have</i> its portion from <i>your</i> enemies.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some ancient versions render, you may <i>bathe your foot in blood</i>  
**68:24** They have seen Your procession, God,<br>The procession of my God, my King, <sup>①</sup>into the sanctuary.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>in holiness</i>  
**68:25** The singers went on, the musicians after <i>them,</i><br> <sup>①</sup>In the midst of the young women beating tambourines.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>The maidens in the midst</i>  
**68:26** Bless God in the congregations,<br><i>Even</i> the LORD, <i>you who are</i> of the fountain of Israel.  
**68:27** Benjamin, the <sup>①</sup>youngest, is there, <sup>②</sup>ruling them,<br>The leaders of Judah <i>in</i> their company,<br>The leaders of Zebulun, the leaders of Naphtali.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>smallest</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>their ruler</i>  
**68:28**  <sup>①</sup>Your God has commanded your strength;<br>Show Yourself strong, God, You who acted <sup>②</sup>in our behalf.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some mss <i>Command, God</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>for us</i>  
**68:29**  <sup>①</sup>Because of Your temple at Jerusalem<br>Kings will bring gifts to You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>From Your temple</i>  
**68:30** Rebuke the animals <sup>①</sup>in the reeds,<br>The herd of bulls with the calves of the peoples,<br>Trampling the pieces of silver;<br>He has scattered the peoples who delight in war.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of</i>  
**68:31**  <sup>①</sup>Messengers will come from Egypt;<br> <sup>②</sup>Cush will quickly stretch out her hands to God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>As in LXX; MT uncertain, possibly, Articles of <i>bronze;</i> or Articles of <i>red cloth</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>Ethiopia</i>  
**68:32** Sing to God, you kingdoms of the earth,<br>Sing praises to the Lord, <i>Selah</i>  
**68:33** To Him who rides upon the <sup>①</sup>highest heavens, which are from ancient times;<br>Behold, He <sup>②</sup>speaks with His voice, a mighty voice.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>heaven of heavens of old</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>gives forth</i>  
**68:34** Ascribe strength to God;<br>His majesty is over Israel,<br>And His strength is in the <sup>①</sup>skies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>clouds</i>  
**68:35**  <sup>①</sup>God, <i>You are</i> awesome from Your <sup>②</sup>sanctuary.<br>The God of Israel Himself gives strength and power to the people.<br>Blessed be God!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Awesome is God from your sanctuary</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>holy places</i>  

## 69장

**69:1** [For the music director; according to <sup>①</sup>Shoshannim. <i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>Save me, God,<br>For the waters have <sup>②</sup>threatened my life.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or possibly Lilies <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>come up to</i> my <i>soul</i>  
**69:2** I have sunk in deep mud, and there is no foothold;<br>I have come into deep waters, and a <sup>①</sup>flood overflows me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>flowing stream</i>  
**69:3** I am weary with my crying; my throat is parched;<br>My eyes fail while I wait for my God.  
**69:4** Those who hate me without a cause are more than the hairs of my head;<br>Those who would <sup>①</sup>destroy me are powerful, those who oppose me with lies;<br>What I did not steal, I then have to restore.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>silence</i>  
**69:5** God, You know my foolishness,<br>And my guilt is not hidden from You.  
**69:6** May those who wait for You not be ashamed because of me, Lord <sup>①</sup>GOD of armies;<br>May those who seek You not be dishonored because of me, God of Israel,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YHWH,</i> usually rendered <i>LORD</i>  
**69:7** Because for Your sake I have endured disgrace;<br>Dishonor has covered my face.  
**69:8** I have become estranged <sup>①</sup>from my brothers,<br>And a stranger to my mother’s sons.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to</i>  
**69:9** For zeal for Your house has consumed me,<br>And the taunts of those who taunt You have fallen on me.  
**69:10** When I wept in my soul with fasting,<br>It became my disgrace.  
**69:11** When I made sackcloth my clothing,<br>I became a proverb to them.  
**69:12** Those who sit in the gate talk about me,<br>And songs of mockery by those habitually drunk <i>are about me.</i>  
**69:13** But as for me, my prayer is to You, LORD, at an acceptable time;<br>God, in the greatness of Your mercy,<br>Answer me with <sup>①</sup>Your saving truth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>the faithfulness of Your salvation</i>  
**69:14** Rescue me from the mud and do not let me sink;<br>May I be rescued from those who hate me, and from the depths of water.  
**69:15** May the <sup>①</sup>flood of water not overflow me<br>Nor the deep swallow me up,<br>Nor the pit close its mouth on me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>stream</i>  
**69:16** Answer me, LORD, for Your mercy is good;<br>According to the greatness of Your compassion, turn to me,  
**69:17** And do not hide Your face from Your servant,<br>For I am in distress; answer me quickly.  
**69:18** Come near to my soul <i>and</i> redeem it;<br>Ransom me because of my enemies!  
**69:19** You know my disgrace, my shame, and my dishonor;<br>All my enemies are <sup>①</sup>known to You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>before You</i>  
**69:20** Disgrace has broken my heart, and I am so sick.<br>And I waited for sympathy, but there was none;<br>And for comforters, but I found none.  
**69:21** They also gave me <sup>①</sup>a bitter herb in my food,<br>And for my thirst they gave me vinegar to drink.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>poison</i>  
**69:22** May their table before them become a snare;<br>And <sup>①</sup>when they are at peace, <i>may it become</i> a trap.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for those who are secure</i>  
**69:23** May their eyes grow dim so that they cannot see,<br>And make their hips shake continually.  
**69:24** Pour out Your indignation on them,<br>And may Your burning anger overtake them.  
**69:25** May their <sup>①</sup>camp be desolated;<br>May there be none living in their tents.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>encampment</i>  
**69:26** For they have persecuted him whom You Yourself struck,<br>And they tell of the pain of those whom You have <sup>①</sup>wounded.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>pierced</i>  
**69:27** Add guilt to their guilt,<br>And may they not come into Your righteousness.  
**69:28** May they be wiped out of the book of life,<br>And may they not be <sup>①</sup>recorded with the righteous.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>written</i>  
**69:29** But I am afflicted and in pain;<br> <sup>①</sup>May Your salvation, God, set me <i>safely</i> on high.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Your salvation, O God, will set...</i>  
**69:30** I will praise the name of God with song,<br>And exalt Him with thanksgiving.  
**69:31** And it will please the LORD better than an ox<br><i>Or</i> bull with horns and hoofs.  
**69:32** The humble <sup>①</sup>have seen <i>it and</i> are glad;<br>You who seek God, let your heart <sup>②</sup>revive.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some mss and ancient versions <i>will see</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>live</i>  
**69:33** For the LORD hears the needy,<br>And does not despise <i>those of</i> His <i>who are</i> prisoners.  
**69:34** Heaven and earth shall praise Him,<br>The seas and everything that moves in them.  
**69:35** For God will save Zion and build the cities of Judah,<br>So that they may live there and possess it.  
**69:36** The <sup>①</sup>descendants of His servants will inherit it,<br>And those who love His name will live in it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  

## 70장

**70:1** [For the music director. <i>A Psalm</i> of David; for a memorial.]<br>God, <i>hurry</i> to save me;<br>LORD, hurry to help me!  
**70:2** May those who seek my <sup>①</sup>life<br>Be put to shame and humiliated;<br>May those who delight <sup>②</sup>in my harm<br>Be turned back and dishonored.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>soul</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>to injure me</i>  
**70:3** May those who say, “Aha, aha!” be <sup>①</sup>turned back<br>Because of their shame.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some mss <i>appalled</i>  
**70:4** May all who seek You rejoice and be glad in You;<br>And may those who love Your salvation say continually,<br>“May God be exalted!”  
**70:5** But I am afflicted and needy;<br>Hurry to me, God!<br>You are my help and my savior;<br>LORD, do not delay.  

## 71장

**71:1** In You, LORD, I have taken refuge;<br>Let me never be put to shame.  
**71:2** In Your righteousness rescue me and save me;<br>Extend Your ear to me and help me.  
**71:3** Be to me a rock of dwelling to which I may continually come;<br>You have given the commandment to save me,<br>For You are my rock and my fortress.  
**71:4** Save me, my God, from the hand of the wicked,<br>From the <sup>①</sup>grasp of the wrongdoer and the ruthless,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>palm</i>  
**71:5** For You are my hope;<br>Lord <sup>①</sup>GOD, <i>You are</i> my confidence from my youth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YHWH,</i> usually rendered <i>LORD</i>  
**71:6** I have leaned on you since <i>my</i> birth;<br>You are He who took me from my mother’s womb;<br>My praise is continually <sup>①</sup>of You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in</i>  
**71:7** I have become a marvel to many,<br>For You are my strong refuge.  
**71:8** My mouth is filled with Your praise<br>And with Your glory all day long.  
**71:9** Do not cast me away at the time of <i>my</i> old age;<br>Do not abandon me when my strength fails.  
**71:10** For my enemies have spoken <sup>①</sup>against me;<br>And those who watch for my <sup>②</sup>life have consulted together,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>with reference to</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>soul</i>  
**71:11** Saying, “God has abandoned him;<br>Pursue and seize him, for there is no one to save <i>him.</i>”  
**71:12** God, do not be far from me;<br>My God, hurry to my aid!  
**71:13** May those who are enemies of my soul be put to shame <i>and</i> consumed;<br>May they be covered with disgrace and dishonor, who seek <sup>①</sup>to injure me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>my injury</i>  
**71:14** But as for me, I will wait continually,<br>And will <sup>①</sup>praise You yet more and more.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>add upon all Your praise</i>  
**71:15** My mouth shall tell of Your righteousness<br><i>And</i> of Your salvation all day long;<br>For I do not know the art of writing.  
**71:16** I will come with the mighty deeds of the Lord <sup>①</sup>GOD;<br>I will make mention of Your righteousness, Yours alone.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YHWH,</i> usually rendered <i>LORD</i>  
**71:17** God, You have taught me from my youth,<br>And I still declare Your wondrous deeds.  
**71:18** And even when <i>I am</i> old and gray, God, do not abandon me,<br>Until I declare Your <sup>①</sup>strength to <i>this</i> generation,<br>Your power to all who are to come.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>arm</i>  
**71:19**  <sup>①</sup>For Your righteousness, God, <i>reaches</i> to the <sup>②</sup>heavens,<br>You who have done great things;<br>God, who is like You?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>And</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>height</i>  
**71:20** You who have shown <sup>①</sup>me many troubles and distresses<br>Will revive <sup>②</sup>me again,<br>And will bring <sup>③</sup>me up again from the depths of the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Another reading is <i>us</i> <sup>②</sup>Another reading is <i>us</i> <sup>③</sup>Another reading is <i>us</i>  
**71:21** May You increase my greatness<br>And turn <i>to</i> comfort me.  
**71:22** I will also praise You with <sup>①</sup>a harp,<br><i>And</i> Your <sup>②</sup>truth, my God;<br>I will sing praises to You with the lyre,<br>Holy One of Israel.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>an instrument of a harp</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**71:23** My lips will shout for joy when I sing praises to You;<br>And my soul, which You have redeemed.  
**71:24** My tongue also will tell of Your righteousness all day long;<br>For they are put to shame, for they are humiliated who seek <sup>①</sup>my harm.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>to injure me</i>  

## 72장

**72:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of Solomon.]<br>Give the king Your judgments, God,<br>And Your righteousness to the king’s son.  
**72:2**  <sup>①</sup>May <sup>②</sup>he judge Your people with righteousness<br>And <sup>③</sup>Your afflicted with justice.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>He</i> will judge <sup>②</sup>Many of the pronouns in this Psalm may be rendered <i>He</i> since the typical reference is to the Messiah <sup>③</sup>Or <i>Your humble</i>  
**72:3**  <sup>①</sup>May the mountains bring <sup>②</sup>peace to the people,<br>And the hills, in righteousness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>The mountains will bring</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>prosperity</i>
