**72:4**  <sup>①</sup>May he vindicate the <sup>②</sup>afflicted of the people,<br>Save the children of the needy,<br>And crush the oppressor.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>He will vindicate</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>humble</i>  
**72:5**  <sup>①</sup>May they fear You while the sun <i>shines,</i><br>And <sup>②</sup>as long as the moon <i>shines,</i> throughout all generations.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>They will fear</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>before the moon</i>  
**72:6**  <sup>①</sup>May he come down like rain upon the mown grass,<br>Like showers that water the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>He will come down</i>  
**72:7**  <sup>①</sup>May the righteous flourish in his days,<br>As well as an abundance of peace, until the moon is no more.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>The righteous will flourish</i>  
**72:8** May he also rule from sea to sea,<br>And from the <i>Euphrates</i> River to the ends of the earth.  
**72:9**  <sup>①</sup>May the nomads of the desert bow before him,<br>And his enemies lick the dust.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>The nomads...will bow</i>  
**72:10**  <sup>①</sup>May the kings of Tarshish and of the <sup>②</sup>islands bring gifts;<br>May the kings of Sheba and Seba offer tributes.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>The kings...will bring</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>coastlands</i>  
**72:11**  <sup>①</sup>And may all kings bow down before him,<br>All nations serve him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>All kings will bow down</i>  
**72:12** For he will save the needy when he cries for help,<br>The <sup>①</sup>afflicted also, and him who has no helper.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>humble</i>  
**72:13** He will have compassion on the poor and needy,<br>And he will save the <sup>①</sup>lives of the needy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>souls</i>  
**72:14** He will <sup>①</sup>rescue their <sup>②</sup>life from oppression and violence,<br>And their blood will be precious in his sight;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>redeem</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>soul</i>  
**72:15** So may he live, and may the gold of Sheba be given to him;<br>And <sup>①</sup>they are to pray for him continually;<br> <sup>②</sup>They are to bless him all day long.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>he is to</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>He is to</i>  
**72:16** May there be abundance of grain on the earth on top of the mountains;<br>Its fruit will wave like <i>the cedars of</i> Lebanon;<br>And may those from the city flourish like the vegetation of the earth.  
**72:17** May his name endure forever;<br>May his name produce descendants <sup>①</sup>as long as the sun <i>shines;</i><br>And may <i>people</i> wish blessings on themselves by him;<br>May all nations call him blessed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>before the sun</i>  
**72:18** Blessed be the LORD God, the God of Israel,<br>Who alone works wonders.  
**72:19** And blessed be His glorious name forever;<br>And may the whole earth be filled with His glory.<br>Amen and Amen.  
**72:20** The prayers of David the son of Jesse are ended.  

## 73장

**73:1** [A Psalm of Asaph.]<br>God certainly is good to Israel,<br>To those who are pure in heart!  
**73:2** But as for me, my feet came close to stumbling,<br>My steps <sup>①</sup>had almost slipped.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>were caused to slip</i>  
**73:3** For I was envious of the <sup>①</sup>arrogant<br><i>As</i> I saw the prosperity of the wicked.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>boasters</i>  
**73:4** For there are no pains in their death,<br>And their belly is fat.  
**73:5** They are not <sup>①</sup>in trouble like other people,<br>Nor are they tormented together with <i>the rest of</i> mankind.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in the trouble of people</i>  
**73:6** Therefore arrogance is their necklace;<br>The garment of violence covers them.  
**73:7** Their eye <sup>①</sup>bulges from fatness;<br>The imaginations of <i>their</i> heart overflow.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>goes forth</i>  
**73:8** They mock and <sup>①</sup>wickedly speak of oppression;<br>They speak from on high.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>they speak in wickedness; From on high they speak of oppression</i>  
**73:9** They have set their mouth <sup>①</sup>against the heavens,<br>And their tongue <sup>②</sup>parades through the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>in</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>walks</i>  
**73:10** Therefore <sup>①</sup>his people return here,<br>And abundant waters are drunk by them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>His</i>  
**73:11** They say, “How does God know?<br>And is there knowledge <sup>①</sup>with the Most High?”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in</i>  
**73:12** Behold, these are the wicked;<br>And always at ease, they have increased <i>in</i> wealth.  
**73:13** Surely in vain I have <sup>①</sup>kept my heart pure<br>And washed my hands in innocence;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>cleansed my heart</i>  
**73:14** For I have been stricken all day long,<br>And <sup>①</sup>punished every morning.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>my punishment</i>  
**73:15** If I had said, “I will speak this way,”<br>Behold, I would have betrayed the generation of Your children.  
**73:16** When I thought of understanding this,<br>It was <sup>①</sup>troublesome in my sight<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>labor, trouble</i>  
**73:17** Until I entered the <sup>①</sup>sanctuary of God;<br><i>Then</i> I perceived their end.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>sanctuaries</i>  
**73:18** You indeed put them on slippery ground;<br>You dropped them into <sup>①</sup>ruin.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>deception</i>  
**73:19** How they are <sup>①</sup>destroyed in a moment!<br>They are utterly swept away by sudden terrors!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>become a desolation</i>  
**73:20** Like a dream when one awakes,<br>Lord, when stirred, You will despise their image.  
**73:21** When my heart was embittered<br>And I was pierced <sup>①</sup>within,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in my kidneys</i>  
**73:22** Then I was stupid and ignorant;<br>I was <i>like</i> an animal <sup>①</sup>before You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>with You</i>  
**73:23** Nevertheless I am continually with You;<br>You have taken hold of my right hand.  
**73:24** You will guide me with Your plan,<br>And afterward receive me <sup>①</sup>to glory.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>with honor</i>  
**73:25** Whom do I have in heaven <i>but You?</i><br>And with You, I desire nothing on earth.  
**73:26** My flesh and my heart <i>may</i> fail,<br><i>But</i> God is the <sup>①</sup>strength of my heart and my portion forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>rock</i>  
**73:27** For, behold, those who are far from You will perish;<br>You have destroyed all those who <sup>①</sup>are unfaithful to You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>go to a whoring from</i>  
**73:28** But as for me, the nearness of God is good for me;<br>I have made the Lord <sup>①</sup>GOD my refuge,<br>So that I may tell of all Your works.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YHWH,</i> usually rendered <i>LORD</i>  

## 74장

**74:1** [A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of Asaph.]<br>God, why have You rejected <i>us</i> forever?<br><i>Why</i> does Your anger smoke against the sheep of Your <sup>②</sup>pasture?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly, Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm <sup>②</sup>Or <i>pasturing</i>  
**74:2** Remember Your congregation, <i>which</i> You purchased of old,<br><i>Which</i> You have redeemed to be the tribe of Your inheritance;<br><i>And</i> this Mount Zion, where You have dwelt.  
**74:3**  <sup>①</sup>Step toward the <sup>②</sup>irreparable ruins;<br>The enemy has damaged everything in the sanctuary.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Lift Your footsteps</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>ruins</i> ruined <i>for all time</i>  
**74:4** Your adversaries have roared in the midst of Your meeting place;<br>They have set up their own signs as signs.  
**74:5** It <sup>①</sup>seems like one bringing up<br><i>His</i> axe into a <sup>②</sup>forest of trees.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>is known as</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>thicket</i>  
**74:6** And now they break down <sup>①</sup>all its carved work<br>With axe and hammers.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>altogether</i>  
**74:7** They have <sup>①</sup>burned Your sanctuary <sup>②</sup>to the ground;<br>They have defiled the dwelling place of Your name.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>set on fire</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>To the ground they...</i>  
**74:8** They said in their heart, “Let’s <sup>①</sup>completely <sup>②</sup>subdue them.”<br>They have burned all the meeting places of God in the land.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>altogether</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>oppress</i>  
**74:9** We do not see our signs;<br>There is no longer any prophet,<br>Nor is there <i>anyone</i> among us who knows how long.  
**74:10** How long, God, will the enemy taunt <i>You?</i><br>Shall the enemy treat Your name disrespectfully forever?  
**74:11** Why do You withdraw Your hand, even Your right hand?<br><i>Extend it</i> from Your chest <i>and</i> destroy <i>them!</i>  
**74:12** Yet God is my King from long ago,<br>Who performs acts of salvation in the midst of the earth.  
**74:13**  <sup>①</sup>You divided the sea by Your strength;<br> <sup>②</sup>You broke the heads of the sea monsters <sup>③</sup>in the waters.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>You Yourself</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>You Yourself</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>on</i>  
**74:14**  <sup>①</sup>You crushed the heads of <sup>②</sup>Leviathan;<br>You gave him as food for the <sup>③</sup>creatures of the wilderness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>You Yourself</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>sea monster</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>people</i>  
**74:15**  <sup>①</sup>You broke open springs and torrents;<br> <sup>②</sup>You dried up ever-flowing streams.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>You Yourself</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>You Yourself</i>  
**74:16** Yours is the day, Yours also is the night;<br> <sup>①</sup>You have prepared the <sup>②</sup>light and the sun.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>You Yourself</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>luminary</i>  
**74:17**  <sup>①</sup>You have established all the boundaries of the earth;<br> <sup>②</sup>You have <sup>③</sup>created summer and winter.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>You Yourself</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>You Yourself</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>formed</i>  
**74:18** Remember this, <sup>①</sup>LORD, that the enemy has taunted <i>You,</i><br>And a foolish people has treated Your name disrespectfully.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>that the enemy has taunted the LORD</i>  
**74:19** Do not give the soul of Your turtledove to the wild animal;<br>Do not forget the life of Your afflicted forever.  
**74:20** Consider the covenant;<br>For the dark places of the land are full of the places of violence.  
**74:21** May the oppressed person not return dishonored;<br>May the afflicted and the needy praise Your name.  
**74:22** Arise, God, <i>and</i> plead Your own cause;<br>Remember <sup>①</sup>how the foolish person taunts You all day long.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Your reproach from the foolish man</i>  
**74:23** Do not forget the voice of Your adversaries,<br>The uproar of those who rise against You, which ascends continually.  

## 75장

**75:1** [For the music director; <i>set to</i> <sup>①</sup>Al-tashheth. A Psalm of Asaph, a Song.]<br>We give thanks to You, God, we give thanks,<br>For Your name is near;<br>People declare Your wondrous works.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit Do Not Destroy  
**75:2** “When I select an appointed time,<br>It is I who judge fairly.  
**75:3** The earth and all who inhabit it <sup>①</sup>are unsteady;<br>It is I who have firmly set its pillars. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>totter</i>  
**75:4** I said to the boastful, ‘Do not boast,’<br>And to the wicked, ‘Do not lift up the horn;  
**75:5** Do not lift up your horn on high,<br>Do not speak with insolent <sup>①</sup>pride.’ ”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>neck</i>  
**75:6** For not from the east, nor from the west,<br>Nor from the <sup>①</sup>desert <i>comes</i> exaltation;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>mountainous desert</i>  
**75:7** But God is the Judge;<br>He puts down one and exalts another.  
**75:8** For a cup is in the hand of the LORD, and the wine foams;<br>It is <sup>①</sup>well mixed, and He pours out of this;<br>Certainly all the wicked of the earth must drain <i>and</i> drink its dregs.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>full of mixture</i>  
**75:9** But as for me, I will declare <i>it</i> forever;<br>I will sing praises to the God of Jacob.  
**75:10** And <sup>①</sup>He will cut off all the horns of the wicked,<br><i>But</i> the horns of the righteous will be lifted up.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>I</i>  

## 76장

**76:1** [For the music director; on stringed instruments. A Psalm of Asaph, a Song.]<br>God is known in Judah;<br>His name is great in Israel.  
**76:2** His <sup>①</sup>tabernacle is in Salem;<br>His dwelling place also is in Zion.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>shelter</i>  
**76:3** There He broke the <sup>①</sup>flaming arrows,<br>The shield, the sword, and the <sup>②</sup>weapons of war. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>fiery shafts of the bow</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>battle</i>  
**76:4** You are resplendent,<br> <sup>①</sup>More majestic than the mountains of prey.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Majestic from the mountains</i>  
**76:5** The stouthearted were plundered,<br> <sup>①</sup>They sank into sleep;<br>And none of the <sup>②</sup>warriors could use his hands.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>They slumbered their sleep</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>men of might have found their hands</i>  
**76:6** At Your rebuke, God of Jacob,<br>Both <sup>①</sup>rider and horse were cast into a dead sleep.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>chariot</i>  
**76:7** You, You <i>indeed</i> are to be feared,<br>And who may stand in Your presence, <sup>①</sup>once You are angry?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>since Your anger</i>  
**76:8** You caused judgment to be heard from heaven;<br>The earth feared and was still  
**76:9** When God arose to judgment,<br>To save all the humble of the earth. <i>Selah</i>  
**76:10** For the <sup>①</sup>wrath of mankind shall praise You;<br>You will encircle Yourself with a remnant of wrath.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>wraths</i>  
**76:11** Make vows to the LORD your God and fulfill <i>them;</i><br>All who are around Him are to bring gifts to Him who is to be feared.  
**76:12** He will cut off the spirit of princes;<br>He is <sup>①</sup>feared by the kings of the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>awesome to</i>  

## 77장

**77:1** [For the music director; <sup>①</sup>according to Jeduthun. A Psalm of Asaph.]<br>My voice <i>rises</i> to God, and I will cry aloud;<br>My voice <i>rises</i> to God, and He will listen to me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>1 Chr 16:41  
**77:2** In the day of my trouble I sought the Lord;<br>In the night my hand was stretched out and did not grow weary;<br>My soul refused to be comforted.  
**77:3** <i>When</i> I remember God, then I am restless;<br><i>When</i> I sigh, then my spirit feels weak. <i>Selah</i>  
**77:4** You have held my eyelids <i>open;</i><br>I am so troubled that I cannot speak.  
**77:5** I have considered the days of old,<br>The years of long ago.  
**77:6** I will remember my song in the night;<br>I will meditate with my heart,<br>And my spirit <sup>①</sup>ponders:<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>searched</i>  
**77:7** Will the Lord reject forever?<br>And will He never be favorable again?  
**77:8** Has His favor ceased forever?<br>Has <i>His</i> <sup>①</sup>promise come to an end <sup>②</sup>forever?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>word</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>from generation to generation</i>  
**77:9** Has God forgotten to be gracious,<br>Or has He in anger <sup>①</sup>withdrawn His compassion? <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>shut up</i>  
**77:10** Then I said, “It is my <sup>①</sup>grief,<br>That the right hand of the Most High has changed.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>infirmity, the years of the right hand of the Most High</i>  
**77:11** I shall remember the deeds of <sup>①</sup>the LORD;<br>I will certainly remember Your wonders of old.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**77:12** I will meditate on all Your work,<br>And on Your deeds with thanksgiving.  
**77:13** Your way, God, is holy;<br>What god is great like our God?  
**77:14** You are the God who works wonders;<br>You have made known Your strength among the peoples.  
**77:15** By Your <sup>①</sup>power You have redeemed Your people,<br>The sons of Jacob and Joseph. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>arm</i>  
**77:16** The waters saw You, God;<br>The waters saw You, they were in anguish;<br>The ocean depths also trembled.  
**77:17** The clouds poured out water;<br>The skies <sup>①</sup>sounded out;<br>Your arrows <sup>②</sup>flashed here and there.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>gave a sound</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>went</i>  
**77:18** The sound of Your thunder was in the whirlwind;<br>The lightning lit up the world;<br>The earth trembled and shook.  
**77:19** Your way was in the sea<br>And Your paths in the mighty waters,<br>And Your footprints were not known.  
**77:20** You led Your people like a flock<br>By the hand of Moses and Aaron.  

## 78장

**78:1** [A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of Asaph.]<br>Listen, my people, to my <sup>②</sup>instruction;<br>Incline your ears to the words of my mouth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly, Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm <sup>②</sup>Or <i>law, teaching</i>  
**78:2** I will open my mouth in a parable;<br>I will tell riddles of old,  
**78:3** Which we have heard and known,<br>And our fathers have told us.  
**78:4** We will not conceal them from their children,<br>But we will tell the generation to come the praises of the LORD,<br>And His power and His wondrous works that He has done.  
**78:5** For He established a testimony in Jacob,<br>And appointed a law in Israel,<br>Which He commanded our fathers<br>That they were to <sup>①</sup>teach them to their children,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>make them known</i>  
**78:6** So that the generation to come would know, the children <i>yet</i> to be born,<br><i>That</i> they would arise and tell <i>them</i> to their children,  
**78:7** So that they would put their confidence in God<br>And not forget the works of God,<br>But comply with His commandments,  
**78:8** And not be like their fathers,<br>A stubborn and rebellious generation,<br>A generation that did not <sup>①</sup>prepare its heart<br>And whose spirit was not faithful to God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>put right</i>  
**78:9** The sons of Ephraim <sup>①</sup>were archers equipped with bows,<br><i>Yet</i> they turned back on the day of battle.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>being</i>  
**78:10** They did not keep the covenant of God<br>And refused to walk in His Law;  
**78:11** They forgot His deeds<br>And His <sup>①</sup>miracles that He had shown them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>wonderful works</i>  
**78:12** He performed wonders before their fathers<br>In the land of Egypt, <i>in</i> the field of Zoan.  
**78:13** He divided the sea and caused them to pass through,<br>And He made the waters stand up like a heap.  
**78:14** Then He led them with the cloud by day<br>And all the night with a light of fire.  
**78:15** He split the rocks in the wilderness<br>And gave <i>them</i> plenty to drink like the ocean depths.  
**78:16** He brought forth streams from the rock<br>And made waters run down like rivers.  
**78:17** Yet they still continued to sin against Him,<br>To rebel against the Most High in the desert.  
**78:18** And in their heart they put God to the test<br>By asking for food <sup>①</sup>that suited their taste.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to their soul</i>  
**78:19** Then they spoke against God;<br>They said, “Can God prepare a table in the wilderness?  
**78:20** Behold, He struck the rock so that waters gushed out,<br>And streams were overflowing;<br>Can He also provide bread?<br>Will He prepare meat for His people?”  
**78:21** Therefore the LORD heard and <sup>①</sup>was full of wrath;<br>And a fire was kindled against Jacob,<br>And anger also mounted against Israel,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>became infuriated</i>  
**78:22** Because they did not believe in God<br>And did not trust in His salvation.  
**78:23** Yet He commanded the clouds above<br>And opened the doors of heaven;  
**78:24** He rained down manna upon them to eat,<br>And gave them <sup>①</sup>food from heaven.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>grain</i>  
**78:25** Man ate the bread of <sup>①</sup>angels;<br>He sent them <sup>②</sup>food <sup>③</sup>in abundance.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>mighty ones</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>provision</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>to satiation</i>  
**78:26** He made the east wind blow in the sky<br>And by His <sup>①</sup>power He directed the south wind.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>strength</i>  
**78:27** When He rained meat upon them like the dust,<br>Even winged fowl like the sand of the seas,  
**78:28** He let <i>them</i> fall in the midst of <sup>①</sup>their camp,<br>All around their dwellings.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>His</i>  
**78:29** So they ate and were well filled,<br>And He <sup>①</sup>satisfied their longing.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>brought to them their</i>  
**78:30**  <sup>①</sup>Yet before they had abandoned their longing,<br>While their food was in their mouths,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>They did not turn away from their</i>  
**78:31** The anger of God rose against them<br>And killed <sup>①</sup>some of their strongest ones,<br>And <sup>②</sup>subdued the choice men of Israel.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>among their fat ones</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>caused to bow down</i>  
**78:32** In spite of all this they still sinned<br>And did not believe in His wonderful works.  
**78:33** So He brought their days to an end in futility,<br>And their years <i>to an end</i> in sudden terror.  
**78:34** When He killed them, then they sought Him,<br>And they returned and searched diligently for God;  
**78:35** And they remembered that God was their rock,<br>And the Most High God their Redeemer.  
**78:36** But they flattered Him with their mouth<br>And lied to Him with their tongue.  
**78:37** For their heart was not steadfast toward Him,<br>Nor were they faithful with His covenant.  
**78:38** But He, being compassionate, <sup>①</sup>forgave <i>their</i> wrongdoing and did not destroy <i>them;</i><br>And often He <sup>②</sup>restrained His anger<br>And did not stir up all His wrath.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>covered over, atoned for</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>turned away</i>  
**78:39** So He remembered that they were <i>only</i> flesh,<br>A <sup>①</sup>wind that passes and does not return.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>breath</i>  
**78:40** How often they rebelled against Him in the wilderness<br>And grieved Him in the desert!  
**78:41** Again and again they <sup>①</sup>tempted God,<br>And pained the Holy One of Israel.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>put God to the test</i>  
**78:42** They did not remember His <sup>①</sup>power,<br>The day when He redeemed them from the enemy,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>hand</i>  
**78:43** When He performed His signs in Egypt<br>And His marvels in the field of Zoan,  
**78:44** And turned their rivers to blood,<br>And their streams, <i>so that</i> they could not drink.  
**78:45** He sent swarms of flies among them that devoured them,<br>And frogs that destroyed them.  
**78:46** He also gave their crops to the grasshopper<br>And the product of their labor to the locust.  
**78:47** He <sup>①</sup>destroyed their vines with hailstones<br>And their sycamore trees with frost.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>was killing</i>  
**78:48** He also turned their cattle over to the hailstones,<br>And their herds to bolts of lightning.  
**78:49** He sent His burning anger upon them,<br>Fury and indignation and trouble,<br>A band of <sup>①</sup>destroying angels.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>angels of evil ones;</i> another reading is <i>evil angels</i>  
**78:50** He leveled a path for His anger;<br>He did not spare their souls from death,<br>But turned their lives over to the plague,  
**78:51** And struck all the firstborn in Egypt,<br>The first and best of their vigor in the tents of Ham.  
**78:52** But He led His own people out like sheep,<br>And guided them in the wilderness like a flock;  
**78:53** He led them safely, so that they did not fear;<br>But the sea engulfed their enemies.  
**78:54** So He brought them to His holy <sup>①</sup>land,<br>To this <sup>②</sup>hill country which His right hand had gained.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>border, territory</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>mountain</i>  
**78:55** He also drove out the nations from them<br>And apportioned them as an inheritance by measurement,<br>And had the tribes of Israel dwell in their tents.  
**78:56** Yet they <sup>①</sup>tempted and rebelled against the Most High God<br>And did not keep His testimonies,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>put to the test</i>  
**78:57** But turned back and acted treacherously like their fathers;<br>They turned aside like a treacherous bow.  
**78:58** For they provoked Him with their high places<br>And moved Him to jealousy with their carved images.  
**78:59** When God heard <i>them,</i> He <sup>①</sup>was filled with wrath<br>And He utterly rejected Israel;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>became infuriated</i>  
**78:60** So that He abandoned the dwelling place at Shiloh,<br>The tent <sup>①</sup>which He had pitched among people,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some ancient versions <i>where He dwelt</i>  
**78:61** And He gave up His strength to captivity<br>And His glory into the hand of the enemy.  
**78:62** He also turned His people over to the sword,<br>And <sup>①</sup>was filled with wrath at His inheritance.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>became infuriated</i>  
**78:63** Fire devoured <sup>①</sup>His young men,<br>And <sup>②</sup>His virgins had no wedding songs.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>their</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>their</i>  
**78:64**  <sup>①</sup>His priests fell by the sword,<br>And <sup>②</sup>His widows could not weep.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>their</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>their</i>  
**78:65** Then the Lord awoke as <i>if from</i> sleep,<br>Like a warrior <sup>①</sup>overcome by wine.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>sobered up from</i>  
**78:66** He <sup>①</sup>drove His adversaries backward;<br>He put on them an everlasting disgrace.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>struck</i>  
**78:67** He also rejected the tent of Joseph,<br>And did not choose the tribe of Ephraim,  
**78:68** But chose the tribe of Judah,<br>Mount Zion, which He loved.  
**78:69** And He built His sanctuary like the heights,<br>Like the earth which He has established forever.  
**78:70** He also chose His servant David<br>And took him from the sheepfolds;  
**78:71** From <sup>①</sup>the care of the <sup>②</sup>ewes with nursing lambs He brought him<br>To shepherd Jacob His people,<br>And Israel His inheritance.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>following</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>ewes that wet-nurse, He</i>  
**78:72** So he shepherded them according to the integrity of his heart,<br>And guided them with his skillful hands.  

## 79장

**79:1** [A Psalm of Asaph.]<br>God, the nations have <sup>①</sup>invaded Your inheritance;<br>They have defiled Your holy temple;<br>They have laid Jerusalem in ruins.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>come into</i>  
**79:2** They have given the dead bodies of Your servants to the birds of the sky as food,<br>The flesh of Your godly ones to the animals of the earth.  
**79:3** They have poured out their blood like water all around Jerusalem;<br>And there was no one to bury them.  
**79:4** We have become a disgrace before our neighbors,<br>An <i>object of</i> derision and ridicule to those around us.  
**79:5** How long, LORD? Will You be angry forever?<br>Will Your jealousy burn like fire?  
**79:6** Pour out Your wrath upon the nations which do not know You,<br>And upon the kingdoms which do not call upon Your name.  
**79:7** For they have devoured Jacob<br>And laid waste his <sup>①</sup>settlement.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>pasture</i>  
**79:8** Do not <sup>①</sup>hold us responsible for <sup>②</sup>the guilty deeds of <i>our</i> forefathers;<br>Let Your compassion come quickly to meet us,<br>For we have become very low.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>remember against us</i> <sup>②</sup>Or our <i>former sins</i>  
**79:9** Help us, God of our salvation, for the glory of Your name;<br>And save us and <sup>①</sup>forgive our sins for the sake of Your name.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>cover over, atone for</i>  
**79:10** Why should the nations say, “Where is their God?”<br>Let vengeance for the blood of Your servants which has been shed<br>Be known among the nations in our sight.  
**79:11** Let the groaning of the prisoner come before You;<br>According to the greatness of Your <sup>①</sup>power, let <sup>②</sup>those who are doomed to die remain.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>arm</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>the sons of death</i>  
**79:12** And return to our neighbors seven times <i>as much</i> into their lap<br>Their taunts with which they have taunted You, Lord.  
**79:13** So we Your people and the sheep of Your <sup>①</sup>pasture<br>Will give thanks to You forever;<br>To all generations we will tell of Your praise.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>pasturing</i>  

## 80장

**80:1** [For the music director; set to <sup>①</sup>El Shoshannim; <sup>②</sup>Eduth. A Psalm of Asaph.]<br>Listen, Shepherd of Israel,<br>Who leads Joseph like a flock;<br>You who are enthroned <i>above</i> the cherubim, shine forth!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly, to the Lilies <sup>②</sup>Lit A testimony  
**80:2** Before Ephraim, Benjamin, and Manasseh, awaken Your power,<br>And come to save us!  
**80:3** God, restore us<br>And make Your face shine <i>upon us,</i> <sup>①</sup>and we will be saved.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>that we may</i>  
**80:4** LORD God of armies,<br>How long will You <sup>①</sup>be angry with the prayer of Your people?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>smoke against</i>  
**80:5** You have fed them with the bread of tears,<br>And You have made them drink tears in <sup>①</sup>large measure.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>a third part of a</i>  
**80:6** You make us <sup>①</sup>an object of contention to our neighbors,<br>And our enemies laugh among themselves.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>strife to</i>  
**80:7** God of armies, restore us<br>And make Your face shine <i>upon us,</i> <sup>①</sup>and we will be saved.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>that we may</i>  
**80:8** You removed a vine from Egypt;<br>You drove out the <sup>①</sup>nations and planted it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Gentiles</i>  
**80:9** You cleared <i>the ground</i> before it,<br>And it took deep root and filled the land.  
**80:10** The mountains were covered with its shadow,<br>And <sup>①</sup>the cedars of God with its branches.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>its boughs are like the cedars of God</i>  
**80:11** It was sending out its branches to the sea<br>And its shoots to the <i>Euphrates</i> River.  
**80:12** Why have You broken down its <sup>①</sup>hedges,<br>So that all who pass <i>that</i> way pick its <i>fruit?</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>walls, fences</i>  
**80:13** A boar from the forest eats it away,<br>And whatever moves in the field feeds on it.  
**80:14** God of armies, do turn back;<br>Look down from heaven and see, and take care of this vine,  
**80:15** The <sup>①</sup>shoot which Your right hand has planted,<br>And of the <sup>②</sup>son whom You have <sup>③</sup>strengthened for Yourself.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>root</i> <sup>②</sup>Or figuratively: <i>branch</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>secured</i>  
**80:16** It is burned with fire, it is cut down;<br>They perish from the rebuke of Your face.  
**80:17** Let Your hand be upon the man of Your right hand,<br>Upon the son of man whom You made strong for Yourself.  
**80:18** Then we will not turn back from You;<br>Revive us, and we will call upon Your name.  
**80:19** LORD God of armies, restore us;<br>Make Your face shine <i>upon us,</i> <sup>①</sup>and we will be saved.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>that we may</i>  

## 81장

**81:1** [For the music director; <sup>①</sup>on the Gittith. A Psalm of Asaph.]<br>Sing for joy to God our strength;<br>Shout joyfully to the God of Jacob.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or according to  
**81:2** Raise a song, strike the tambourine,<br>The sweet sounding lyre with the harp.  
**81:3** Blow the trumpet at the new moon,<br>At the full moon, on our feast day.  
**81:4** For it is a statute for Israel,<br>An ordinance of the God of Jacob.  
**81:5** He established it as a testimony in Joseph<br>When he <sup>①</sup>went throughout the land of Egypt.<br>I heard a language I did not know:<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>went out over</i>  
**81:6** “I <sup>①</sup>relieved his shoulder of the burden,<br>His hands were freed from the <sup>②</sup>basket.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>removed his shoulder from</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>brick load</i>  
**81:7** You called in trouble and I rescued you;<br>I answered you in the hiding place of thunder;<br>I put you to the test at the waters of Meribah. <i>Selah</i>  
**81:8** Hear, My people, and I will admonish you;<br>Israel, if you would listen to Me!  
**81:9** There shall be no strange god among you;<br>Nor shall you worship a foreign god.  
**81:10** I, the LORD, am your God,<br>Who brought you up from the land of Egypt;<br>Open your mouth wide and I will fill it.  
**81:11** “But My people did not listen to My voice,<br>And Israel did not <sup>①</sup>obey Me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>yield to</i>  
**81:12** So I gave <sup>①</sup>them over to the stubbornness of their heart,<br>To walk by their own plans.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>him</i>  
**81:13** Oh that My people would listen to Me,<br>That Israel would walk in My ways!  
**81:14** I would quickly subdue their enemies<br>And turn My hand against their adversaries.  
**81:15** Those who hate the LORD would pretend to obey Him,<br>And their time <i>of punishment</i> would be forever.  
**81:16**  <sup>①</sup>But I would feed you with the <sup>②</sup>finest of the wheat,<br>And with honey from the rock I would satisfy you.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>He would feed him</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>fat</i>  

## 82장

**82:1** [A Psalm of Asaph.]<br>God takes His position in <sup>①</sup>His assembly;<br>He judges in the midst of the gods.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the assembly of God</i>  
**82:2** How long will you judge unjustly<br>And show partiality to the wicked? <i>Selah</i>  
**82:3** Vindicate the weak and fatherless;<br>Do justice to the afflicted and destitute.  
**82:4** Rescue the weak and needy;<br>Save <i>them</i> from the hand of the wicked.  
**82:5** They do not know nor do they understand;<br>They walk around in darkness;<br>All the foundations of the earth are shaken.  
**82:6**  <sup>①</sup>I said, “You are gods,<br>And all of you are sons of the Most High.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>I, on my part</i>  
**82:7** Nevertheless you will die like men,<br>And fall like one of the princes.”  
**82:8** Arise, God, judge the earth!<br>For You possess all the nations.  

## 83장

**83:1** [A Song, a Psalm of Asaph.]<br>God, do not remain quiet;<br>Do not be silent and, God, do not be still.  
**83:2** For behold, Your enemies make an uproar,<br>And those who hate You have <sup>①</sup>exalted themselves.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>lifted up the head</i>  
**83:3** They make shrewd plans against Your people,<br>And <sup>①</sup>conspire together against Your <sup>②</sup>treasured ones.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>consult</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>hidden ones</i>  
**83:4** They have said, “Come, and let’s wipe them out <sup>①</sup>as a nation,<br>So that the name of Israel will no longer be remembered.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>from</i>  
**83:5** For they have <sup>①</sup>conspired together with one mind;<br>They make a covenant against You:<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>consulted</i>  
**83:6** The tents of Edom and the Ishmaelites,<br>Moab and the Hagrites;  
**83:7** Gebal, Ammon, and Amalek,<br>Philistia with the inhabitants of Tyre;  
**83:8** Assyria also has joined them;<br>They have become <sup>①</sup>a help to the children of Lot. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>an arm</i>  
**83:9** Deal with them as with Midian,<br>As with Sisera <i>and</i> Jabin at the river of Kishon,  
**83:10** Who were destroyed at En-dor,<br>Who became <i>like</i> dung for the ground.  
**83:11** Make their nobles like Oreb and Zeeb,<br>And all their leaders like Zebah and Zalmunna,  
**83:12** Who said, “Let’s possess for ourselves<br>The pastures of God.”  
**83:13** My God, make them like the <sup>①</sup>whirling dust,<br>Like chaff before the wind.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>tumbleweed</i>  
**83:14** Like fire that burns the forest,<br>And like a flame that sets the mountains on fire,  
**83:15** So pursue them with Your heavy gale,<br>And terrify them with Your storm.  
**83:16** Fill their faces with dishonor,<br>So that they will seek Your name, LORD.  
**83:17** May they be ashamed and dismayed forever,<br>And may they be humiliated and perish,  
**83:18** So that they will know that You alone, whose name is the LORD,<br>Are the Most High over all the earth.  

## 84장

**84:1** [For the music director; <sup>①</sup>on the Gittith. A Psalm of the sons of Korah.]<br>How lovely are Your dwelling places,<br>LORD of armies!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or according to  
**84:2** My soul longed and even yearned for the courtyards of the LORD;<br>My heart and my flesh sing for joy to the living God.  
**84:3** The bird also has found a house,<br>And the swallow a nest for herself, where she may put her young:<br>Your altars, LORD of armies,<br>My King and my God.  
**84:4** Blessed are those who dwell in Your house!<br>They are ever praising You. <i>Selah</i>  
**84:5** Blessed is the person whose strength is in You,<br>In <sup>①</sup>whose heart are the roads <i>to Zion!</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>their</i>  
**84:6** Passing through the Valley of <sup>①</sup>Baca they make it a <sup>②</sup>spring;<br>The early rain also covers it with blessings.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Prob. <i>Weeping;</i> or <i>Balsam-shrubs</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>place of springs</i>  
**84:7** They go from strength to strength,<br> <sup>①</sup><i>Every one of them</i> appears before God in Zion.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some ancient versions <i>The God of gods will be seen in Zion</i>  
**84:8** LORD God of armies, hear my prayer;<br>Listen, God of Jacob! <i>Selah</i>  
**84:9** See our shield, God,<br>And look at the face of Your anointed.  
**84:10** For a day in Your courtyards is better than a thousand <i>elsewhere.</i><br>I would rather stand at the threshold of the house of my God<br>Than live in the tents of wickedness.  
**84:11** For the LORD God is a sun and shield;<br>The LORD gives grace and glory;<br>He withholds no good thing <sup>①</sup>from those who walk with integrity.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>with regard to</i>  
**84:12** LORD of armies,<br>Blessed is the person who trusts in You!  

## 85장

**85:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of the sons of Korah.]<br>LORD, You showed favor to Your land;<br>You restored the fortunes of Jacob.  
**85:2** You forgave the guilt of Your people;<br>You covered all their sin. <i>Selah</i>  
**85:3** You withdrew all Your fury;<br>You turned away from Your burning anger.  
**85:4** Restore us, God of our salvation,<br>And cause Your indignation toward us to cease.  
**85:5** Will You be angry with us forever?<br>Will You prolong Your anger to <sup>①</sup>all generations?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>generation and generation</i>  
**85:6** Will You not <sup>①</sup>revive us again,<br>So that Your people may rejoice in You?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>bring to life</i>  
**85:7** Show us Your mercy, LORD,<br>And grant us Your salvation.  
**85:8**  <sup>①</sup>I will hear what God the LORD will say;<br>For He will speak peace to His people, to His godly ones;<br>And may they not turn back to <sup>②</sup>foolishness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Let me hear</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., foolish self-confidence  
**85:9** Certainly His salvation is near to those who <sup>①</sup>fear Him,<br>That glory may dwell in our land.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**85:10** Graciousness and <sup>①</sup>truth have met together;<br>Righteousness and peace have kissed each other.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**85:11**  <sup>①</sup>Truth sprouts from the earth,<br>And righteousness looks down from heaven.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Faithfulness</i>  
**85:12** Indeed, the LORD will give what is good,<br>And our land will yield its produce.  
**85:13** Righteousness will go before Him<br>And will make His footsteps into a way.  

## 86장

**86:1** [A Prayer of David.]<br>Incline Your ear, LORD, <i>and</i> answer me;<br>For I am afflicted and needy.  
**86:2** Protect my <sup>①</sup>soul, for I am godly;<br>You my God, save Your servant who trusts in You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>life</i>  
**86:3** Be gracious to me, Lord,<br>For I call upon You all day long.  
**86:4** Make the soul of Your servant joyful,<br>For to You, Lord, I lift up my soul.  
**86:5** For You, Lord, are good, and ready to forgive,<br>And abundant in mercy to all who call upon You.  
**86:6** Listen, LORD, to my prayer;<br>And give <i>Your</i> attention to the sound of my pleading!  
**86:7** On the day of my trouble I will call upon You,<br>For You will answer me.  
**86:8** There is no one like You among the gods, Lord,<br>Nor are there any works like Yours.  
**86:9** All nations whom You have made will come and worship before You, Lord,<br>And they will glorify Your name.  
**86:10** For You are great, and you do <sup>①</sup>wondrous deeds;<br>You alone are God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>miracles</i>  
**86:11** Teach me Your way, LORD;<br>I will walk in Your truth;<br>Unite my heart to fear Your name.  
**86:12** I will give thanks to You, Lord my God, with all my heart,<br>And I will glorify Your name forever.  
**86:13** For Your graciousness toward me is great,<br>And You have saved my soul from the <sup>①</sup>depths of <sup>②</sup>Sheol.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>lowest Sheol</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**86:14** God, arrogant men have risen up against me,<br>And a gang of violent men have sought my <sup>①</sup>life,<br>And they have not set You before them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>soul</i>  
**86:15** But You, Lord, are a compassionate and gracious God,<br>Slow to anger and abundant in mercy and <sup>①</sup>truth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**86:16** Turn to me, and be gracious to me;<br>Grant Your strength to Your servant,<br>And save the son of Your maidservant.  
**86:17** Show me a sign of good,<br>That those who hate me may see <i>it</i> and be ashamed,<br>Because You, LORD, have helped me and comforted me.  

## 87장

**87:1** [A Psalm of the sons of Korah. A Song.]<br>His foundation is in the holy mountains.  
**87:2** The LORD loves the gates of Zion<br>More than all the <i>other</i> dwelling places of Jacob.  
**87:3** Glorious things are spoken of you,<br>City of God. <i>Selah</i>  
**87:4** “I shall mention <sup>①</sup>Rahab and Babylon <sup>②</sup>among those who know Me;<br>Behold, Philistia and Tyre with <sup>③</sup>Cush:<br>‘This one was born there.’ ”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., Egypt, as a sea monster; not to be confused with Rahab in Joshua 2 <sup>②</sup>Or <i>as</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>Ethiopia</i>  
**87:5** But of Zion it will be said, “This one and that one were born in her”;<br>And the Most High Himself will establish her.  
**87:6** The LORD will count when He registers the peoples,<br>“This one was born there.” <i>Selah</i>  
**87:7** Then those who sing as <i>well as</i> those who <sup>①</sup>play the flutes <i>will say,</i><br>“All my springs <i>of joy</i> are in you.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>dance</i>  

## 88장

**88:1** [A Song. A Psalm of the sons of Korah. For the music director; according to Mahalath Leannoth. A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of Heman <sup>②</sup>the Ezrahite.]<br>LORD, the God of my salvation,<br>I have cried out by day and in the night before You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly, Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm <sup>②</sup>1 Kin 4:31; 1 Chr 2:6; Ps 89: title  
**88:2** Let my prayer come before You;<br>Incline Your ear to my cry!  
**88:3** For my soul has <sup>①</sup>had enough troubles,<br>And my life has approached <sup>②</sup>Sheol.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>been satisfied with</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**88:4** I am counted among those who go down to the pit;<br>I have become like a man without strength,  
**88:5**  <sup>①</sup>Abandoned among the dead,<br>Like the slain who lie in the grave,<br>Whom You no longer remember,<br>And they are cut off from Your hand.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>A freed one among the dead</i>  
**88:6** You have put me in the lowest pit,<br>In dark places, in the depths.  
**88:7** Your wrath has rested upon me,<br>And You have afflicted me with all Your waves. <i>Selah</i>  
**88:8** You have removed my acquaintances far from me;<br>You have made me an <sup>①</sup>object of loathing to them;<br>I am shut up and cannot go out.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>abomination to them</i>  
**88:9** My eye grows dim from misery;<br>I have called upon You every day, LORD;<br>I have spread out my <sup>①</sup>hands to You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>palms</i>  
**88:10** Will You perform wonders for the dead?<br>Or will the departed spirits rise <i>and</i> praise You? <i>Selah</i>  
**88:11** Will Your graciousness be declared in the grave,<br>Your faithfulness in <sup>①</sup>Abaddon?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., place of destruction  
**88:12** Will Your wonders be made known in the darkness?<br>And Your righteousness in the land of forgetfulness?  
**88:13** But I, LORD, have cried out to You for help,<br>And in the morning my prayer comes before You.  
**88:14** LORD, why do You reject my soul?<br><i>Why</i> do You hide Your face from me?  
**88:15** I was miserable and about to die from my youth on;<br>I suffer Your terrors; I grow weary.  
**88:16** Your burning anger has passed over me;<br>Your terrors have <sup>①</sup>destroyed me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>silenced</i>  
**88:17** They have surrounded me like water all day long;<br>They have encircled me altogether.  
**88:18** You have removed lover and friend far from me;<br>My acquaintances are <i>in</i> a hiding place.  

## 89장

**89:1** [A <sup>①</sup>Maskil of <sup>②</sup>Ethan <sup>③</sup>the Ezrahite.]<br>I will sing of the graciousness of the LORD forever;<br>To all generations I will make Your faithfulness known with my mouth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly, Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm <sup>②</sup>1 Kin 4:31 <sup>③</sup>Ps 88: title  
**89:2** For I have said, “Graciousness will be built up forever;<br>In the heavens You will establish Your faithfulness.”  
**89:3** “I have made a covenant with My chosen;<br>I have sworn to My servant David,  
**89:4** I will establish your <sup>①</sup>descendants forever<br>And build up your throne to all generations.” <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**89:5** The heavens will praise Your wonders, LORD;<br>Your faithfulness also in the assembly of the holy ones.  
**89:6** For who in the skies is comparable to the LORD?<br>Who among the <sup>①</sup>sons of the mighty is like the LORD,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>sons of gods</i>  
**89:7** A God greatly feared in the council of the holy ones,<br>And awesome above all those who are around Him?  
**89:8** LORD God of armies, who is like You, mighty <sup>①</sup>LORD?<br>Your faithfulness also surrounds You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**89:9** You rule the surging of the sea;<br>When its waves rise, You calm them.  
**89:10** You Yourself crushed <sup>①</sup>Rahab like one who is slain;<br>You scattered Your enemies with <sup>②</sup>Your mighty arm.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., Egypt, as a sea monster; not to be confused with Rahab in Joshua 2 <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>the arm of Your might</i>  
**89:11** The heavens are Yours, the earth also is Yours;<br>The world and <sup>①</sup>all it contains, You have established them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>its fullness</i>  
**89:12** The north and the south, You have created them;<br>Tabor and Hermon shout for joy at Your name.  
**89:13** You have <sup>①</sup>a strong arm;<br>Your hand is mighty, Your right hand is exalted.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>an arm with strength</i>  
**89:14** Righteousness and justice are the foundation of Your throne;<br>Mercy and <sup>①</sup>truth go before You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**89:15** Blessed are the people who know the <sup>①</sup>joyful sound!<br>LORD, they walk in the light of Your face.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>blast of the trumpet, shout of joy</i>  
**89:16** In Your name they rejoice all the day,<br>And by Your righteousness they are exalted.  
**89:17** For You are the glory of their strength,<br>And by Your favor <sup>①</sup>our horn is exalted.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Another reading is <i>You exalt our horn</i>  
**89:18** For our shield belongs to the LORD,<br> <sup>①</sup>And our king to the Holy One of Israel.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Even to the Holy One of Israel our King</i>  
**89:19**  <sup>①</sup>Once You spoke in vision to Your godly <sup>②</sup>ones,<br>And said, “I have <sup>③</sup>given help to one who is mighty;<br>I have exalted one chosen from the people.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>At that time</i> <sup>②</sup>Some mss <i>one</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>placed help upon</i>  
**89:20** I have found My servant David;<br>With My holy oil I have anointed him,  
**89:21** With whom My hand will be established;<br>My arm also will strengthen him.  
**89:22** The enemy will not <sup>①</sup>deceive him,<br>Nor will the <sup>②</sup>son of wickedness afflict him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>exact usury from him</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>wicked man</i>  
**89:23** But I will crush his adversaries before him,<br>And strike those who hate him.  
**89:24** My faithfulness and My favor <i>will be</i> with him,<br>And in My name his horn will be exalted.  
**89:25** I will also place his hand on the sea,<br>And his right hand on the rivers.  
**89:26** He will call to Me, ‘You are my Father,<br>My God, and the rock of my salvation.’  
**89:27** I will also make him <i>My</i> firstborn,<br>The highest of the kings of the earth.  
**89:28** I will maintain My favor for him forever,<br>And My covenant shall be confirmed to him.  
**89:29** So I will establish his <sup>①</sup>descendants forever,<br>And his throne as the days of heaven.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**89:30** “If his sons abandon My Law<br>And do not walk in My judgments,  
**89:31** If they <sup>①</sup>violate My statutes<br>And do not keep My commandments,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>profane</i>  
**89:32** Then I will punish their wrongdoing with the rod,<br>And their guilt with <sup>①</sup>afflictions.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>plagues</i>  
**89:33** But I will not <sup>①</sup>withhold My favor from him,<br>Nor deal falsely in My faithfulness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>break</i>  
**89:34** I will not <sup>①</sup>violate My covenant,<br>Nor will I alter <sup>②</sup>the utterance of My lips.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>profane</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>that which goes forth</i>  
**89:35**  <sup>①</sup>Once I have sworn by My holiness;<br>I will not lie to David.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>One thing</i>  
**89:36** His <sup>①</sup>descendants shall endure forever,<br>And his throne as the sun before Me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**89:37** It shall be established forever like the moon,<br>And a witness in the sky is faithful.” <i>Selah</i>  
**89:38** But You have rejected and refused,<br>You have been full of wrath <sup>①</sup>against Your anointed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>with</i>  
**89:39** You have <sup>①</sup>repudiated the covenant of Your servant;<br>You have profaned his crown <sup>②</sup>in the dust.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., scornfully rejected <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>to the ground</i>  
**89:40** You have broken down all his walls;<br>You have brought his strongholds to ruin.  
**89:41** All who pass along the way plunder him;<br>He has become a disgrace to his neighbors.  
**89:42** You have exalted the right hand of his adversaries;<br>You have made all his enemies rejoice.  
**89:43** You also turn back the edge of his sword,<br>And have not made him stand in battle.  
**89:44** You have put an end to his <sup>①</sup>splendor<br>And cast his throne to the ground.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>clearness, luster</i>  
**89:45** You have shortened the days of his youth;<br>You have covered him with shame. <i>Selah</i>  
**89:46** How long, LORD?<br>Will You hide Yourself forever?<br>Will Your wrath burn like fire?  
**89:47** Remember <sup>①</sup>what my lifespan is;<br>For what futility <sup>②</sup>You have created all the sons of mankind!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of what duration I am</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>have You...mankind?</i>  
**89:48** What man can live and not see death?<br>Can he save his soul from the <sup>①</sup>power of <sup>②</sup>Sheol? <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>hand</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**89:49** Where are Your former acts of favor, Lord,<br><i>Which</i> You swore to David in Your faithfulness?  
**89:50** Remember, Lord, the taunt against Your servants;<br> <sup>①</sup>How I carry in my heart <i>the taunts of</i> all the many peoples,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>My bearing in my chest</i>  
**89:51** With which Your enemies have taunted, LORD,<br>With which they have taunted the footsteps of Your anointed.  
**89:52** Blessed be the LORD forever!<br>Amen and Amen.  

## 90장

**90:1** [A Prayer of <sup>①</sup>Moses, the man of God.]<br>Lord, You have been our <sup>②</sup>dwelling place in all generations.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Deut 33:1 <sup>②</sup>Or <i>hiding place;</i> some ancient mss <i>place of refuge</i>  
**90:2** Before the mountains were born<br> <sup>①</sup>Or You gave birth to the earth and the world,<br>Even from everlasting to everlasting, You are God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>And</i>  
**90:3** You turn mortals back into dust<br>And say, “Return, you sons of mankind.”  
**90:4** For a thousand years in Your sight<br>Are like yesterday when it passes by,<br> <sup>①</sup>Or <i>like</i> a watch in the night.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>And</i>  
**90:5** You have <sup>①</sup>swept them away like a flood, they <sup>②</sup>fall asleep;<br>In the morning they are like grass that <sup>③</sup>sprouts anew.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>flooded</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>become asleep</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>passes away</i>  
**90:6** In the morning it flourishes and <sup>①</sup>sprouts anew;<br>Toward evening it wilts and withers away.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>passes away</i>  
**90:7** For we have been consumed by Your anger,<br>And we have been terrified by Your wrath.  
**90:8** You have placed our guilty deeds before You,<br>Our hidden <i>sins</i> in the light of Your presence.  
**90:9** For all our days have dwindled away in Your fury;<br>We have finished our years like a <sup>①</sup>sigh.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>whisper</i>  
**90:10** As for the days of our <sup>①</sup>life, <sup>②</sup>they contain seventy years,<br>Or if due to strength, eighty years,<br>Yet their pride is <i>only</i> trouble and tragedy;<br>For it quickly passes, and we disappear.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>years</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>in them are</i>  
**90:11** Who <sup>①</sup>understands the power of Your anger<br>And Your fury, according to the fear <sup>②</sup>that is due You?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>knows</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>of You</i>  
**90:12** So teach <i>us</i> to number our days,<br>That we may <sup>①</sup>present <i>to You</i> a heart of wisdom.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>bring in</i>  
**90:13** Do return, LORD; how long <i>will it be?</i><br>And <sup>①</sup>be sorry for Your servants.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>repent in regard to</i>  
**90:14** Satisfy us in the morning with Your graciousness,<br>That we may sing for joy and rejoice all our days.  
**90:15** Make us glad <sup>①</sup>according to the days You have afflicted us,<br><i>And</i> the years we have seen <sup>②</sup>evil.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>as many days as</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>trouble</i>  
**90:16** Let Your work appear to Your servants<br>And Your majesty <sup>①</sup>to their children.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>upon</i>  
**90:17** May the kindness of the Lord our God be upon us;<br>And <sup>①</sup>confirm for us the work of our hands;<br>Yes, <sup>②</sup>confirm the work of our hands.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>give permanence to</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>give permanence to</i>  

## 91장

**91:1** One who dwells in the shelter of the Most High<br>Will lodge in the shadow of the Almighty.  
**91:2** I will say to the LORD, “My refuge and my fortress,<br>My God, in whom I trust!”  
**91:3** For it is He who rescues you from the net of the trapper<br>And from the deadly plague.  
**91:4** He will cover you with His pinions,<br>And under His wings you may take refuge;<br>His faithfulness is a shield and wall.  
**91:5** You will not be afraid of the terror by night,<br>Or of the arrow that flies by day;  
**91:6** Of the plague that <sup>①</sup>stalks in darkness,<br>Or of the destruction that devastates at noon.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>walks</i>  
**91:7** A thousand may fall at your side<br>And ten thousand at your right hand,<br><i>But</i> it shall not approach you.  
**91:8** You will only look on with your eyes<br>And see the retaliation <i>against</i> the wicked.  
**91:9**  <sup>①</sup>For you have made the LORD, my refuge,<br>The Most High, your dwelling place.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>For You, O LORD</i>, are my Refuge; You have made the Most High your dwelling place  
**91:10** No evil will happen to you,<br>Nor will any plague come near your <sup>①</sup>tent.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>dwelling</i>  
**91:11** For He will give His angels orders concerning you,<br>To protect you in all your ways.  
**91:12** On their hands they will lift you up,<br>So that you do not strike your foot against a stone.  
**91:13** You will walk upon the lion and cobra,<br>You will trample the young lion and the <sup>①</sup>serpent.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>dragon</i>  
**91:14** “Because he has loved Me, I will save him;<br>I will set him <i>securely</i> on high, because he has known My name.  
**91:15** He will call upon Me, and I will answer him;<br>I will be with him in <sup>①</sup>trouble;<br>I will rescue him and honor him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>distress</i>  
**91:16** I will satisfy him with <sup>①</sup>a long life,<br>And <sup>②</sup>show him My salvation.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>length of days</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>cause him to feast his eyes on</i>  

## 92장

**92:1** [A Psalm, a Song for the Sabbath day.]<br>It is good to give thanks to the LORD<br>And to sing praises to Your name, Most High;  
**92:2** To declare Your goodness in the morning<br>And Your faithfulness by <sup>①</sup>night,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>nights</i>  
**92:3**  <sup>①</sup>With the ten-stringed lute and <sup>②</sup>with the harp,<br> <sup>③</sup>With resounding music on the lyre.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Upon</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Upon</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>Upon</i>  
**92:4** For You, LORD, have made me joyful by <sup>①</sup>what You have done,<br>I will sing for joy over the works of Your hands.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Your working</i>  
**92:5** How great are Your works, LORD!<br>Your <sup>①</sup>thoughts are very deep.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>purposes</i>  
**92:6** A stupid person has no knowledge,<br>Nor does a foolish person understand this:  
**92:7** When the wicked sprouted up like grass<br>And all who did injustice flourished,<br>It <i>was only</i> that they might be destroyed forevermore.  
**92:8** But You, LORD, are on high forever.  
**92:9** For, behold, Your enemies, LORD,<br>For, behold, Your enemies will perish;<br>All who do injustice will be scattered.  
**92:10** But You have exalted my horn like <i>that of</i> the wild ox;<br>I have <sup>①</sup>been anointed with fresh oil.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>become moist</i>  
**92:11** And my eye has looked at my enemies,<br>My ears hear of the evildoers who rise up against me.  
**92:12** The righteous person will <sup>①</sup>flourish like the palm tree,<br>He will grow like a cedar in Lebanon.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>sprout</i>  
**92:13** Planted in the house of the LORD,<br>They will flourish in the courtyards of our God.  
**92:14** They will still <sup>①</sup>yield fruit in advanced age;<br>They will be <sup>②</sup>full of sap and very green,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>thrive in</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>fat and</i>  
**92:15** To <sup>①</sup>declare that the LORD is just;<br><i>He is</i> my rock, and there is no malice in Him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>show forth</i>  

## 93장

**93:1** The LORD <sup>①</sup>reigns, He is clothed with majesty;<br>The LORD has clothed and encircled Himself with strength.<br>Indeed, the world is <i>firmly</i> established; it will not be moved.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>has assumed kingship</i>  
**93:2** Your throne is established from of old;<br>You are from eternity.  
**93:3** The floods have lifted up, LORD,<br>The floods have lifted up their voice,<br>The floods lift up their pounding waves.  
**93:4** More than the sounds of many waters,<br><i>Than</i> the mighty breakers of the sea,<br>The LORD on high is mighty.  
**93:5** Your testimonies are fully confirmed;<br>Holiness is pleasing to Your house,<br>LORD, <sup>①</sup>forevermore.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for length of days</i>  

## 94장

**94:1** LORD, God of <sup>①</sup>vengeance,<br>God of <sup>②</sup>vengeance, <sup>③</sup>shine forth!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>avenging acts</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>avenging acts</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>has shone forth</i>  
**94:2** Rise up, Judge of the earth,<br>Pay back retribution to the proud.  
**94:3** How long, LORD, shall the wicked—<br>How long shall the wicked triumph?  
**94:4** They pour out <i>words,</i> they speak arrogantly;<br>All who do injustice boast.  
**94:5** They crush Your people, LORD,<br>And afflict Your inheritance.  
**94:6** They kill the widow and the stranger<br>And murder the orphans.  
**94:7** They have said, “ <sup>①</sup>The LORD does not see,<br>Nor does the God of Jacob perceive.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**94:8** Pay attention, you stupid ones among the people;<br>And when will you understand, foolish ones?  
**94:9** He who planted the ear, <sup>①</sup>does He not hear?<br>Or He who formed the eye, <sup>②</sup>does He not see?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>can</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>can</i>  
**94:10** He who <sup>①</sup>disciplines the nations, will He not rebuke,<br>He who teaches mankind knowledge?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>instructs</i>  
**94:11** The LORD knows human thoughts,<br> <sup>①</sup>That they are <i>mere</i> breath.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>For</i>  
**94:12** Blessed is the man whom You discipline, <sup>①</sup>LORD,<br>And whom You teach from Your Law,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**94:13** So that You may grant him relief from the days of adversity,<br>Until a pit is dug for the wicked.  
**94:14** For the LORD will not abandon His people,<br>Nor will He abandon His inheritance.  
**94:15** For <sup>①</sup>judgment <sup>②</sup>will again be righteous,<br>And all the upright in heart <sup>③</sup>will follow it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., administration of justice <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>will return to righteousness</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>will be after it</i>  
**94:16** Who will stand up for me against evildoers?<br>Who will take his stand for me against those who do injustice?  
**94:17** If the LORD had not been my help,<br>My soul would soon have dwelt <i>in the land of</i> silence.  
**94:18** If I should say, “My foot has slipped,”<br>Your faithfulness, LORD, will support me.  
**94:19** When my anxious thoughts <sup>①</sup>multiply within me,<br>Your comfort delights my soul.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>are many</i>  
**94:20** Can a <sup>①</sup>throne of destruction be allied with You,<br>One which devises <sup>②</sup>mischief by decree?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>tribunal</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>trouble, misfortune</i>  
**94:21** They band themselves together against the <sup>①</sup>life of the righteous<br>And condemn <sup>②</sup>the innocent to death.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>soul</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>innocent blood</i>  
**94:22** But the LORD has been my refuge,<br>And my God the rock of my refuge.  
**94:23** He has brought back their injustice upon them,<br>And He will <sup>①</sup>destroy them in their evil;<br>The LORD our God will <sup>②</sup>destroy them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>silence</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>silence</i>  

## 95장

**95:1** Come, let’s sing for joy to the LORD,<br>Let’s shout joyfully to the rock of our salvation.  
**95:2** Let’s come before His presence with a song of thanksgiving,<br>Let’s shout joyfully to Him in songs <i>with instruments.</i>  
**95:3** For the LORD is a great God<br>And a great King above all gods,  
**95:4** In whose hand are the depths of the earth,<br>The peaks of the mountains are also His.  
**95:5**  <sup>①</sup>The sea is His, for it was He who made it,<br>And His hands formed the dry land.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Who has the sea</i>  
**95:6** Come, let’s worship and bow down,<br>Let’s kneel before the LORD our Maker.  
**95:7** For He is our God,<br>And we are the people of His <sup>①</sup>pasture and the sheep of His hand.<br>Today, <sup>②</sup>if you will hear His voice,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>pasturing</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>O that you would obey</i>  
**95:8** Do not harden your hearts as at <sup>①</sup>Meribah,<br>As on the day of <sup>②</sup>Massah in the wilderness,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>place of strife</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>temptation</i>  
**95:9** “When your fathers put Me to the test,<br>They tested Me, though they had seen My work.  
**95:10** For forty years I was disgusted with <i>that</i> generation,<br>And said they are a people who err in their heart,<br>And they do not know My ways.  
**95:11** Therefore I swore in My anger,<br>They certainly shall not enter My rest.”  

## 96장

**96:1** Sing to the LORD a new song;<br>Sing to the LORD, all the earth.  
**96:2** Sing to the LORD, bless His name;<br>Proclaim the good news of His salvation from day to day.  
**96:3** Tell of His glory among the nations,<br>His wonderful deeds among all the peoples.  
**96:4** For great is the LORD, and greatly to be praised;<br>He is to be feared above all gods.  
**96:5** For all the gods of the peoples are <sup>①</sup>idols,<br>But the LORD made the heavens.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>non-existent things</i>  
**96:6** Splendor and majesty are before Him,<br>Strength and beauty are in His sanctuary.  
**96:7**  <sup>①</sup>Ascribe to the LORD, you families of the peoples,<br> <sup>②</sup>Ascribe to the LORD glory and strength.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Give</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Give</i>  
**96:8**  <sup>①</sup>Ascribe to the LORD the glory of His name;<br>Bring an <sup>②</sup>offering and come into His courtyards.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Give</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>meal offering</i>  
**96:9** Worship the LORD in <sup>①</sup>holy attire;<br>Tremble before Him, all the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>the splendor of holiness</i>  
**96:10** Say among the nations, “The LORD reigns;<br>Indeed, the world is <i>firmly</i> established, it will not be moved;<br>He will judge the peoples fairly.”  
**96:11** May the heavens be joyful, and may the earth rejoice;<br>May the sea <sup>①</sup>roar, and <sup>②</sup>all it contains;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>thunder</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>its fullness</i>  
**96:12** May the field be jubilant, and all that is in it.<br>Then all the trees of the forest will sing for joy  
**96:13** Before the LORD, for He is coming,<br>For He is coming to judge the earth.<br>He will judge the world in righteousness,<br>And the peoples in His faithfulness.  

## 97장

**97:1** The LORD <sup>①</sup>reigns, may the earth rejoice;<br>May the many <sup>②</sup>islands be joyful.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>has assumed Kingship</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>coastlands</i>  
**97:2** Clouds and thick darkness surround Him;<br>Righteousness and justice are the foundation of His throne.  
**97:3** Fire goes before Him<br>And burns up His enemies all around.  
**97:4** His lightning lit up the world;<br>The earth saw <i>it</i> and trembled.  
**97:5** The mountains melted like wax at the presence of the LORD,<br>At the presence of the Lord of the whole earth.  
**97:6** The heavens declare His righteousness,<br>And all the peoples have seen His glory.  
**97:7** May all those be ashamed who serve carved images,<br>Who boast in idols;<br> <sup>①</sup>Worship Him, all you <sup>②</sup>gods.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>All the gods have worshiped Him</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>supernatural powers</i>  
**97:8** Zion <sup>①</sup>heard <i>this</i> and was joyful,<br>And the daughters of Judah have rejoiced<br>Because of Your judgments, LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or possibly <i>hears and is glad</i>  
**97:9** For You are the LORD Most High over all the earth;<br>You are exalted far above all <sup>①</sup>gods.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>supernatural powers</i>  
**97:10** Hate evil, you who love the LORD,<br>Who watches over the souls of His godly ones;<br>He saves them from the hand of the wicked.  
**97:11** Light is sown <i>like seed</i> for the righteous,<br>And gladness for the upright in heart.  
**97:12** Be joyful in the LORD, you righteous ones,<br>And praise <sup>①</sup>the mention of His holy name.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for the memory of His holiness</i>  

## 98장

**98:1** [A Psalm.]<br>Sing a new song to the LORD,<br>For He has done wonderful things,<br>His right hand and His holy arm have <sup>①</sup>gained the victory for Him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>accomplished salvation</i>  
**98:2** The LORD has made His salvation known;<br>He has revealed His righteousness in the sight of the nations.  
**98:3** He has remembered His graciousness and His faithfulness to the house of Israel;<br>All the ends of the earth have seen the salvation of our God.  
**98:4** Shout joyfully to the LORD, all the earth;<br>Be cheerful and sing for joy and sing praises.  
**98:5** Sing praises to the LORD with the lyre,<br>With the lyre and the <sup>①</sup>sound of melody.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>voice of song</i> (accompanied by music)  
**98:6** With trumpets and the sound of the horn<br>Shout joyfully before the King, the LORD.  
**98:7** May the sea roar and <sup>①</sup>all it contains,<br>The world and those who dwell in it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>its fullness</i>  
**98:8** May the rivers clap their hands,<br>May the mountains sing together for joy  
**98:9** Before the LORD, for He is coming to judge the earth;<br>He will judge the world with righteousness<br>And the peoples with fairness.  

## 99장

**99:1** The LORD reigns, <sup>①</sup>the peoples tremble!<br>He sits <i>enthroned above</i> the cherubim, <sup>②</sup>the earth quakes!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>let the</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>let the...quake</i>  
**99:2** The LORD <sup>①</sup>is great in Zion,<br>And He is exalted above all the peoples.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>in Zion is great</i>  
**99:3** May they praise Your great and awesome name;<br>Holy is <sup>①</sup>He.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>it</i>  
**99:4** The <sup>①</sup>strength of the King loves <sup>②</sup>justice;<br>You have established <sup>③</sup>order;<br>You have executed <sup>④</sup>justice and righteousness in Jacob.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>You have established in equity the strength of the King who loves justice</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>judgment</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>uprightness</i> <sup>④</sup>Or <i>judgment</i>  
**99:5**  <sup>①</sup>Exalt the LORD our God<br>And worship at His footstool;<br>Holy is He.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>The verb is plural  
**99:6** Moses and Aaron were among His priests,<br>And Samuel was among those who called on His name;<br>They called upon the LORD and He answered them.  
**99:7** He spoke to them in the pillar of cloud;<br>They kept His testimonies<br>And the statute that He gave them.  
**99:8** LORD our God, You answered them;<br>You were a forgiving God to them,<br>And <i>yet</i> an avenger of their <i>evil</i> deeds.  
**99:9** Exalt the LORD our God<br>And worship at His holy hill,<br>For the LORD our God is holy.  

## 100장

**100:1** [A Psalm for <sup>①</sup>Thanksgiving.]<br>Shout joyfully to the LORD, all the earth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or thank offering  
**100:2** Serve the LORD with jubilation;<br>Come before Him with rejoicing.  
**100:3** Know that the LORD <sup>①</sup>Himself is God;<br>It is He who has made us, and <sup>②</sup>not we ourselves;<br><i>We are</i> His people and the sheep of His pasture.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>He</i> <sup>②</sup>Some mss <i>His we are</i>  
**100:4** Enter His gates with <sup>①</sup>thanksgiving,<br><i>And</i> His courtyards with praise.<br>Give thanks to Him, bless His name.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>a thank offering</i>  
**100:5** For the LORD is good;<br>His mercy is everlasting<br>And His faithfulness is to all generations.  

## 101장

**101:1** [A Psalm of David.]<br>I will sing of mercy and <sup>①</sup>justice;<br>To You, LORD, I will sing praises.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>judgment</i>  
**101:2** I will carefully attend to the <sup>①</sup>blameless way.<br>When will You come to me?<br>I will walk within my house in the <sup>②</sup>integrity of my heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>way of integrity</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>blamelessness</i>  
**101:3** I will set no worthless thing before my eyes;<br>I hate the <sup>①</sup>work of those who fall away;<br>It shall not cling to me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>practice of apostasy</i>  
**101:4** A perverse heart shall leave me;<br>I will know no evil.  
**101:5** Whoever secretly slanders his neighbor, him I will <sup>①</sup>destroy;<br>I will not endure one who has a haughty look and an arrogant heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>silence</i>  
**101:6** My eyes shall be upon the faithful of the land, that they may dwell with me;<br>One who walks in a <sup>①</sup>blameless way is one who will serve me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>way of integrity</i>  
**101:7** One who practices deceit shall not dwell within my house;<br>One who speaks lies shall not <sup>①</sup>maintain his position before me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>be established before my eyes</i>  
**101:8** Every morning I will <sup>①</sup>destroy all the wicked of the land,<br>So as to eliminate from the city of the LORD all those who do injustice.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>silence</i>  

## 102장

**102:1** [A Prayer of the afflicted when he is weak and <sup>①</sup>pours out his complaint before the LORD.]<br>Hear my prayer, LORD!<br>And let my cry for help come to You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Ps 142:2  
**102:2** Do not hide Your face from me on the day of my distress;<br>Incline Your ear to me;<br>On the day when I call answer me quickly.  
**102:3** For my days have ended in smoke,<br>And my bones have been scorched like a hearth.  
**102:4** My heart has been struck like <sup>①</sup>grass and has withered,<br>Indeed, I forget to eat my bread.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>herbage</i>  
**102:5** Because of the <sup>①</sup>loudness of my groaning<br>My bones <sup>②</sup>cling to my flesh.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>voice</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>have cleaved</i>  
**102:6** I resemble a pelican of the wilderness;<br>I have become like an owl of the ruins.  
**102:7** I lie awake,<br>I have become like a solitary bird on a housetop.  
**102:8** My enemies have taunted me all day long;<br>Those who <sup>①</sup>deride me <sup>②</sup>have used my <i>name</i> as a curse.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>have sworn by me</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>made a fool of</i>  
**102:9** For I have eaten ashes like bread,<br>And mixed my drink with weeping  
**102:10** Because of Your indignation and Your wrath;<br>For You have lifted me up and thrown me away.  
**102:11** My days are like a <sup>①</sup>lengthened shadow,<br>And <sup>②</sup>I wither away like <sup>③</sup>grass.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>stretched out</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>as for me, I</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>herbage</i>  
**102:12** But You, LORD, <sup>①</sup>remain forever,<br>And Your <sup>②</sup>name <i>remains</i> to all generations.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>sit enthroned</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>memorial</i>  
**102:13** You will arise <i>and</i> have compassion on Zion;<br>For it is time to be gracious to her,<br>For the appointed time has come.  
**102:14** Surely Your servants <sup>①</sup>take pleasure in her stones,<br>And feel pity for her dust.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>have found</i>  
**102:15**  <sup>①</sup>So the <sup>②</sup>nations will fear the name of the LORD,<br>And all the kings of the earth, Your glory.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>And</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>Gentiles, heathen</i>  
**102:16** For the LORD has built up Zion;<br>He has appeared in His glory.  
**102:17** He has turned His attention to the prayer of the <sup>①</sup>destitute<br>And has not despised their prayer.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>naked</i>  
**102:18**  <sup>①</sup>This will be written for the generation to come,<br> <sup>②</sup>That a people yet to be created <sup>③</sup>may praise <sup>④</sup>the LORD:<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Let this be written</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>And</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>will</i> <sup>④</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**102:19** For He looked down from His holy height;<br>From heaven the LORD looked <sup>①</sup>upon the earth,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>toward</i>  
**102:20** To hear the groaning of the prisoner,<br>To set free <sup>①</sup>those who were doomed to death,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the sons of death</i>  
**102:21** So that <i>people</i> may tell of the name of the LORD in Zion,<br>And His praise in Jerusalem,  
**102:22** When the peoples are gathered together,<br>And the kingdoms, to serve the LORD.  
**102:23** He has broken my strength in the way;<br>He has shortened my days.  
**102:24** I say, “My God, do not take me away in the middle of my days,<br>Your years are <sup>①</sup>throughout all generations.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in generation of generations</i>  
**102:25** In time of old You founded the earth,<br>And the heavens are the work of Your hands.  
**102:26**  <sup>①</sup>Even they will perish, but You endure;<br>All of them will wear out like a garment;<br>Like clothing You will change them and they will pass away.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>They themselves</i>  
**102:27** But You are <sup>①</sup>the same,<br>And Your years will not come to an end.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>He</i>  
**102:28** The children of Your servants will continue,<br>And their <sup>①</sup>descendants will be established before You.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  

## 103장

**103:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>Bless the LORD, my soul,<br>And all that is within me, <i>bless</i> His holy name.  
**103:2** Bless the LORD, my soul,<br>And do not forget any of His benefits;  
**103:3** Who pardons all your guilt,<br>Who heals all your diseases;  
**103:4** Who redeems your life from the pit,<br>Who crowns you with favor and compassion;  
**103:5** Who satisfies your <sup>①</sup>years with good things,<br><i>So that</i> your youth is renewed like the eagle.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>desire</i>  
**103:6** The LORD performs <sup>①</sup>righteous deeds<br>And judgments for all who are oppressed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>deeds of vindication</i>  
**103:7** He made known His ways to Moses,<br>His deeds to the sons of Israel.  
**103:8** The LORD is compassionate and gracious,<br>Slow to anger and abounding in mercy.  
**103:9** He will not always contend <i>with us,</i><br>Nor will He keep <i>His anger</i> forever.  
**103:10** He has not dealt with us according to our sins,<br>Nor rewarded us according to our guilty deeds.  
**103:11** For as high as the heavens are above the earth,<br>So great is His mercy toward those who <sup>①</sup>fear Him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**103:12** As far as the east is from the west,<br>So far has He removed our wrongdoings from us.  
**103:13** Just as a father has compassion on <i>his</i> children,<br>So the LORD has compassion on those who <sup>①</sup>fear Him.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>
