**103:14** For He Himself knows <sup>①</sup>our form;<br>He is mindful that we are <i>nothing but</i> dust.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., what we are made of  
**103:15** As for man, his days are like grass;<br>Like a flower of the field, so he flourishes.  
**103:16** When the wind has passed over it, it is no more,<br>And its place no longer knows about it.  
**103:17** But the <sup>①</sup>mercy of the LORD is from everlasting to everlasting for those who <sup>②</sup>fear Him,<br>And His justice to the children’s children,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**103:18** To those who keep His covenant<br>And remember His precepts, <i>so as</i> to do them.  
**103:19** The LORD has established His throne in the heavens,<br>And His <sup>①</sup>sovereignty rules over <sup>②</sup>all.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>kingdom</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the universe  
**103:20** Bless the LORD, you His angels,<br>Mighty in strength, who perform His word,<br>Obeying the voice of His word!  
**103:21** Bless the LORD, all you His <sup>①</sup>angels,<br>You who serve Him, doing His will.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>hosts</i>  
**103:22** Bless the LORD, all you works of His,<br>In all places of His dominion;<br>Bless the LORD, my soul!  

## 104장

**104:1** Bless the LORD, my soul!<br>LORD my God, You are very great;<br>You are clothed with splendor and majesty,  
**104:2** Covering Yourself with light as with a cloak,<br>Stretching out heaven like a tent curtain.  
**104:3**  <sup>①</sup>He lays the beams of His upper chambers in the waters;<br> <sup>②</sup>He makes the clouds His chariot;<br> <sup>③</sup>He walks on the wings of the wind;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i>  
**104:4**  <sup>①</sup>He makes <sup>②</sup>the winds His messengers,<br> <sup>③</sup>Flaming fire His ministers.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Who</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>His angels, spirits</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>His ministers flames of fire</i>  
**104:5** He established the earth upon its foundations,<br>So that it will not <sup>①</sup>totter forever and ever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>move out of place</i>  
**104:6** You covered it with the deep sea as with a garment;<br>The waters were standing above the mountains.  
**104:7** They fled from Your rebuke,<br>At the sound of Your thunder they hurried away.  
**104:8** The mountains rose; the valleys sank down<br>To the place which You established for them.  
**104:9** You set a boundary <i>so that</i> they will not pass over,<br><i>So that</i> they will not return to cover the earth.  
**104:10**  <sup>①</sup>He sends forth springs in the valleys;<br>They flow between the mountains;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>The one who sends</i>  
**104:11** They give drink to every animal of the field;<br>The wild donkeys quench their thirst.  
**104:12** The birds of the sky dwell <sup>①</sup>beside them;<br>They <sup>②</sup>lift up <i>their</i> voices from among the branches.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>over;</i> or <i>above</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>give forth</i>  
**104:13**  <sup>①</sup>He waters the mountains from His upper chambers;<br>The earth is satisfied with the fruit of His works.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Who</i>  
**104:14**  <sup>①</sup>He causes the grass to grow for the <sup>②</sup>cattle,<br>And vegetation for the labor of mankind,<br>So that <sup>③</sup>they may produce <sup>④</sup>food from the earth,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Who</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>animals</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>He</i> <sup>④</sup>Lit <i>bread</i>  
**104:15** And wine, which makes a human heart cheerful,<br>So that he makes <i>his</i> face gleam with oil,<br>And <sup>①</sup>food, which sustains a human heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>bread</i>  
**104:16** The trees of the LORD <sup>①</sup>drink their fill,<br>The cedars of Lebanon which He planted,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>are satisfied</i>  
**104:17** Where the birds build their nests,<br><i>And</i> the stork, whose home is the juniper trees.  
**104:18** The high mountains are for the wild goats;<br>The cliffs are a refuge for the rock hyrax.  
**104:19** He made the moon for the seasons;<br>The sun knows the place of its setting.  
**104:20** You appoint darkness and it becomes night,<br>In which all the animals of the forest <sup>①</sup>prowl about.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>crawl</i>  
**104:21** The young lions roar for their prey<br> <sup>①</sup>And seek their food from God.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>And to seek</i>  
**104:22** <i>When</i> the sun rises they withdraw,<br>And they lie down in their dens.  
**104:23** A person goes out to his work<br>And to his labor until evening.  
**104:24** LORD, how many are Your works!<br> <sup>①</sup>In wisdom You have made them all;<br>The earth is full of Your <sup>②</sup>possessions.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>With</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>creatures</i>  
**104:25**  <sup>①</sup>There is the sea, great and <sup>②</sup>broad,<br>In which are swarms without number,<br>Animals both small and great.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>This</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>broad of dimensions;</i> lit <i>hands</i>  
**104:26** The ships move along there,<br><i>And</i> <sup>①</sup>Leviathan, which You have formed to have fun in it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>a sea monster</i>  
**104:27** They all wait for You<br>To give them their food in <sup>①</sup>due season.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>its appointed time</i>  
**104:28** You give to them, they gather <i>it</i> up;<br>You open Your hand, they are satisfied with good.  
**104:29** You hide Your face, they are terrified;<br>You take away their breath, they perish<br>And return to their dust.  
**104:30** You send forth Your <sup>①</sup>Spirit, they are created;<br>And You renew the face of the ground.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>breath</i>  
**104:31** May the glory of the LORD endure forever;<br>May the LORD rejoice in His works;  
**104:32**  <sup>①</sup>He looks at the earth, and it trembles;<br>He touches the mountains, and they smoke.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i>  
**104:33**  <sup>①</sup>I will sing to the LORD <sup>②</sup>as long as I live;<br> <sup>③</sup>I will sing praise to my God <sup>④</sup>while I have my being.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Let me sing</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>in my lifetime</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>Let me sing</i> <sup>④</sup>Lit <i>while I still am</i>  
**104:34** May my praise be pleasing to Him;<br>As for me, I shall rejoice in the LORD.  
**104:35** May sinners be removed from the earth<br>And <i>may</i> the wicked be no more.<br>Bless the LORD, my soul.<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 105장

**105:1** Give thanks to the LORD, call upon His name;<br>Make His deeds known among the peoples.  
**105:2** Sing to Him, sing praises to Him;<br> <sup>①</sup>Tell of all His <sup>②</sup>wonders.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Meditate on</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., wonderful acts  
**105:3** Boast in His holy name;<br>May the heart of those who seek the LORD be joyful.  
**105:4** Seek the LORD and His strength;<br>Seek His face continually.  
**105:5** Remember His <sup>①</sup>wonders which He has done,<br>His marvels and the judgments <sup>②</sup>spoken by His mouth,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>of His mouth</i>  
**105:6** You <sup>①</sup>descendants of Abraham, His servant,<br>You sons of Jacob, His chosen ones!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**105:7** He is the LORD our God;<br>His judgments are in all the earth.  
**105:8** He has remembered His covenant forever,<br>The word which He commanded to a thousand generations,  
**105:9** <i>The covenant</i> which He made with Abraham,<br>And His oath to Isaac.  
**105:10** Then He confirmed it to Jacob as a statute,<br>To Israel as an everlasting covenant,  
**105:11** Saying, “To you I will give the land of Canaan<br>As the <sup>①</sup>portion of your inheritance,”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>measuring line</i>  
**105:12** When they were <i>only</i> a few people in number,<br>Very few, and strangers in it.  
**105:13** And they wandered from nation to nation,<br>From <i>one</i> kingdom to another people,  
**105:14** He allowed no one to oppress them,<br>And He <sup>①</sup>rebuked kings for their sakes, <i>saying,</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>punished</i>  
**105:15** “Do not touch My anointed ones,<br>And do not harm My prophets.”  
**105:16** And He called for a famine upon the land;<br>He broke the whole staff of bread.  
**105:17** He sent a man before them,<br>Joseph, <i>who</i> was sold as a slave.  
**105:18** They forced his feet into shackles,<br> <sup>①</sup>He was put in irons;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>His soul came into</i>  
**105:19** Until the time that his word came to pass,<br>The word of the LORD refined him.  
**105:20** The king sent and released him,<br>The ruler of peoples, and set him free.  
**105:21** He made him lord of his house,<br>And ruler over all his possessions,  
**105:22** To <sup>①</sup>imprison his high officials <sup>②</sup>at will,<br>That he might teach his elders wisdom.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>bind</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>at his</i>  
**105:23** Israel also came into Egypt;<br>So Jacob lived in the land of Ham.  
**105:24** And He made His people very fruitful,<br>And made them stronger than their enemies.  
**105:25** He turned their heart to hate His people,<br>To deal cunningly with His servants.  
**105:26** He sent His servant Moses,<br><i>And</i> Aaron, whom He had chosen.  
**105:27** They <sup>①</sup>performed His wondrous acts among them,<br>And miracles in the land of Ham.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>set the words of His signs</i>  
**105:28** He sent darkness and made <i>it</i> dark;<br>And they did not rebel against His words.  
**105:29** He turned their waters into blood,<br>And caused their fish to die.  
**105:30** Their land swarmed with frogs<br><i>Even</i> in the chambers of their kings.  
**105:31** He spoke, and a swarm of flies<br><i>And</i> gnats invaded all their territory.  
**105:32** He <sup>①</sup>gave them hail for rain,<br><i>And</i> flaming fire in their land.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>made their rain hail</i>  
**105:33** He also struck their vines and their fig trees,<br>And smashed the trees of their territory.  
**105:34** He spoke, and locusts came,<br>And creeping locusts, beyond number,  
**105:35** And they ate all the vegetation in their land,<br>And ate the fruit of their ground.  
**105:36** He also fatally struck all the firstborn in their land,<br>The first fruits of all their vigor.  
**105:37** Then He brought <sup>①</sup>the Israelites out with silver and gold,<br>And among His tribes there was not one who stumbled.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>them</i>  
**105:38** Egypt was glad when they departed,<br>For the dread of them had fallen upon <sup>①</sup>the Egyptians.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>them</i>  
**105:39** He spread out a cloud as a <sup>①</sup>covering,<br>And fire to illumine by night.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>curtain</i>  
**105:40**  <sup>①</sup>They asked, and He brought quail,<br>And satisfied them with the <sup>②</sup>bread of heaven.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>One</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>food</i>  
**105:41** He opened the <sup>①</sup>rock and water flowed out;<br> <sup>②</sup>It ran in the dry places <i>like</i> a river.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>boulder</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>They went</i>  
**105:42** For He remembered His holy word<br><i>With</i> His servant Abraham;  
**105:43** And He led out His people with joy,<br>His chosen ones with a joyful shout.  
**105:44** He also gave them the lands of the <sup>①</sup>nations,<br>So that they might take possession of <i>the fruit of</i> the peoples’ labor,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Gentiles</i>  
**105:45** <i>And</i> that they might keep His statutes<br>And comply with His laws;<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 106장

**106:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>Oh give thanks to the LORD, for He is good;<br>For His mercy is everlasting.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**106:2** Who can speak of the mighty deeds of the LORD,<br>Or can proclaim all His praise?  
**106:3** How blessed are those who maintain <sup>①</sup>justice,<br> <sup>②</sup>Who practice righteousness at all times!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>judgment</i> <sup>②</sup>Many Heb mss <i>The one who performs</i>  
**106:4** Remember me, LORD, in <i>Your</i> favor <sup>①</sup>toward Your people.<br>Visit me with Your salvation,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of</i>  
**106:5** So that I may see the prosperity of Your chosen ones,<br>That I may rejoice in the joy of Your nation,<br>That I may boast with Your <sup>①</sup>inheritance.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., people  
**106:6** We have sinned <sup>①</sup>like our fathers,<br>We have gone astray, we have behaved wickedly.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>with</i>  
**106:7** Our fathers in Egypt did not understand Your <sup>①</sup>wonders;<br>They did not remember <sup>②</sup>Your abundant kindnesses,<br>But rebelled by the sea, at the <sup>③</sup>Red Sea.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>the multitude of Your kindnesses</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>Sea of Reeds</i>  
**106:8** Nevertheless He saved them for the sake of His name,<br>So that He might make His power known.  
**106:9** So He rebuked the <sup>①</sup>Red Sea and it dried up,<br>And He led them through the mighty waters, as <i>through</i> the wilderness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Sea of Reeds</i>  
**106:10** So He saved them from the <sup>①</sup>hand of one who hated <i>them,</i><br>And redeemed them from the <sup>②</sup>hand of the enemy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>power</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>power</i>  
**106:11** The waters covered their adversaries;<br>Not one of them was left.  
**106:12** Then they believed His words;<br>They sang His praise.  
**106:13** They quickly forgot His works;<br>They did not wait for His plan,  
**106:14** But became lustfully greedy in the wilderness,<br>And put God to the test in the desert.  
**106:15** So He gave them their request,<br>But sent a <sup>①</sup>wasting disease among them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>leanness into their soul</i>  
**106:16** When they became envious of Moses in the camp,<br>And of Aaron, the holy one of the LORD,  
**106:17** The earth opened and swallowed up Dathan,<br>And engulfed the <sup>①</sup>company of Abiram.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>assembly, band</i>  
**106:18** And a fire blazed up in their <sup>①</sup>company;<br>The flame consumed the wicked.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>assembly, band</i>  
**106:19** They made a calf in Horeb,<br>And worshiped a cast metal image.  
**106:20** So they exchanged their glory<br>For the image of an ox that eats grass.  
**106:21** They forgot God their Savior,<br>Who had done great things in Egypt,  
**106:22**  <sup>①</sup>Wonders in the land of Ham,<br><i>And</i> awesome things by the <sup>②</sup>Red Sea.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., Wonderful acts <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Sea of Reeds</i>  
**106:23** Therefore He said that He would destroy them,<br>If Moses, His chosen one, had not stood in the gap before Him,<br>To turn away His wrath from destroying <i>them.</i>  
**106:24** Then they rejected the pleasant land;<br>They did not believe His word,  
**106:25** But grumbled in their tents;<br>They did not listen to the voice of the LORD.  
**106:26** Therefore He <sup>①</sup>swore to them<br>That He would have them fall in the wilderness,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>lifted up His hand</i>  
**106:27** And that He would bring down their <sup>①</sup>descendants among the nations,<br>And scatter them in the lands.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i>  
**106:28** They also followed <sup>①</sup>Baal-peor,<br>And ate sacrifices <i>offered to</i> the dead.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Baal of Peor</i>  
**106:29** So they provoked <i>Him</i> to anger with their deeds,<br>And a plague broke out among them.  
**106:30** Then Phinehas stood up and <sup>①</sup>intervened,<br>And so the plague was brought to a halt.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>pronounced judgment</i>  
**106:31** And it was credited to him as righteousness,<br>To all generations forever.  
**106:32** They also provoked <i>Him</i> to wrath at the waters of <sup>①</sup>Meribah,<br>So that it went badly for Moses on their account.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>strife</i>  
**106:33** Because they were rebellious against <sup>①</sup>His Spirit,<br>He spoke rashly with his lips.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>his spirit</i>  
**106:34** They did not destroy the peoples,<br>As the LORD had commanded them,  
**106:35** But they got involved with the nations<br>And learned their <sup>①</sup>practices,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>works</i>  
**106:36** And served their idols,<br>Which became a snare to them.  
**106:37** They even sacrificed their sons and their daughters to the demons,  
**106:38** And shed innocent blood,<br>The blood of their sons and their daughters<br>Whom they sacrificed to the idols of Canaan;<br>And the land was defiled with the blood.  
**106:39** So they became unclean in their <sup>①</sup>practices,<br>And <sup>②</sup>were unfaithful in their deeds.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>works</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>prostituted themselves</i>  
**106:40** Therefore the anger of the LORD was kindled against His people,<br>And He loathed His <sup>①</sup>inheritance.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., people  
**106:41** So He handed them over to the <sup>①</sup>nations,<br>And those who hated them ruled over them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Gentiles</i>  
**106:42** Their enemies also oppressed them,<br>And they were subdued under their <sup>①</sup>power.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>hand</i>  
**106:43** Many times He would rescue them;<br>They, however, were rebellious in their plan,<br>And they sank down into their guilt.  
**106:44** Nevertheless He looked at their distress<br>When He heard their cry;  
**106:45** And He remembered His covenant for their sake,<br>And <sup>①</sup>relented according to the greatness of His mercy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>was sorry</i>  
**106:46** He also made them <i>objects</i> of compassion<br>In the presence of all their captors.  
**106:47** Save us, LORD our God,<br>And gather us from the nations,<br>To give thanks to Your holy name<br>And glory in Your praise.  
**106:48** Blessed be the LORD, the God of Israel,<br>From everlasting to everlasting.<br>And all the people shall say, “Amen.”<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 107장

**107:1** Give thanks to the LORD, for He is good,<br>For His mercy is everlasting.  
**107:2** The redeemed of the LORD shall say <i>so,</i><br><i>Those</i> whom He has redeemed from the hand of the enemy  
**107:3** And gathered from the lands,<br>From the east and from the west,<br>From the north and from the <sup>①</sup>south.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>sea</i>  
**107:4** They wandered in the wilderness in a <sup>①</sup>desert region;<br>They did not find a way to <sup>②</sup>an inhabited city.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>waste</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>a habitable city;</i> lit <i>a city of habitation</i>  
**107:5** <i>They were</i> hungry <sup>①</sup>and thirsty;<br>Their souls felt weak within them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>also</i>  
**107:6** Then they cried out to the LORD in their trouble;<br>He saved them from their distresses.  
**107:7** He also had them walk on a <sup>①</sup>straight way,<br>To go to <sup>②</sup>an inhabited city.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>level</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>a habitable city;</i> lit <i>a city of habitation</i>  
**107:8** They shall give thanks to the LORD for His mercy,<br>And for His <sup>①</sup>wonders to the sons of mankind!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts  
**107:9** For He has satisfied the <sup>①</sup>thirsty soul,<br>And He has filled the hungry soul with what is good.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>parched</i>  
**107:10** There were those who lived in darkness and in <sup>①</sup>the shadow of death,<br>Prisoners in misery and <sup>②</sup>chains,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>deep gloom</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>irons</i>  
**107:11** Because they had rebelled against the words of God<br>And rejected the plan of the Most High.  
**107:12** Therefore He humbled their heart with labor;<br>They stumbled and there was no one to help.  
**107:13** Then they cried out to the LORD in their trouble;<br>He saved them from their distresses.  
**107:14** He brought them out of darkness and <sup>①</sup>the shadow of death<br>And broke their bands apart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>deep darkness</i>  
**107:15** They shall give thanks to the LORD for His mercy,<br>And for His <sup>①</sup>wonders to the sons of mankind!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts  
**107:16** For He has shattered gates of bronze<br>And cut off bars of iron.  
**107:17** Fools, because of <sup>①</sup>their rebellious way,<br>And because of their guilty deeds, were afflicted.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the way of their offense</i>  
**107:18** Their souls loathed all kinds of food,<br>And they came close to the gates of death.  
**107:19** Then they cried out to the LORD in their trouble;<br>He saved them from their distresses.  
**107:20** He sent His word and healed them,<br>And saved <i>them</i> from their <sup>①</sup>destruction.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>pits</i>  
**107:21** They shall give thanks to the LORD for His mercy,<br>And for His <sup>①</sup>wonders to the sons of mankind!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts  
**107:22** They shall also offer sacrifices of thanksgiving,<br>And tell of His works with joyful singing.  
**107:23** Those who go down to the sea in ships,<br>Who do business on great waters;  
**107:24** They have seen the works of the LORD,<br>And His <sup>①</sup>wonders in the deep.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts  
**107:25** For He spoke and raised a stormy wind,<br>Which lifted the waves <sup>①</sup>of the sea.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of it</i>  
**107:26** They rose up to the heavens, they went down to the depths;<br>Their soul melted away in <i>their</i> misery.  
**107:27** They reeled and staggered like a drunken person,<br>And <sup>①</sup>were at their wits’ end.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>all their wisdom was swallowed up</i>  
**107:28** Then they cried out to the LORD in their trouble,<br>And He brought them out of their distresses.  
**107:29** He caused the storm to be still,<br>So that the waves <sup>①</sup>of the sea were hushed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of it</i>  
**107:30** Then they were glad because they were quiet,<br>So He guided them to their desired harbor.  
**107:31** They shall give thanks to the LORD for His mercy,<br>And for His <sup>①</sup>wonders to the sons of mankind!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts  
**107:32** They shall also exalt Him in the congregation of the people,<br>And praise Him at the seat of the elders.  
**107:33** He turns rivers into a <sup>①</sup>wilderness,<br>And springs of water into a thirsty ground;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>desert</i>  
**107:34** <i>And</i> a fruitful land into a salt waste,<br>Because of the wickedness of those who dwell in it.  
**107:35** He turns a <sup>①</sup>wilderness into a pool of water,<br>And a dry land into springs of water;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>desert</i>  
**107:36** And He has the hungry live there,<br>So that they may establish <sup>①</sup>an inhabited city,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>a habitable city;</i> lit <i>a city of habitation</i>  
**107:37** And sow fields and plant vineyards,<br>And <sup>①</sup>gather a fruitful harvest.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>acquire fruits of yield</i>  
**107:38** He also blesses them and they multiply greatly,<br>And He does not let their cattle decrease.  
**107:39** When they become few and lowly<br>Because of oppression, misery, and sorrow,  
**107:40** He pours contempt upon noblemen<br>And makes them wander in a pathless wasteland.  
**107:41** But He sets the needy <sup>①</sup>securely on high, away from affliction,<br>And makes <i>his</i> families like a flock.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in an inaccessibly high place</i>  
**107:42** The upright see it and are glad;<br>But all injustice shuts its mouth.  
**107:43** Who is wise? He is to pay attention to these things,<br>And consider the mercy of the LORD.  

## 108장

**108:1** [A Song, a Psalm of David.]<br>My heart is steadfast, God;<br>I will sing, I will sing praises also with my <sup>①</sup>soul.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>glory</i>  
**108:2** Awake, harp and lyre;<br>I will awaken the dawn!  
**108:3** I will give thanks to You, LORD, among the peoples,<br>And I will sing praises to You among the nations.  
**108:4** For Your mercy is great above the heavens,<br>And Your truth <i>reaches</i> to the skies.  
**108:5** Be exalted above the heavens, God,<br>And <i>may</i> Your glory <i>be</i> above all the earth.  
**108:6** So that Your beloved may be rescued,<br>Save with Your right hand, and answer me!  
**108:7** God has spoken in His <sup>①</sup>holiness:<br>“I will triumph, I will divide up Shechem,<br>And measure out the Valley of Succoth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>sanctuary</i>  
**108:8** Gilead is Mine, Manasseh is Mine;<br>Ephraim also is the <sup>①</sup>helmet of My head;<br>Judah is My <sup>②</sup>scepter.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>protection</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>lawgiver</i>  
**108:9** Moab is My washbowl;<br>I will throw My sandal over Edom;<br>I will shout aloud over Philistia.”  
**108:10** Who will bring me into the fortified city?<br>Who <sup>①</sup>will lead me to Edom?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>has led</i>  
**108:11** God, have You Yourself not rejected us?<br>And will You not go forth with our armies, God?  
**108:12** Give us help against the enemy,<br>For deliverance <sup>①</sup>by man is worthless.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>of</i>  
**108:13**  <sup>①</sup>Through God we will do valiantly,<br>And it is He who will trample down our enemies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>In;</i> or <i>With</i>  

## 109장

**109:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>God of my praise,<br>Do not be silent!  
**109:2** For they have opened a <sup>①</sup>wicked and deceitful mouth against me;<br>They have spoken <sup>②</sup>against me with a lying tongue.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>wicked mouth and the deceitful</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>with</i>  
**109:3** They have also surrounded me with words of hatred,<br>And have fought against me without cause.  
**109:4** In return for my love they act as my accusers;<br>But I am <i>in</i> prayer.  
**109:5** So they have <sup>①</sup>repaid me evil for good,<br>And hatred for my love.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>laid upon me</i>  
**109:6** Appoint a wicked person over him,<br>And may an <sup>①</sup>accuser stand at his right hand.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>adversary, Satan</i>  
**109:7** When he is judged, may he come out guilty,<br>And may his prayer become sin.  
**109:8** May his days be few;<br>May another take his office.  
**109:9** May his children be fatherless,<br>And his wife a widow.  
**109:10** May his children wander about and beg;<br>And may they seek <i>sustenance</i> <sup>①</sup>far from their ruined homes.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>out of their desolate places</i>  
**109:11** May the creditor <sup>①</sup>seize everything that he has,<br>And may strangers plunder the product of his labor.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>ensnare, strike at</i>  
**109:12** May there be none to <sup>①</sup>extend kindness to him,<br>Nor any to be gracious to his fatherless children.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>continue</i>  
**109:13** May his descendants be <sup>①</sup>eliminated;<br>May their name be wiped out in a following generation.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for cutting off</i>  
**109:14** May the guilt of his fathers be remembered <sup>①</sup>before the LORD,<br>And do not let the sin of his mother be wiped out.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to</i>  
**109:15** May they be before the LORD continually,<br>So that He may eliminate their memory from the earth;  
**109:16** Because he did not remember to show mercy,<br>But persecuted the afflicted and needy person,<br>And the despondent in heart, to put <i>them</i> to death.  
**109:17** He also loved cursing, so it came to him;<br>And he did not delight in blessing, so it was far from him.  
**109:18** But he clothed himself with cursing as with his garment,<br>And it entered <sup>①</sup>his body like water,<br>And like oil into his bones.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>his inward parts</i>  
**109:19** May it be to him as a garment with which he covers himself,<br>And as a belt which he constantly wears around himself.  
**109:20** <i>May</i> this <i>be</i> the reward of my accusers from the LORD,<br>And of those who speak evil against my soul.  
**109:21** But You, <sup>①</sup>GOD, the Lord, deal <i>kindly</i> with me for the sake of Your name;<br>Because Your mercy is good, rescue me;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YHWH,</i> usually rendered <i>LORD</i>  
**109:22** For I am afflicted and needy,<br>And <sup>①</sup>my heart is wounded within me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>one has pierced my heart within me</i>  
**109:23** I am passing like a shadow when it lengthens;<br>I am shaken off like the locust.  
**109:24** My knees <sup>①</sup>are weak from fasting,<br>And my flesh has grown lean, without fatness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>totter</i>  
**109:25** I also have become a disgrace to them;<br>When they see me, they shake their head.  
**109:26** Help me, LORD my God;<br>Save me according to Your mercy.  
**109:27**  <sup>①</sup>And may they know that this is Your hand;<br>You, LORD, have done it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>That they may know</i>  
**109:28** They will curse, but You bless;<br>When they arise, they will be ashamed,<br>But Your servant will be glad.  
**109:29**  <sup>①</sup>May my accusers be clothed with dishonor,<br>And <sup>②</sup>may they cover themselves with their own shame as with a robe.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>My accusers will be</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>they will cover</i>  
**109:30** With my mouth I will give thanks abundantly to the LORD;<br>And I will praise Him in the midst of many.  
**109:31** For He stands at the right hand of the needy,<br>To save him from those who judge his soul.  

## 110장

**110:1** [A Psalm of David.]<br>The LORD says to my Lord:<br>“Sit at My right hand<br>Until I make Your enemies a footstool for Your feet.”  
**110:2** The LORD will stretch out Your strong scepter from Zion, <i>saying,</i><br>“Rule in the midst of Your enemies.”  
**110:3** Your people <sup>①</sup>will volunteer freely on the day of Your <sup>②</sup>power;<br>In <sup>③</sup>holy splendor, from the womb of the dawn,<br> <sup>④</sup>Your youth are to You <i>as</i> the dew.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>will be voluntary offerings</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>army</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>the splendor of holiness</i> <sup>④</sup>Or <i>The dew of Your youth is Yours</i>  
**110:4** The LORD has sworn and will not <sup>①</sup>change His mind,<br>“You are a priest forever<br>According to the order of Melchizedek.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>be sorry</i>  
**110:5** The Lord is at Your right hand;<br>He <sup>①</sup>will shatter kings in the day of His wrath.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>has shattered</i>  
**110:6** He will judge among the nations,<br>He <sup>①</sup>will fill <i>them</i> with corpses,<br>He <sup>②</sup>will shatter the <sup>③</sup>chief men over a broad country.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>has filled</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>has shattered</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>head over</i>  
**110:7** He will drink from the brook by the wayside;<br>Therefore He will lift up <i>His</i> head.  

## 111장

**111:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>I will give thanks to the LORD with all <i>my</i> heart,<br>In the company of the upright and in the assembly.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah! I will</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**111:2** Great are the works of the LORD;<br><i>They are</i> <sup>①</sup>studied by all who delight in them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>sought out</i>  
**111:3**  <sup>①</sup>Splendid and majestic is His work,<br>And His righteousness endures forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Splendor and majesty</i>  
**111:4** He has caused His <sup>①</sup>wonders <sup>②</sup>to be remembered;<br>The LORD is gracious and compassionate.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>a memorial</i>  
**111:5** He has given <sup>①</sup>food to those who <sup>②</sup>fear Him;<br>He will remember His covenant forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>prey</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**111:6** He has made known to His people the power of His works,<br>In giving them the inheritance of the nations.  
**111:7** The works of His hands are <sup>①</sup>truth and justice;<br>All His precepts are trustworthy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**111:8** They are upheld forever and ever;<br>They are performed in <sup>①</sup>truth and uprightness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**111:9** He has sent redemption to His people;<br>He has <sup>①</sup>ordained His covenant forever;<br>Holy and <sup>②</sup>awesome is His name.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>commanded</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., inspiring reverence  
**111:10** The <sup>①</sup>fear of the LORD is the beginning of wisdom;<br>All those who <sup>②</sup>follow His commandments have a good understanding;<br>His praise endures forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>reverence for</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>do them</i>  

## 112장

**112:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>Blessed is a person who <sup>③</sup>fears the LORD,<br>Who greatly delights in His commandments.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah! Blessed</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>reveres</i>  
**112:2** His <sup>①</sup>descendants will be mighty <sup>②</sup>on the earth;<br>The generation of the upright will be blessed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>seed</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>in the land</i>  
**112:3** Wealth and riches are in his house,<br>And his righteousness endures forever.  
**112:4** Light shines in the darkness for the upright;<br><i>He is</i> gracious, compassionate, and righteous.  
**112:5** It <i>goes</i> well for a person who is gracious and lends;<br>He will <sup>①</sup>maintain his cause in judgment.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>conduct his affairs with justice</i>  
**112:6** For he will never be shaken;<br>The righteous will be <sup>①</sup>remembered forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for an eternal remembrance</i>  
**112:7** He will not fear bad news;<br>His heart is steadfast, trusting in the LORD.  
**112:8** His heart is firm, he will not fear,<br> <sup>①</sup>But will look <i>with satisfaction</i> on his enemies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>until he looks</i>  
**112:9**  <sup>①</sup>He has given freely to the poor,<br>His righteousness endures forever;<br>His horn will be exalted in honor.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>He has scattered, he has given to...</i>  
**112:10** The wicked will see it and be <sup>①</sup>vexed,<br>He will gnash his teeth and melt away;<br>The desire of the wicked will perish.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>angry</i>  

## 113장

**113:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>Praise <i>Him,</i> you servants of the LORD,<br>Praise the name of the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah! Praise</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**113:2** Blessed be the name of the LORD<br>From this time <i>on</i> and forever.  
**113:3** From <sup>①</sup>the rising of the sun to its setting,<br>The name of the LORD is to be praised.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the east to the west  
**113:4** The LORD is high above all nations;<br>His glory is above the heavens.  
**113:5** Who is like the LORD our God,<br>Who is enthroned on high,  
**113:6** Who looks far down to<br>The heavens and the earth?  
**113:7** He raises the poor from the dust,<br>He lifts the needy from the garbage heap,  
**113:8** To seat <i>them</i> with noblemen,<br>With the noblemen of His people.  
**113:9** He has the infertile woman live in the house<br><i>As</i> a joyful mother of children.<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 114장

**114:1** When Israel went forth from Egypt,<br>The house of Jacob from a people of a foreign language,  
**114:2** Judah became His sanctuary;<br>Israel, His dominion.  
**114:3** The sea looked and fled;<br>The Jordan turned back.  
**114:4** The mountains skipped like rams,<br>The hills, like lambs.  
**114:5** What ails you, sea, that you flee?<br>Jordan, that you turn back?  
**114:6** Mountains, that you skip like rams?<br>Hills, like lambs?  
**114:7** Tremble, earth, before the Lord,<br>Before the God of Jacob,  
**114:8** Who turned the rock into a pool of water,<br>The flint into a fountain of water.  

## 115장

**115:1** Not to us, LORD, not to us,<br>But to Your name give glory,<br>Because of Your mercy, because of Your <sup>①</sup>truth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i>  
**115:2** Why should the nations say,<br>“Where, then, is their God?”  
**115:3** But our God is in the heavens;<br>He does whatever He pleases.  
**115:4** Their idols are silver and gold,<br>The work of human hands.  
**115:5** They have mouths, but they cannot speak;<br>They have eyes, but they cannot see;  
**115:6** They have ears, but they cannot hear;<br>They have noses, but they cannot smell;  
**115:7**  <sup>①</sup>They have hands, but they cannot feel;<br> <sup>②</sup>They have feet, but they cannot walk;<br>They cannot make a sound with their throat.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Their hands</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Their feet</i>  
**115:8** Those who make them <sup>①</sup>will become like them,<br>Everyone who trusts in them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>are like them</i>  
**115:9** Israel, trust in the LORD;<br>He is their help and their shield.  
**115:10** House of Aaron, trust in the LORD;<br>He is their help and their shield.  
**115:11** You who <sup>①</sup>fear the LORD, trust in the LORD;<br>He is their help and their shield.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**115:12** The LORD has been mindful of us; He will bless <i>us.</i><br>He will bless the house of Israel;<br>He will bless the house of Aaron.  
**115:13** He will bless those who <sup>①</sup>fear the LORD,<br>The small together with the great.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**115:14** May the LORD increase you,<br>You and your children.  
**115:15** May you be blessed of the LORD,<br>Maker of heaven and earth.  
**115:16** The heavens are the heavens of the LORD,<br>But the earth He has given to the sons of mankind.  
**115:17** The dead do not praise <sup>①</sup>the LORD,<br>Nor <i>do</i> any who go down into silence;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**115:18** But as for us, we will bless <sup>①</sup>the LORD<br>From this time and forever.<br> <sup>②</sup>Praise <sup>③</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>③</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 116장

**116:1** I love the LORD, because He hears<br>My voice <i>and</i> my pleas.  
**116:2** Because He has inclined His ear to me,<br>Therefore I will call <i>upon Him</i> as long as I live.  
**116:3** The snares of death encompassed me<br>And the <sup>①</sup>terrors of <sup>②</sup>Sheol <sup>③</sup>came upon me;<br>I found distress and sorrow.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>straits</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., the netherworld <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>found me</i>  
**116:4** Then I called upon the name of the LORD:<br>“Please, LORD, save my <sup>①</sup>life!”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>soul</i>  
**116:5** Gracious is the LORD, and righteous;<br>Yes, our God is compassionate.  
**116:6** The LORD watches over the simple;<br>I was brought low, and He saved me.  
**116:7** Return to your rest, my soul,<br>For the LORD has dealt <i>generously</i> with you.  
**116:8** For You have rescued my soul from death,<br>My eyes from tears,<br><i>And</i> my feet from stumbling.  
**116:9** I shall walk before the LORD<br>In the <sup>①</sup>land of the living.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>lands</i>  
**116:10** I believed when I said,<br>“I am greatly afflicted.”  
**116:11** I said in my alarm,<br>“All people are liars.”  
**116:12** What shall I repay to the LORD<br>For all His benefits <sup>①</sup>to me?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>upon</i>  
**116:13** I will lift up the cup of salvation,<br>And call upon the name of the LORD.  
**116:14** I will pay my vows to the LORD;<br><i>May it be</i> in the presence of all His people!  
**116:15** Precious in the sight of the LORD<br>Is the death of His godly ones.  
**116:16** O LORD, <sup>①</sup>I surely am Your slave,<br>I am Your slave, the son of Your female slave,<br>You have unfastened my restraints.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>because I am</i>  
**116:17** I will offer You a sacrifice of thanksgiving,<br>And call upon the name of the LORD.  
**116:18** I will pay my vows to the LORD,<br><i>May it be</i> in the presence of all His people,  
**116:19** In the courtyards of the LORD’S house,<br>In the midst of you, Jerusalem!<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 117장

**117:1** Praise the LORD, all nations;<br>Sing His praises, all peoples!  
**117:2** For His mercy toward us is <sup>①</sup>great,<br>And the <sup>②</sup>truth of the LORD is everlasting.<br> <sup>③</sup>Praise <sup>④</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>superior to us</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>④</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 118장

**118:1** Give thanks to the LORD, for He is good;<br>For His mercy is everlasting.  
**118:2** Let Israel say,<br>“His mercy is everlasting.”  
**118:3** Oh let the house of Aaron say,<br>“His mercy is everlasting.”  
**118:4** Let those who <sup>①</sup>fear the LORD say,<br>“His mercy is everlasting.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**118:5** From <i>my</i> distress I called upon <sup>①</sup>the LORD;<br> <sup>②</sup>The LORD answered me <i>and put me</i> in an open space.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**118:6** The LORD is for me; I will not fear;<br>What can man do to me?  
**118:7** The LORD is for me among those who help me;<br>Therefore I will look <i>with satisfaction</i> on those who hate me.  
**118:8** It is better to take refuge in the LORD<br>Than to trust in people.  
**118:9** It is better to take refuge in the LORD<br>Than to trust in noblemen.  
**118:10** All nations surrounded me;<br>In the name of the LORD I will certainly fend them off.  
**118:11** They surrounded me, yes, they surrounded me;<br>In the name of the LORD I will certainly fend them off.  
**118:12** They surrounded me like bees;<br>They were extinguished like a fire of thorn bushes;<br>In the name of the LORD I will certainly fend them off.  
**118:13** You pushed me violently so that I <sup>①</sup>was falling,<br>But the LORD helped me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>fell</i>  
**118:14**  <sup>①</sup>The LORD is my strength and song,<br>And He has become my salvation.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**118:15** The sound of joyful shouting and salvation is in the tents of the righteous;<br>The right hand of the LORD performs valiantly.  
**118:16** The right hand of the LORD is exalted;<br>The right hand of the LORD performs valiantly.  
**118:17** I will not die, but live,<br>And tell of the works of <sup>①</sup>the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**118:18**  <sup>①</sup>The LORD has disciplined me severely,<br>But He has not turned me over to death.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**118:19** Open the gates of righteousness to me;<br>I will enter through them, I will give thanks to <sup>①</sup>the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**118:20** This is the gate of the LORD;<br>The righteous will enter through it.  
**118:21** I will give thanks to You, for You have answered me,<br>And You have become my salvation.  
**118:22** A stone which the builders rejected<br>Has become the <sup>①</sup>chief cornerstone.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>head of the corner</i>  
**118:23** This came about from the LORD;<br>It is marvelous in our eyes.  
**118:24** This is the day which the LORD has made;<br>Let’s rejoice and be glad in it.  
**118:25** Please, O LORD, do save <i>us;</i><br>Please, O LORD, do send prosperity!  
**118:26** Blessed is the one who comes in the name of the LORD;<br>We have blessed you from the house of the LORD.  
**118:27** The LORD is God, and He has given us light;<br>Bind the festival sacrifice <sup>①</sup>to the horns of the altar with cords.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>unto</i>  
**118:28** You are my God, and I give thanks to You;<br><i>You are</i> my God, I exalt You.  
**118:29** Give thanks to the LORD, for He is good;<br>For His mercy is everlasting.  

## 119장

**119:1** [Aleph] Blessed are those whose way is <sup>①</sup>blameless,<br>Who walk in the Law of the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>complete;</i> or <i>having integrity</i>  
**119:2** Blessed are those who comply with His <sup>①</sup>testimonies,<br><i>And</i> seek Him with all <i>their</i> heart.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>provisions,</i> and so throughout the Psalm  
**119:3** They also do no injustice;<br>They walk in His ways.  
**119:4** You have <sup>①</sup>ordained Your precepts,<br> <sup>②</sup>That we are to keep <i>them</i> diligently.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>commanded</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>To keep</i>  
**119:5** Oh that my ways may be established<br>To keep Your statutes!  
**119:6** Then I will not be ashamed<br>When I look at all Your commandments.  
**119:7** I will give thanks to You with uprightness of heart,<br>When I learn Your righteous judgments.  
**119:8** I will keep Your statutes;<br>Do not utterly abandon me!  
**119:9** [Beth] How can a young man keep his way pure?<br>By keeping <i>it</i> according to Your word.  
**119:10** With all my heart I have sought You;<br>Do not let me wander from Your commandments.  
**119:11** I have treasured Your word in my heart,<br>So that I may not sin against You.  
**119:12** Blessed are You, LORD;<br>Teach me Your statutes.  
**119:13** With my lips I have told of<br>All the ordinances of Your mouth.  
**119:14** I have rejoiced in the way of Your testimonies,<br> <sup>①</sup>As much as in all riches.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>As over all</i>  
**119:15** I will meditate on Your precepts<br>And <sup>①</sup>regard Your ways.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>look at</i>  
**119:16** I shall <sup>①</sup>delight in Your statutes;<br>I will not forget Your word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>delight myself</i>  
**119:17** [Gimel] Deal <i>generously</i> with Your servant,<br>That I may live and keep Your word.  
**119:18** Open my eyes, that I may behold<br>Wonderful things from Your Law.  
**119:19** I am a stranger on the earth;<br>Do not hide Your commandments from me.  
**119:20** My soul is crushed <sup>①</sup>with longing<br>For Your ordinances at all times.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>for</i>  
**119:21** You rebuke the arrogant, <sup>①</sup>the cursed,<br>Who wander from Your commandments.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Cursed are those who wander...</i>  
**119:22** Take disgrace and contempt away from me,<br>For I comply with Your testimonies.  
**119:23** Even though rulers sit <i>and</i> speak against me,<br>Your servant meditates on Your statutes.  
**119:24** Your testimonies also are my delight;<br><i>They are</i> <sup>①</sup>my advisers.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the men of my counsel</i>  
**119:25** [Daleth] My soul clings to the dust;<br>Revive me according to Your word.  
**119:26** I have told of my ways, and You have answered me;<br>Teach me Your statutes.  
**119:27** Make me understand the way of Your precepts,<br>And I will meditate on Your wonders.  
**119:28** My soul <sup>①</sup>weeps because of grief;<br>Strengthen me according to Your word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>drops</i>  
**119:29** Remove the false way from me,<br>And graciously grant me Your Law.  
**119:30** I have chosen the faithful way;<br>I have <sup>①</sup>placed Your judgments <i>before me.</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>accounted Your ordinances worthy</i>  
**119:31** I cling to Your testimonies;<br>LORD, do not put me to shame!  
**119:32** I shall run the way of Your commandments,<br>For You will enlarge my heart.  
**119:33** [He] Teach me, the way of Your statutes, LORD,<br>And I shall comply with it to the end.  
**119:34** Give me understanding, so that I may comply with Your Law<br>And keep it with all <i>my</i> heart.  
**119:35** Make me walk in the path of Your commandments,<br>For I delight in it.  
**119:36** Incline my heart to Your testimonies,<br>And not to <i>dishonest</i> gain.  
**119:37** Turn my eyes away from looking at what is worthless,<br>And revive me in Your ways.  
**119:38** Establish Your <sup>①</sup>word to Your servant<br> <sup>②</sup>As that which produces reverence for You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Which is for the fear of You</i>  
**119:39** Take away my disgrace which I dread,<br>For Your judgments are good.  
**119:40** Behold, I long for Your precepts;<br>Revive me through Your righteousness.  
**119:41** [Vav] May Your favor also come to me, LORD,<br>Your salvation according to Your <sup>①</sup>word;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:42** So that I will have an answer for one who taunts me,<br>For I trust in Your word.  
**119:43** And do not take the word of truth utterly out of my mouth,<br>For I <sup>①</sup>wait for Your judgments.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>hope in</i>  
**119:44** So I will keep Your Law continually,<br>Forever and ever.  
**119:45** And I will walk <sup>①</sup>at liberty,<br>For I seek Your precepts.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in a wide place</i>  
**119:46** I will also speak of Your testimonies before kings<br>And shall not be ashamed.  
**119:47** I will delight in Your commandments,<br>Which I love.  
**119:48** And I shall lift up my hands to Your commandments,<br>Which I love;<br>And I will meditate on Your statutes.  
**119:49** [Zayin] Remember the word to Your servant,<br> <sup>①</sup>In which You have made me hope.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>On</i>  
**119:50** This is my comfort in my misery,<br>That Your word has <sup>①</sup>revived me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>preserved me alive</i>  
**119:51** The arrogant utterly deride me,<br><i>Yet</i> I do not turn aside from Your Law.  
**119:52** I have remembered Your judgments from <sup>①</sup>of old, LORD,<br>And comfort myself.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>everlasting</i>  
**119:53** Burning indignation has seized me because of the wicked,<br>Who abandon Your Law.  
**119:54** Your statutes are my songs<br>In the house of my pilgrimage.  
**119:55** LORD, I remember Your name in the night,<br>And keep Your Law.  
**119:56** This has become mine,<br> <sup>①</sup>That I comply with Your precepts.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Because</i>  
**119:57** [Heth] The LORD is my portion;<br>I have <sup>①</sup>promised to keep Your words.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>said that I would keep</i>  
**119:58** I sought Your favor with all <i>my</i> heart;<br>Be gracious to me according to Your <sup>①</sup>word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:59** I considered my ways<br>And turned my feet to Your testimonies.  
**119:60** I hurried and did not delay<br>To keep Your commandments.  
**119:61** The snares of the wicked have surrounded me,<br><i>But</i> I have not forgotten Your Law.  
**119:62** At midnight I will rise to give thanks to You<br>Because of Your righteous judgments.  
**119:63** I am a companion to all those who <sup>①</sup>fear You,<br>And to those who keep Your precepts.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**119:64** The earth is full of Your goodness, LORD;<br>Teach me Your statutes.  
**119:65** [Teth] You have treated Your servant well,<br>LORD, according to Your word.  
**119:66** Teach me good <sup>①</sup>discernment and knowledge,<br>For I believe in Your commandments.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>judgment</i>  
**119:67** Before I was afflicted I went astray,<br>But now I keep Your word.  
**119:68** You are good and You do good;<br>Teach me Your statutes.  
**119:69** The arrogant <sup>①</sup>have forged a lie against me;<br>With all <i>my</i> heart I will comply with Your precepts.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>besmear me with lies</i>  
**119:70** Their heart is insensitive, like fat,<br><i>But</i> I delight in Your Law.  
**119:71** It is good for me that I was afflicted,<br>So that I may learn Your statutes.  
**119:72** The Law of Your mouth is better to me<br>Than thousands of gold and silver <i>pieces.</i>  
**119:73** [Yodh] Your hands made me and <sup>①</sup>fashioned me;<br>Give me understanding, so that I may learn Your commandments.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>established</i>  
**119:74** May those who <sup>①</sup>fear You see me and be glad,<br>Because I <sup>②</sup>wait for Your word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>hope in</i>  
**119:75** I know, LORD, that Your judgments are righteous,<br>And that You have afflicted me in faithfulness.  
**119:76** May Your favor <sup>①</sup>comfort me,<br>According to Your <sup>②</sup>word to Your servant.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>be for my comfort</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:77** May Your compassion come to me so that I may live,<br>For Your Law is my delight.  
**119:78** May the arrogant be put to shame, because they lead me astray with a lie;<br><i>But</i> I shall meditate on Your precepts.  
**119:79** May those who <sup>①</sup>fear You turn to me,<br>And those who know Your testimonies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>revere</i>  
**119:80** May my heart be <sup>①</sup>blameless in Your statutes,<br>So that I will not be ashamed.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>complete;</i> or <i>having integrity</i>  
**119:81** [Kaph] My soul languishes for Your salvation;<br>I <sup>①</sup>wait for Your word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>hope in</i>  
**119:82** My eyes fail <i>with longing</i> for Your <sup>①</sup>word,<br> <sup>②</sup>While I say, “When will You comfort me?”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Saying</i>  
**119:83** Though I have become like a wineskin in the smoke,<br>I do not forget Your statutes.  
**119:84** How many are the days of Your servant?<br>When will You execute judgment on those who persecute me?  
**119:85** The arrogant have dug pits for me,<br><i>People</i> who are not in accord with Your Law.  
**119:86** All Your commandments are faithful;<br>They have persecuted me with a lie; help me!  
**119:87** They almost destroyed me on earth,<br>But as for me, I did not abandon Your precepts.  
**119:88** Revive me according to Your faithfulness,<br>So that I may keep the testimony of Your mouth.  
**119:89** [Lamedh] Forever, LORD,<br>Your word stands in heaven.  
**119:90** Your faithfulness <i>continues</i> <sup>①</sup>throughout generations;<br>You established the earth, and it stands.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to</i>  
**119:91** They stand this day by Your ordinances,<br>For all things are Your servants.  
**119:92** If Your Law had not been my delight,<br>Then I would have perished in my misery.  
**119:93** I will never forget Your precepts,<br>For by them You have <sup>①</sup>revived me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>kept me alive</i>  
**119:94** I am Yours, save me;<br>For I have sought Your precepts.  
**119:95** The wicked wait for me to destroy me;<br>I will diligently consider Your testimonies.  
**119:96** I have seen <sup>①</sup>a limit to all perfection;<br>Your commandment is exceedingly broad.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>an end of</i>  
**119:97** [Mem] How I love Your Law!<br>It is my meditation all the day.  
**119:98** Your commandments make me wiser than my enemies,<br>For they are ever <sup>①</sup>mine.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>with me</i>  
**119:99** I have more insight than all my teachers,<br>For Your testimonies are my meditation.  
**119:100** I understand more than those who are old,<br>Because I have complied with Your precepts.  
**119:101** I have restrained my feet from every evil way,<br>So that I may keep Your word.  
**119:102** I have not turned aside from Your judgments,<br>For You Yourself have taught me.  
**119:103** How sweet are Your <sup>①</sup>words to my <sup>②</sup>taste!<br><i>Yes, sweeter</i> than honey to my mouth!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promises</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>palate</i>  
**119:104** From Your precepts I get understanding;<br>Therefore I hate every false way.  
**119:105** [Nun] Your word is a lamp to my feet<br>And a light to my path.  
**119:106** I have sworn and I will confirm it,<br>That I will keep Your righteous judgments.  
**119:107** I am exceedingly afflicted;<br> <sup>①</sup>Revive me, LORD, according to Your word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Keep me alive</i>  
**119:108** Be pleased to accept the <sup>①</sup>voluntary offerings of my mouth, LORD,<br>And teach me Your judgments.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>freewill offerings</i>  
**119:109** My <sup>①</sup>life is continually <sup>②</sup>in my hand,<br>Yet I do not forget Your Law.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>soul</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., in danger  
**119:110** The wicked have set a <sup>①</sup>trap for me,<br>Yet I have not wandered from Your precepts.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>trapping net</i>  
**119:111** I have inherited Your testimonies forever,<br>For they are the joy of my heart.  
**119:112** I have inclined my heart to perform Your statutes<br>Forever, <i>even</i> to the end.  
**119:113** [Samekh] I hate those who are double-minded,<br>But I love Your Law.  
**119:114** You are my hiding place and my shield;<br>I <sup>①</sup>wait for Your word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>hope in</i>  
**119:115** Leave me, you evildoers,<br>So that I may comply with the commandments of my God.  
**119:116** Sustain me according to Your <sup>①</sup>word, that I may live;<br>And do not let me be <sup>②</sup>ashamed of my hope.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>put to shame because of</i>  
**119:117** Sustain me so that I may be safe,<br>That I may have regard for Your statutes continually.  
**119:118** You have <sup>①</sup>rejected all those who stray from Your statutes,<br>For their deceitfulness is <sup>②</sup>useless.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>made light of</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>falsehood</i>  
**119:119** You have <sup>①</sup>removed all the wicked of the earth <i>like</i> <sup>②</sup>impurities;<br>Therefore I love Your testimonies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>caused to cease</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>dross</i>  
**119:120** My flesh <sup>①</sup>trembles from the fear of You,<br>And I am afraid of Your judgments.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>bristles up from</i>  
**119:121** [Ayin] I have done justice and righteousness;<br>Do not leave me to my oppressors.  
**119:122** Be a guarantor for Your servant for good;<br>Do not let the arrogant oppress me.  
**119:123** My eyes fail <i>with longing</i> for Your salvation,<br>And for Your righteous <sup>①</sup>word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:124** Deal with Your servant according to Your graciousness,<br>And teach me Your statutes.  
**119:125** I am Your servant; give me understanding,<br>So that I may know Your testimonies.  
**119:126** It is time for the LORD to act,<br><i>For</i> they have broken Your Law.  
**119:127** Therefore I love Your commandments<br>Above gold, yes, above pure gold.  
**119:128** Therefore I carefully follow all <i>Your</i> precepts concerning everything,<br>I hate every false way.  
**119:129** [Pe] Your testimonies are wonderful;<br>Therefore my soul complies with them.  
**119:130** The unfolding of Your words gives light;<br>It gives understanding to the simple.  
**119:131** I opened my mouth wide and panted,<br>For I longed for Your commandments.  
**119:132** Turn to me and be gracious to me,<br>As is right for those who love Your name.  
**119:133** Establish my footsteps in Your <sup>①</sup>word,<br>And do not let any <sup>②</sup>wrongdoing have power over me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>injustice</i>  
**119:134** Redeem me from oppression by man,<br>So that I may keep Your precepts.  
**119:135** Make Your face shine upon Your servant,<br>And teach me Your statutes.  
**119:136** My eyes <sup>①</sup>shed streams of water,<br>Because they do not keep Your Law.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>run down</i>  
**119:137** [Tsadhe] You are righteous, LORD,<br>And Your judgments are right.  
**119:138** You have commanded Your testimonies in righteousness<br>And great faithfulness.  
**119:139** My zeal has <sup>①</sup>consumed me,<br>Because my enemies have forgotten Your words.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>put an end to</i>  
**119:140** Your word is very <sup>①</sup>pure,<br>Therefore Your servant loves it.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>refined</i>  
**119:141** I am small and despised,<br><i>Yet</i> I do not forget Your precepts.  
**119:142** Your righteousness is an everlasting righteousness,<br>And Your Law is truth.  
**119:143** Trouble and anguish have <sup>①</sup>come upon me,<br><i>Yet</i> Your commandments are my delight.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>found me</i>  
**119:144** Your testimonies are righteous forever;<br>Give me understanding that I may live.  
**119:145** [Qoph] I cried out with all my heart; answer me, LORD!<br>I will comply with Your statutes.  
**119:146** I cried to You; save me<br>And I shall keep Your testimonies.  
**119:147** I <sup>①</sup>rise before dawn and cry for help;<br>I <sup>②</sup>wait for Your words.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>anticipate the dawn</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>hope in</i>  
**119:148** My eyes anticipate the night watches,<br>So that I may meditate on Your <sup>①</sup>word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:149** Hear my voice according to Your faithfulness;<br>Revive me, LORD, according to Your judgments.  
**119:150** Those who follow after wickedness approach;<br>They are far from Your Law.  
**119:151** You are near, LORD,<br>And all Your commandments are truth.  
**119:152** From long ago I have known from Your testimonies<br>That You have founded them forever.  
**119:153** [Resh] Look at my affliction and rescue me,<br>For I have not forgotten Your Law.  
**119:154** Plead my cause and redeem me;<br>Revive me according to Your <sup>①</sup>word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:155** Salvation is far from the wicked,<br>For they do not seek Your statutes.  
**119:156**  <sup>①</sup>Great are Your mercies, LORD;<br>Revive me according to Your judgments.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Many</i>  
**119:157** Many are my persecutors and my enemies,<br><i>Yet</i> I do not turn aside from Your testimonies.  
**119:158** I see the treacherous and loathe <i>them,</i><br>Because they do not keep Your word.  
**119:159** Consider how I love Your precepts;<br>Revive me, LORD, according to Your faithfulness.  
**119:160** The sum of Your word is truth,<br>And every one of Your righteous judgments is everlasting.  
**119:161** [Shin] Rulers persecute me without cause,<br>But my heart stands in awe of Your words.  
**119:162** I rejoice at Your <sup>①</sup>word,<br>Like one who finds great plunder.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:163** I hate and loathe falsehood,<br><i>But</i> I love Your Law.  
**119:164** Seven times a day I praise You<br>Because of Your righteous judgments.  
**119:165** Those who love Your Law have great peace,<br>And <sup>①</sup>nothing causes them to stumble.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>they have no stumbling block.</i>  
**119:166** I hope for Your salvation, LORD,<br>And do Your commandments.  
**119:167** My soul keeps Your testimonies,<br>And I love them exceedingly.  
**119:168** I keep Your precepts and Your testimonies,<br>For all my ways are before You.  
**119:169** [Tav] Let my cry <sup>①</sup>come before You, LORD;<br>Give me understanding according to Your word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>come near before</i>  
**119:170** Let my pleading come before You;<br>Save me according to Your <sup>①</sup>word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:171** Let my lips pour out praise,<br>For You teach me Your statutes.  
**119:172** Let my tongue sing about Your <sup>①</sup>word,<br>For all Your commandments are righteousness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>promise</i>  
**119:173** Let Your hand be <sup>①</sup>ready to help me,<br>For I have chosen Your precepts.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to help me</i>  
**119:174** I long for Your salvation, LORD,<br>And Your Law is my delight.  
**119:175** Let my soul live that it may praise You,<br>And let Your ordinances help me.  
**119:176** I have wandered about like a lost sheep; search for Your servant,<br>For I do not forget Your commandments.  

## 120장

**120:1** [A Song of <sup>①</sup>Ascents.]<br>I cried to the LORD in my trouble,<br>And He answered me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Ex 34:24; 1 Kin 12:27  
**120:2** Rescue my soul, LORD, from lying lips,<br>From a deceitful tongue.  
**120:3** What will <i>He</i> give to you, and what more will <i>He</i> do to you,<br>You deceitful tongue?  
**120:4** Sharp arrows of the warrior,<br>With the <i>burning</i> coals of the broom tree!  
**120:5** Woe to me, for I reside in Meshech,<br>For I have settled among the tents of Kedar!  
**120:6** Too long has my soul had its dwelling<br>With those who hate peace.  
**120:7** I am <i>for</i> peace, but when I speak,<br>They are for war.  

## 121장

**121:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>I will raise my eyes to the mountains;<br>From where will my help come?  
**121:2** My help <i>comes</i> from the LORD,<br>Who made heaven and earth.  
**121:3** He will not allow your foot to slip;<br>He who watches over you will not slumber.  
**121:4** Behold, He who watches over Israel<br>Will neither slumber nor sleep.  
**121:5** The LORD is your <sup>①</sup>protector;<br>The LORD is your shade on your right hand.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>keeper</i>  
**121:6** The sun will not <sup>①</sup>beat down on you by day,<br>Nor the moon by night.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>strike you</i>  
**121:7** The LORD will <sup>①</sup>protect you from all evil;<br>He will keep your soul.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>keep</i>  
**121:8** The LORD will <sup>①</sup>guard your going out and your coming in<br>From this time and forever.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>keep</i>  

## 122장

**122:1** [A Song of Ascents, of David.]<br>I was glad when they said to me,<br>“Let’s go to the house of the LORD.”  
**122:2** Our feet are standing<br>Within your gates, Jerusalem,  
**122:3** Jerusalem, that has been built<br>As a city that is firmly joined together;  
**122:4** To which the tribes go up, the tribes of <sup>①</sup>the LORD—<br> <sup>②</sup>An ordinance for Israel—<br>To give thanks to the name of the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>A testimony</i>  
**122:5** For thrones were set there for judgment,<br>The thrones of the house of David.  
**122:6** Pray for the peace of Jerusalem:<br>“May they prosper who love you.  
**122:7** May peace be within your walls,<br>And prosperity within your palaces.”  
**122:8** For the sake of my brothers and my friends,<br>I will now say, “May peace be within you.”  
**122:9** For the sake of the house of the LORD our God,<br>I will seek your good.  

## 123장

**123:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>To You I have raised my eyes,<br>You who are enthroned in the heavens!  
**123:2** Behold, as the eyes of servants <i>look</i> to the hand of their master,<br>As the eyes of a female servant to the hand of her mistress,<br>So our eyes <i>look</i> to the LORD our God,<br>Until He is gracious to us.  
**123:3** Be gracious to us, LORD, be gracious to us,<br>For we have had much more than enough of contempt.  
**123:4** Our soul has had much more than enough<br>Of the scoffing of those who are at ease,<br><i>And</i> with the contempt of the proud.  

## 124장

**124:1** [A Song of Ascents, of David.]<br>“Had it not been the LORD who was on our side,”<br>Let Israel say,  
**124:2** “Had it not been the LORD who was on our side<br>When people rose up against us,  
**124:3** Then they would have swallowed us alive,<br>When their anger was kindled against us;  
**124:4** Then the waters would have flooded over us,<br>The stream would have <sup>①</sup>swept over our souls;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>passed over</i>  
**124:5** Then the raging waters would have <sup>①</sup>swept over our souls.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>passed over</i>  
**124:6** Blessed be the LORD,<br>Who has not given us <sup>①</sup>to be torn by their teeth.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>as a prey to</i>  
**124:7** Our souls have escaped like a bird from the trapper’s snare;<br>The snare is broken and we have escaped.  
**124:8** Our help is in the name of the LORD,<br>Who made heaven and earth.  

## 125장

**125:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>Those who trust in the LORD<br>Are like Mount Zion, <i>which</i> cannot be moved <i>but</i> remains forever.  
**125:2** <i>As</i> the mountains surround Jerusalem,<br>So the LORD surrounds His people<br>From this time and forever.  
**125:3** For the scepter of wickedness will not rest upon the <sup>①</sup>land of the righteous,<br>So that the righteous will not extend their hands to do wrong.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>lot</i>  
**125:4** Do good, LORD, to those who are good<br>And to those who are upright in their hearts.  
**125:5** But as for those who turn aside to their crooked ways,<br>The LORD will lead them away with those who practice injustice.<br>Peace be upon Israel.  

## 126장

**126:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>When the LORD brought back <sup>①</sup>the captives of Zion,<br>We were like those who dream.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>those who returned to</i>  
**126:2** Then our mouth was filled with laughter<br>And our tongue with joyful shouting;<br>Then they said among the nations,<br>“The LORD has done great things for them.”  
**126:3** The LORD has done great things for us;<br>We are joyful.  
**126:4** Restore our fortunes, LORD,<br>As the <sup>①</sup>streams in the <sup>②</sup>South.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>stream-beds</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>Negev</i>  
**126:5** Those who sow in tears shall harvest with joyful shouting.  
**126:6** One who goes here and there weeping, carrying <i>his</i> bag of seed,<br>Shall indeed come again with a shout of joy, bringing his sheaves <i>with him.</i>  

## 127장

**127:1** [A Song of Ascents, of Solomon.]<br>Unless the LORD builds a house,<br>They who build it labor in vain;<br>Unless the LORD guards a city,<br>The watchman stays awake in vain.  
**127:2** It is futile for you to rise up early,<br>To <sup>①</sup>stay up late,<br>To eat the bread of painful labor;<br>This is how He gives to His beloved sleep.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>sit late</i>  
**127:3** Behold, children are a <sup>①</sup>gift of the LORD,<br>The fruit of the womb is a reward.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>heritage</i>  
**127:4** Like arrows in the hand of a warrior,<br>So are the children of one’s youth.  
**127:5** Blessed is the man whose quiver is full of them;<br>They will not be ashamed<br>When they speak with their enemies in the gate.  

## 128장

**128:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>Blessed is everyone who fears the LORD,<br>Who walks in His ways.  
**128:2** When you eat the fruit of the labor of your hands,<br>You will be happy and it will go well for you.  
**128:3** Your wife will be like a fruitful vine<br> <sup>①</sup>Within your house,<br>Your children like olive plants<br>Around your table.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>In the innermost parts of</i>  
**128:4** Behold, for so shall a man<br>Who fears the LORD be blessed.  
**128:5** The LORD bless you from Zion,<br>And may you see the prosperity of Jerusalem all the days of your life.  
**128:6** Indeed, may you see your children’s children.<br>Peace be upon Israel!  

## 129장

**129:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>“ <sup>①</sup>Many times they have attacked me from my youth up,”<br>Let Israel say,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Much</i>  
**129:2** “ <sup>①</sup>Many times they have attacked me from my youth up;<br>Yet they have not prevailed against me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Much</i>  
**129:3** The plowers plowed upon my back;<br>They lengthened their furrows.”  
**129:4** The LORD is righteous;<br>He has cut up the ropes of the wicked.  
**129:5** May all who hate Zion<br>Be put to shame and turned backward;  
**129:6** May they be like grass upon the housetops,<br>Which withers before it <sup>①</sup>grows up;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>draws out</i>  
**129:7** With which the harvester does not fill his <sup>①</sup>hand,<br>Or the binder of sheaves his <sup>②</sup>arms;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>palm</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>the fold of his robe</i>  
**129:8** Nor do those who pass by say,<br>“The blessing of the LORD be upon you;<br>We bless you in the name of the LORD.”  

## 130장

**130:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>Out of the depths I have cried to You, LORD.  
**130:2** Lord, hear my voice!<br>Let Your ears be attentive<br>To the sound of my pleadings.  
**130:3** If You, <sup>①</sup>LORD, were to keep <i>account of</i> guilty deeds,<br>Lord, who could stand?<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**130:4** But there is forgiveness with You,<br>So that You may be <sup>①</sup>revered.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>feared</i>  
**130:5** I wait for the LORD, my soul waits,<br>And I <sup>①</sup>wait for His word.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>hope in</i>  
**130:6** My soul <i>waits in hope</i> for the Lord<br>More than the watchmen for the morning;<br><i>Yes, more than</i> the watchmen for the morning.  
**130:7** Israel, wait for the LORD;<br>For with the LORD there is mercy,<br>And with Him is abundant redemption.  
**130:8** And He will redeem Israel<br>From all his guilty deeds.  

## 131장

**131:1** [A Song of Ascents, of David.]<br>LORD, my heart is not proud, nor my eyes arrogant;<br>Nor do I <sup>①</sup>involve myself in great matters,<br>Or in things too <sup>②</sup>difficult for me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>go after,</i> walk <sup>②</sup>Or <i>marvelous</i>  
**131:2** I have certainly soothed and quieted my soul;<br>Like a weaned child <i>resting</i> <sup>①</sup>against his mother,<br>My soul <sup>②</sup>within me is like a weaned child.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>upon</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>upon</i>  
**131:3** Israel, wait for the LORD<br>From this time <i>on</i> and forever.  

## 132장

**132:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>Remember, LORD, in David’s behalf,<br>All his affliction;  
**132:2** How he swore to the LORD<br><i>And</i> vowed to the Mighty One of Jacob,  
**132:3** “I certainly will not <sup>①</sup>enter my house,<br>Nor <sup>②</sup>lie on my bed;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>come into the tabernacle of</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>go up into the couch of</i>  
**132:4** I will not give sleep to my eyes<br>Or slumber to my eyelids,  
**132:5** Until I find a place for the LORD,<br> <sup>①</sup>A dwelling place for the Mighty One of Jacob.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Dwelling places</i>  
**132:6** Behold, we heard <i>about</i> it in Ephrathah,<br>We found it in the field of <sup>①</sup>Jaar.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>the wood</i>  
**132:7** Let’s go into His <sup>①</sup>dwelling place;<br>Let’s worship at His footstool.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>dwelling places</i>  
**132:8** Arise, LORD, to Your resting place,<br>You and the ark of Your strength.  
**132:9** May Your priests be clothed with righteousness,<br>And may Your godly ones sing for joy.  
**132:10** For the sake of Your servant David,<br>Do not turn away the face of Your anointed.  
**132:11** The LORD has sworn to David<br>A truth from which He will not turn back:<br>“I will set upon your throne <i>one</i> from the fruit of your body.  
**132:12** If your sons will keep My covenant<br>And My testimony which I will teach them,<br>Their sons also will sit upon your throne forever.”  
**132:13** For the LORD has chosen Zion;<br>He has desired it as His dwelling place.  
**132:14** “This is My resting place forever;<br>Here I will dwell, for I have desired it.  
**132:15** I will abundantly bless her food;<br>I will satisfy her needy with bread.  
**132:16** I will also clothe her priests with salvation,<br>And her godly ones will sing aloud for joy.  
**132:17** I will make the horn of David spring forth there;<br>I have prepared a lamp for My anointed.  
**132:18** I will clothe his enemies with shame,<br>But upon himself his crown will gleam.”  

## 133장

**133:1** [A Song of Ascents, of David.]<br>Behold, how good and how pleasant it is<br>For brothers to live together in unity!  
**133:2** It is like the precious oil on the head,<br>Running down upon the beard,<br><i>As on</i> Aaron’s beard,<br><i>The oil</i> which ran down upon the edge of his robes.  
**133:3** It is like the dew of Hermon<br>Coming down upon the mountains of Zion;<br>For the LORD commanded the blessing there—life forever.  

## 134장

**134:1** [A Song of Ascents.]<br>Behold, bless the LORD, all you servants of the LORD,<br>Who <sup>①</sup>serve by night in the house of the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>stand</i>  
**134:2** Lift up your hands to the sanctuary<br>And bless the LORD.  
**134:3** May the LORD bless you from Zion,<br>He who made heaven and earth.  

## 135장

**135:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>Praise the name of the LORD;<br>Praise <i>Him,</i> you servants of the LORD,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**135:2** You who stand in the house of the LORD,<br>In the courtyards of the house of our God!  
**135:3**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD, for the LORD is good;<br>Sing praises to His name, for it is lovely.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**135:4** For <sup>①</sup>the LORD has chosen Jacob for Himself,<br>Israel as His own possession.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**135:5** For I know that the LORD is great<br>And that our Lord is above all gods.  
**135:6** Whatever the LORD pleases, He does,<br>In heaven and on earth, in the seas and in all the ocean depths.  
**135:7**  <sup>①</sup>He causes the <sup>②</sup>mist to ascend from the ends of the earth,<br> <sup>③</sup>He makes lightning for the rain;<br> <sup>④</sup>He brings forth the wind from His treasuries.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i> <sup>②</sup>I.e., clouds <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i> <sup>④</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i>  
**135:8**  <sup>①</sup>He struck the firstborn of Egypt,<br> <sup>②</sup>Both human <i>firstborn</i> and animal.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>From man to animal</i>  
**135:9**  <sup>①</sup>He sent signs and wonders into your midst, Egypt,<br>Upon Pharaoh and all his servants.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i>  
**135:10**  <sup>①</sup>He struck many nations<br>And brought death to mighty kings,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>The one who</i>  
**135:11** Sihon, king of the Amorites,<br>Og, king of Bashan,<br>And all the kingdoms of Canaan;  
**135:12** And He gave their land as an inheritance,<br>An inheritance to His people Israel.  
**135:13** Your name, LORD, is everlasting,<br>The mention of You, LORD, is <sup>①</sup>throughout all generations.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to</i>  
**135:14** For the LORD will judge His people<br>And will have compassion on His servants.  
**135:15** The idols of the nations are <i>nothing but</i> silver and gold,<br>The work of human hands.  
**135:16** They have mouths, but they do not speak;<br>They have eyes, but they do not see;  
**135:17** They have ears, but they do not hear,<br>Nor is there any breath at all in their mouths.  
**135:18** Those who make them will become like them,<br><i>Yes,</i> everyone who trusts in them.  
**135:19** House of Israel, bless the LORD;<br>House of Aaron, bless the LORD;  
**135:20** House of Levi, bless the LORD;<br>You who <sup>①</sup>revere the LORD, bless the LORD.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>fear</i>
