**135:21** Blessed be the LORD from Zion,<br>Who dwells in Jerusalem.<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 136장

**136:1** Give thanks to the LORD, for He is good,<br>For His <sup>①</sup>faithfulness is everlasting.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>mercy,</i> and so throughout the Psalm  
**136:2** Give thanks to the God of gods,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting.  
**136:3** Give thanks to the Lord of lords,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting.  
**136:4** To Him who alone does great <sup>①</sup>wonders,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., wonderful acts  
**136:5** To Him who made the heavens <sup>①</sup>with skill,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>with understanding</i>  
**136:6** To Him who spread out the earth above the waters,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting;  
**136:7** To Him who made <i>the</i> great lights,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting:  
**136:8** The sun to rule <sup>①</sup>by day,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>over the</i>  
**136:9** The moon and stars to rule <sup>①</sup>by night,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>over the</i>  
**136:10** To Him who struck <sup>①</sup>the Egyptians, that is, their firstborn,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Egypt</i>  
**136:11** And brought Israel out from their midst,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,  
**136:12** With a strong hand and an outstretched arm,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting.  
**136:13** To Him who divided the <sup>①</sup>Red Sea in parts,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Sea of Reeds</i>  
**136:14** And allowed Israel to pass through the midst of it,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting;  
**136:15** But He <sup>①</sup>overthrew Pharaoh and his army in the <sup>②</sup>Red Sea,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>shook off</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>Sea of Reeds</i>  
**136:16** To Him who led His people through the wilderness,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting;  
**136:17** To Him who struck great kings,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,  
**136:18** And brought death to <sup>①</sup>mighty kings,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting:<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>majestic</i>  
**136:19** Sihon, king of the Amorites,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,  
**136:20** And Og, king of Bashan,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,  
**136:21** And gave their land as an inheritance,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,  
**136:22** An inheritance to His servant Israel,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting.  
**136:23** Who remembered us in our lowliness,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting,  
**136:24** And has rescued us from our enemies,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting;  
**136:25** Who gives food to all flesh,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting.  
**136:26** Give thanks to the God of heaven,<br>For His faithfulness is everlasting.  

## 137장

**137:1** By the rivers of Babylon,<br>There we sat down and wept,<br>When we remembered Zion.  
**137:2** Upon the <sup>①</sup>willows in the midst of it<br>We hung our <sup>②</sup>harps.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>poplars</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>lyres</i>  
**137:3** For there our captors <sup>①</sup>demanded of us <sup>②</sup>songs,<br>And our tormentors, jubilation, <i>saying,</i><br>“Sing for us one of the songs of Zion!”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>asked</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>words of song</i>  
**137:4** How can we sing the LORD’S song<br>In a foreign land?  
**137:5** If I forget you, Jerusalem,<br>May my right hand <sup>①</sup>forget <i>its skill.</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., become useless  
**137:6** May my tongue cling to the roof of my mouth<br>If I do not remember you,<br>If I do not <sup>①</sup>exalt Jerusalem<br>Above my chief joy.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>cause to ascend</i>  
**137:7** Remember, LORD, against the sons of Edom<br>The day of Jerusalem,<br>Those who said, “Lay it bare, lay it bare<br>To its foundation!”  
**137:8** Daughter of Babylon, you <sup>①</sup>devastated one,<br>Blessed will be one who repays you<br>With <sup>②</sup>the retribution with which you have repaid us.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>devastator</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>your requital</i>  
**137:9** Blessed will be one who seizes and dashes your children<br>Against the rock.  

## 138장

**138:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>I will give You thanks with all my heart;<br>I will sing Your praises before the gods.  
**138:2** I will bow down toward Your holy temple<br>And give thanks to Your name for Your mercy and Your <sup>①</sup>truth;<br>For You have made Your <sup>②</sup>word great <sup>③</sup>according to all Your name.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faithfulness</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>promise</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>together with</i>  
**138:3** On the day I called, You answered me;<br>You made me bold <i>with</i> strength in my soul.  
**138:4** All the kings of the earth will give thanks to You, LORD,<br>When they have heard the words of Your mouth.  
**138:5** And they will sing of the ways of the LORD,<br>For great is the glory of the LORD.  
**138:6** For the LORD is exalted,<br>Yet He looks after the lowly,<br>But He knows the haughty from afar.  
**138:7** Though I walk in the midst of trouble, You will <sup>①</sup>revive me;<br>You will reach out with Your hand against the wrath of my enemies,<br>And Your right hand will save me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>keep me alive</i>  
**138:8** The LORD will accomplish what concerns me;<br>Your faithfulness, LORD, is everlasting;<br>Do not abandon the works of Your hands.  

## 139장

**139:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>LORD, You have searched me and known <i>me.</i>  
**139:2** You know <sup>①</sup>when I sit down and <sup>②</sup>when I get up;<br>You understand my thought from far away.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>my sitting</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>my rising</i>  
**139:3** You <sup>①</sup>scrutinize my <sup>②</sup>path and my lying down,<br>And are acquainted with all my ways.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>winnow</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>journeying</i>  
**139:4**  <sup>①</sup>Even before there is a word on my tongue,<br>Behold, LORD, You know it all.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>For there is not</i>  
**139:5** You have encircled me behind and in front,<br>And placed Your hand upon me.  
**139:6** <i>Such</i> knowledge is too wonderful for me;<br>It is <i>too</i> high, I cannot comprehend it.  
**139:7** Where can I go from Your Spirit?<br>Or where can I flee from Your presence?  
**139:8** If I ascend to heaven, You are there;<br>If I make my bed in <sup>①</sup>Sheol, behold, You are there.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**139:9** <i>If</i> I take up the wings of the dawn,<br><i>If</i> I dwell in the remotest part of the sea,  
**139:10** Even there Your hand will lead me,<br>And Your right hand will take hold of me.  
**139:11** <i>If</i> I say, “Surely the darkness will <sup>①</sup>overwhelm me,<br>And the light around me will be night,”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>bruise;</i> some commentators <i>cover</i>  
**139:12** Even darkness is not dark <sup>①</sup>to You,<br>And the night is as bright as the day.<br>Darkness and light are alike <i>to You.</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>from</i>  
**139:13** For You created my innermost parts;<br>You wove me in my mother’s womb.  
**139:14** I will give thanks to You, because <sup>①</sup>I am awesomely and wonderfully <i>made;</i><br>Wonderful are Your works,<br>And my soul knows it very well.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Some ancient versions <i>You are fearfully wonderful</i>  
**139:15** My <sup>①</sup>frame was not hidden from You<br>When I was made in secret,<br><i>And</i> skillfully formed in the depths of the earth;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>bones were</i>  
**139:16** Your eyes have seen my formless substance;<br>And in Your book were written<br>All the days that were ordained <i>for me,</i><br>When as yet there was not one of them.  
**139:17** How precious also are Your thoughts for me, God!<br>How vast is the sum of them!  
**139:18** Were I to count them, they would outnumber the sand.<br>When I awake, I am still with You.  
**139:19** If only You would put the wicked to death, God;<br>Leave me, you men of bloodshed.  
**139:20** For they speak <sup>①</sup>against You wickedly,<br>And Your enemies <sup>②</sup>take <i>Your name</i> in vain.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>of</i> <sup>②</sup>Some mss <i>lift themselves up</i> against You  
**139:21** Do I not hate those who hate You, LORD?<br>And do I not loathe those who rise up against You?  
**139:22** I hate them with the utmost hatred;<br>They have become my enemies.  
**139:23** Search me, God, and know my heart;<br>Put me to the test and know my anxious thoughts;  
**139:24** And see if there is <i>any</i> <sup>①</sup>hurtful way in me,<br>And lead me in the everlasting way.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>way of pain</i>  

## 140장

**140:1** [For the music director. A Psalm of David.]<br>Rescue me, LORD, from evil people;<br>Protect me from violent men  
**140:2** Who devise evil things in <i>their</i> hearts;<br>They continually stir up wars.  
**140:3** They sharpen their tongues like a snake;<br>The venom of a viper is under their lips. <i>Selah</i>  
**140:4** Keep me, LORD, from the hands of the wicked;<br>Protect me from violent men<br>Who <sup>①</sup>intend to <sup>②</sup>trip up my feet.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>devise</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>push</i>  
**140:5** The proud have hidden a trap for me, and snares;<br>They have spread a net at the <sup>①</sup>wayside;<br>They have set snares for me. <i>Selah</i><br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>track</i>  
**140:6** I said to the LORD, “You are my God;<br>Listen, LORD, to the sound of my pleadings.  
**140:7**  <sup>①</sup>GOD the Lord, the strength of my salvation,<br>You have covered my head on the day of <sup>②</sup>battle.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YHWH,</i> usually rendered <i>LORD</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>weapons</i>  
**140:8** Do not grant, LORD, the desires of the wicked;<br>Do not bring about his evil planning, <i>so that</i> they <i>are not</i> exalted. <i>Selah</i>  
**140:9** “As for the head of those who surround me,<br>May the harm of their lips cover them.  
**140:10** May burning coals fall upon them;<br>May they be cast into the fire,<br>Into bottomless pits from which they cannot rise.  
**140:11** May a <sup>①</sup>slanderer not endure on the earth;<br>May evil hunt a violent person <sup>②</sup>violently.”<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>man of tongue</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>blow upon blow</i>  
**140:12** I know that the LORD will maintain the cause of the afflicted,<br>And justice for the poor.  
**140:13** Certainly the righteous will give thanks to Your name;<br>The upright will dwell in Your presence.  

## 141장

**141:1** [A Psalm of David.]<br>LORD, I call upon You; hurry to me!<br>Listen to my voice when I call to You!  
**141:2** May my prayer be <sup>①</sup>counted as incense before You;<br>The raising of my hands as the evening offering.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>fixed</i>  
**141:3** Set a guard, LORD, <sup>①</sup>over my mouth;<br>Keep watch over the door of my lips.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>to</i>  
**141:4** Do not incline my heart to <i>any</i> evil thing,<br>To practice deeds <sup>①</sup>of wickedness<br>With people who do wrong;<br>And may I not taste their delicacies.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>in</i>  
**141:5** May the righteous strike me with mercy and discipline me;<br>It is oil for the head;<br>My head shall not refuse it,<br>For my prayer is still against their evil deeds.  
**141:6** Their judges are thrown down by the sides of the rock,<br>And they hear my words, for they are pleasant.  
**141:7** As when one plows and breaks open the earth,<br>Our bones have been scattered at the mouth of <sup>①</sup>Sheol.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the netherworld  
**141:8** For my eyes are toward You, <sup>①</sup>GOD, the Lord;<br>In You I take refuge; do not <sup>②</sup>leave me defenseless.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YHWH,</i> usually rendered <i>LORD</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>pour out my soul</i>  
**141:9** Keep me from the <sup>①</sup>jaws of the trap which they have set for me,<br>And from the snares of those who do wrong.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>hands of the trap</i>  
**141:10** May the wicked fall into their own nets,<br>While I pass by <sup>①</sup>safely.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>altogether</i>  

## 142장

**142:1** [ <sup>①</sup>Maskil of David, when he was <sup>②</sup>in the cave. A Prayer.]<br>I cry out with my voice to the LORD;<br>With my voice I implore the LORD for compassion.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Possibly Contemplative; or Didactic; or Skillful Psalm <sup>②</sup>1 Sam 22:1; 24:3  
**142:2** I pour out my complaint before Him;<br>I declare my trouble before Him.  
**142:3** When my spirit felt weak within me,<br>You knew my path.<br>In the way where I walk<br>They have hidden a trap for me.  
**142:4** Look to the right and see;<br>For there is no one who regards me <i>favorably;</i><br> <sup>①</sup>There is no escape for me;<br>No one cares for my soul.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>Escape has perished from me</i>  
**142:5** I cried out to You, LORD;<br>I said, “You are my refuge,<br>My portion in the land of the living.  
**142:6** Give <i>Your</i> attention to my cry,<br>For I have been brought very low;<br>Rescue me from my persecutors,<br>For they are too strong for me.  
**142:7** Bring my soul out of prison,<br>So that I may give thanks to Your name;<br>The righteous will surround me,<br>For You will look after me.”  

## 143장

**143:1** [A Psalm of David.]<br>Hear my prayer, LORD,<br>Listen to my pleadings!<br>Answer me in Your faithfulness, in Your righteousness!  
**143:2** And do not enter into judgment with Your servant,<br>For no person living is righteous in Your sight.  
**143:3** For the enemy has persecuted my soul;<br>He has crushed my life to the ground;<br>He has made me dwell in dark places, like those who have long been dead.  
**143:4** Therefore my spirit <sup>①</sup>feels weak within me;<br>My heart is <sup>②</sup>appalled within me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>faints within</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>desolate</i>  
**143:5** I remember the days of old;<br>I meditate on all Your accomplishments;<br>I reflect on the work of Your hands.  
**143:6** I spread out my hands to You;<br>My soul <i>longs</i> for You, like a weary land. <i>Selah</i>  
**143:7** Answer me quickly, LORD, my spirit fails;<br>Do not hide Your face from me,<br>Or I will be the same as those who go down to the pit.  
**143:8** Let me hear Your faithfulness in the morning,<br>For I trust in You;<br>Teach me the way in which I should walk;<br>For to You I lift up my soul.  
**143:9** Save me, LORD, from my enemies;<br> <sup>①</sup>I take refuge in You.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>To You have I hidden</i>  
**143:10** Teach me to do Your will,<br>For You are my God;<br>Let Your good Spirit lead me on level <sup>①</sup>ground.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>land</i>  
**143:11** For the sake of Your name, LORD, revive me.<br>In Your righteousness bring my soul out of trouble.  
**143:12** And in Your faithfulness, <sup>①</sup>destroy my enemies,<br>And eliminate all those who attack my soul,<br>For I am Your servant.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>silence</i>  

## 144장

**144:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of David.]<br>Blessed be the LORD, my rock,<br>Who trains my hands for war,<br><i>And</i> my fingers for battle;  
**144:2** My faithfulness and my fortress,<br>My stronghold and my savior,<br>My shield and He in whom I take refuge,<br>Who subdues <sup>①</sup>my people under me.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Another reading is <i>peoples</i>  
**144:3** LORD, what is man, that You look after him?<br>Or a son of man, that You think of him?  
**144:4** Man is like the breath;<br>His days are like a passing shadow.  
**144:5** Bend down Your heavens, LORD, and come down;<br>Touch the mountains, that they may smoke.  
**144:6** Flash forth lightning and scatter them;<br>Send out Your arrows and confuse them.  
**144:7** Reach out with Your hand from on high;<br>Rescue me and save me from great waters,<br>From the hand of foreigners  
**144:8** Whose mouths speak deceit,<br>And whose right hand is a right hand of falsehood.  
**144:9** God, I will sing a new song to You;<br>On a harp of ten strings I will sing praises to You,  
**144:10** Who gives salvation to kings,<br>Who rescues His servant David from the evil sword.  
**144:11** Rescue me and save me from the hand of foreigners,<br>Whose mouth speaks deceit<br>And whose right hand is a right hand of falsehood.  
**144:12** When our sons in their youth <i>are</i> like growing plants,<br>And our daughters like corner pillars <sup>①</sup>fashioned for a palace,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>cut after the pattern of</i>  
**144:13** Our granaries <i>are</i> full, providing every kind <i>of produce,</i><br><i>And</i> our flocks deliver thousands and ten thousands in our <sup>①</sup>fields;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>outside</i>  
**144:14** <i>May</i> our cattle <sup>①</sup>be bred<br>Without <sup>②</sup>mishap and without <sup>③</sup>loss,<br><i>May there be</i> no outcry in our streets!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>be laden</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>breach</i> <sup>③</sup>Lit <i>going out;</i> i.e., miscarriage  
**144:15** Blessed are the people who are so situated;<br>Blessed are the people whose God is the LORD!  

## 145장

**145:1** [<i>A Psalm</i> of Praise, of David.]<br>I will exalt You, my God, the King,<br>And I will bless Your name forever and ever.  
**145:2** Every day I will bless You,<br>And I will praise Your name forever and ever.  
**145:3** Great is the LORD, and highly to be praised;<br>And His greatness is unsearchable.  
**145:4** One generation will praise Your works to another,<br>And will declare Your mighty acts.  
**145:5** On the glorious <sup>①</sup>splendor of Your majesty<br>And on Your wonderful works, I will meditate.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>majesty of Your splendor</i>  
**145:6** People will speak of the <sup>①</sup>power of Your awesome acts,<br>And I will tell of Your greatness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>strength</i>  
**145:7** They will burst forth <sup>①</sup>in speaking of Your abundant goodness,<br>And will shout joyfully of Your righteousness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>the mention of</i>  
**145:8** The LORD is gracious and compassionate;<br>Slow to anger and great in mercy.  
**145:9** The LORD is good to all,<br>And His mercies are over all His works.  
**145:10** All Your works will give thanks to You, LORD,<br>And Your godly ones will bless You.  
**145:11** They will speak of the glory of Your kingdom,<br>And talk of Your might,  
**145:12** To make known to the sons of mankind <sup>①</sup>Your mighty acts,<br>And the glory of the majesty of <sup>②</sup>Your kingdom.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>His</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>His</i>  
**145:13** Your kingdom is <sup>①</sup>an everlasting kingdom,<br>And Your dominion <i>endures</i> throughout all generations.<br> <sup>②</sup>The LORD is faithful in His words,<br>And holy in all His works.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>a kingdom of all ages</i> <sup>②</sup><i>The LORD</i>...<i>His works</i> in LXX (cf. DSS); not found in MT  
**145:14** The LORD supports all who fall,<br>And raises up all who are bowed down.  
**145:15** The eyes of all <sup>①</sup>look to You,<br>And You give them their food in due time.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>wait;</i> or <i>hope for</i>  
**145:16** You open Your hand<br>And satisfy the desire of every living thing.  
**145:17** The LORD is righteous in all His ways,<br>And kind in all His works.  
**145:18** The LORD is near to all who call on Him,<br>To all who call on Him in truth.  
**145:19** He will fulfill the desire of those who fear Him;<br>He will also hear their cry for help and save them.  
**145:20** The LORD watches over all who love Him,<br>But He will destroy all the wicked.  
**145:21** My mouth will speak the praise of the LORD,<br>And all flesh will bless His holy name forever and ever.  

## 146장

**146:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>Praise the LORD, my soul!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**146:2** I will praise the LORD while I live;<br>I will sing praises to my God while I <sup>①</sup>have my being.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>still am</i>  
**146:3** Do not trust in noblemen,<br>In <sup>①</sup>mortal man, in whom there is no salvation.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>a son of man</i>  
**146:4** His spirit departs, he returns to <sup>①</sup>the earth;<br>On that <i>very</i> day his plans perish.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>his earth</i>  
**146:5** Blessed is he whose help is the God of Jacob,<br>Whose hope is in the LORD his God,  
**146:6** Who made heaven and earth,<br>The sea and everything that is in them;<br>Who keeps <sup>①</sup>faith forever;<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>truth</i>  
**146:7** Who executes justice for the oppressed;<br>Who gives food to the hungry.<br>The LORD frees the prisoners.  
**146:8** The LORD opens <i>the eyes of</i> those who are blind;<br>The LORD raises up those who are bowed down;<br>The LORD loves the righteous.  
**146:9** The LORD watches over strangers;<br>He <sup>①</sup>supports the fatherless and the widow,<br>But He <sup>②</sup>thwarts the way of the wicked.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>relieves</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>bends</i>  
**146:10** The LORD will reign forever,<br>Your God, Zion, to all generations.<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 147장

**147:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>For it is good to sing praises to our God;<br>For <sup>③</sup>it is pleasant <i>and</i> praise is <sup>④</sup>beautiful.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>He is delightful</i> <sup>④</sup>Or <i>fitting</i>  
**147:2** The LORD builds up Jerusalem;<br>He gathers the outcasts of Israel.  
**147:3** He heals the brokenhearted<br>And binds up their <sup>①</sup>wounds.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>pains</i>  
**147:4** He counts the number of the stars;<br>He <sup>①</sup>gives names to all of them.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>calls them all by</i> their <i>names</i>  
**147:5** Great is our Lord and abundant in strength;<br>His understanding is <sup>①</sup>infinite.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>innumerable</i>  
**147:6** The LORD <sup>①</sup>supports the afflicted;<br>He brings the wicked down to the ground.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>relieves</i>  
**147:7** Sing to the LORD with thanksgiving;<br>Sing praises to our God on the lyre;  
**147:8** <i>It is</i> He who covers the heavens with clouds,<br>Who provides rain for the earth,<br>Who makes grass sprout on the mountains.  
**147:9** <i>It is</i> He who gives an animal its food,<br><i>And feeds</i> young ravens that cry.  
**147:10** He does not delight in <sup>①</sup>the strength of the horse;<br>He does not take pleasure in the <sup>②</sup>legs of a man.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., as a military asset <sup>②</sup>I.e., as a military asset  
**147:11** The LORD favors those who fear Him,<br>Those who wait for His <sup>①</sup>faithfulness.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>mercy</i>  
**147:12** Praise the LORD, Jerusalem!<br>Praise your God, Zion!  
**147:13** For He has strengthened the bars of your gates;<br>He has blessed your sons among you.  
**147:14** He makes <sup>①</sup>peace in your borders;<br>He satisfies you with the <sup>②</sup>finest of the wheat.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>your borders peace</i> <sup>②</sup>Lit <i>fat</i>  
**147:15** He sends His command to the earth;<br>His word runs very swiftly.  
**147:16** He <sup>①</sup>showers snow like wool;<br>He scatters the frost like ashes.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>gives</i>  
**147:17** He hurls His ice as fragments;<br>Who can stand before His cold?  
**147:18** He sends His word and makes them melt;<br>He makes His wind blow, and the waters flow.  
**147:19** He declares His words to Jacob,<br>His statutes and His judgments to Israel.  
**147:20** He has not dealt this way with any <i>other</i> nation;<br>And as for His judgments, <sup>①</sup>they have not known them.<br> <sup>②</sup>Praise <sup>③</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>I.e., the other nations <sup>②</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>③</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 148장

**148:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>Praise the LORD from the heavens;<br>Praise Him in the heights!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**148:2** Praise Him, all His angels;<br>Praise Him, all His <i>heavenly</i> armies!  
**148:3** Praise Him, sun and moon;<br>Praise Him, all stars of light!  
**148:4** Praise Him, <sup>①</sup>highest heavens,<br>And the waters that are above the heavens!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>heavens of heavens</i>  
**148:5** They are to praise the name of the LORD,<br>For He commanded and they were created.  
**148:6** He has also established them forever and ever;<br>He has made a decree, and it will not pass away.  
**148:7** Praise the LORD from the earth,<br>Sea monsters, and all the ocean depths;  
**148:8** Fire and hail, snow and clouds;<br>Stormy wind, fulfilling His word;  
**148:9** Mountains and all hills;<br>Fruit trees and all cedars;  
**148:10** Animals and all cattle;<br>Crawling things and winged fowl;  
**148:11** Kings of the earth and all peoples;<br>Rulers and all judges of the earth;  
**148:12** Both young men and virgins;<br>Old men and children.  
**148:13** They are to praise the name of the LORD,<br>For His name alone is exalted;<br>His majesty is above earth and heaven.  
**148:14** And He has lifted up a horn for His people,<br>Praise for all His godly ones,<br>For the sons of Israel, a people near to Him.<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 149장

**149:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>Sing a new song to the LORD,<br><i>And</i> His praise in the congregation of the godly ones.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  
**149:2** Israel shall be joyful in his Maker;<br>The sons of Zion shall rejoice in their King.  
**149:3** They shall praise His name with dancing;<br>They shall sing praises to Him with tambourine and lyre.  
**149:4** For the LORD takes pleasure in His people;<br>He will glorify the lowly with salvation.  
**149:5** The godly ones shall be jubilant in glory;<br>They shall sing for joy on their beds.  
**149:6** The high praises of God <i>shall be</i> in their <sup>①</sup>mouths,<br>And a two-edged sword in their hands,<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Lit <i>throat</i>  
**149:7** To execute vengeance on the nations,<br>And punishment on the peoples,  
**149:8** To bind their kings with chains,<br>And their dignitaries with shackles of iron,  
**149:9** To execute against them the judgment written.<br>This is an honor for all His godly ones.<br> <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>  

## 150장

**150:1**  <sup>①</sup>Praise <sup>②</sup>the LORD!<br>Praise God in His sanctuary;<br>Praise Him in His mighty <sup>③</sup>expanse.<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>②</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i> <sup>③</sup>Or <i>firmament;</i> i.e., atmosphere and space  
**150:2** Praise Him for His mighty deeds;<br>Praise Him according to His excellent greatness.  
**150:3** Praise Him with trumpet sound;<br>Praise Him with harp and lyre.  
**150:4** Praise Him with tambourine and dancing;<br>Praise Him with stringed instruments and flute.  
**150:5** Praise Him with loud cymbals;<br>Praise Him with resounding cymbals.  
**150:6** Everything that has breath shall praise <sup>①</sup>the LORD.<br> <sup>②</sup>Praise <sup>③</sup>the LORD!<br>→ <sup>①</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i> <sup>②</sup>Or <i>Hallelujah!</i> <sup>③</sup>Heb <i>YAH</i>
